Extraido 2026-10-07. 188 paginas. Se quitaron Gallery, Update history y matrices de iconos. "| a | b |" = fila de tabla; [Nombre] = icono; "## " = encabezado. Los otros temas estan en otros archivos.

# Academy Badge
DLC Content (Tides of the Foscari)
This article primarily features content only available in DLC, which may not be available in all copies of the game.
| Academy Badge [DLC]
| Will provide bonus Amount and Revivals in exchange for Growth. Will provide bonus Amount and Revivals in exchange for Growth.
| Passive item
| Release | 13 April 2023 (Update)
| ID | ACADEMYBADGE
| Player stat | Growth, Amount, Revival
| Rarity | 10
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | -3% Growth, +1 Amount and +1 Revival alternatively
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | -15% Growth, +3 Amount and +2 Revival
| Stage item | Lake Foscari, Abyss Foscari
| {"stage-item":"Lake Foscari, Abyss Foscari","description":"Will provide bonus Amount and Revivals in exchange for Growth.","max-level":"5","id":"ACADEMYBADGE","stacking":"Additive","update":"New DLC & Patch 1.4.0","dlc":"tf","sprite":"Sprite-Academy Badge.png","max-effect":"-15% Growth, +3 Amount and +2 Revival","name":"Academy Badge","stat":"Growth, Amount, Revival","release":"13 April 2023","rarity":"10","icon":"Icon-Academy Badge.png","per-level":"-3% Growth, +1 Amount and +1 Revival alternatively"}
Academy Badge is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is only obtainable as a stage item or by reaching Level 30 as Eleanor, Maruto, or Keitha.
Academy Badge reduces experience gain by -3% per level, in exchange for +1 Amount or +1 Revival alternatively.
Academy Badge can be found as a stage item in Lake Foscari and Abyss Foscari.
## Levels
Academy Badge has 6 levels. At max level, Academy Badge provides -15% Growth, +3 Amount, and +2 Revival.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Will provide bonus Amount and Revivals in exchange for Growth."
| Level 2 | Fires 1 more projectile. XP gain decreases by -3%.
| Level 3 | Adds 1 Revival. XP gain decreases by -3%.
| Level 4 | Fires 1 more projectile. XP gain decreases by -3%.
| Level 5 | Adds 1 Revival. XP gain decreases by -3%.
| Level 6 | Fires 1 more projectile. XP gain decreases by -3%.
## Tips
- The extra Amount and Revival it gives are worth more than the experience loss, as Limit Break is not strong enough for enemies with HP x Level skill, and without it, more experience makes them harder only for more gold or healing.
- If you are farming for gold and Golden Eggs, more level is better because you would have enough stats to cap Amount and not die for any relevant period of time.
## Trivia
- Although it has a rarity of 10 the Academy Badge cannot be found in the level up screen options. It is only collectable on the Tides of the Foscari stages or when playing as Eleanor, Maruto, or Keitha.
=====
# Amount
Amount
Amount is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It determines how many extra projectiles most weapons have. 
The player has a base Amount of 0 before modifications. Bonuses to Amount are flat bonuses and thus stack additively.
The upper limit of Amount from characters, PowerUps and passive item or weapon bonuses is 10.
For certain weapons, Amount may work differently. It affects:
- Base Damage of  Four Seasons [DLC],  Godai Shuffle [DLC],  Flash Arrow [DLC],  Millionaire [DLC],  Endo Gears [DLC],  Peri Pendulum [DLC],  Myo Lift [DLC], and  Epi Head [DLC]
- Duration of  Globus [DLC] and  Nitesco [DLC]
- Pierce of  Just Vent [DLC],  Mace [DLC], and  Stamazza [DLC]
- Healing effect of  Lifesign Scan [DLC] and  Paranormal Scan [DLC]
- Number of bounces of  Carréllo
## PowerUp
| Amount
| Fires 1 more projectile (all weapons).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | AMOUNT
| Max level | 1
| Bonus per level | +1
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +1
| Initial cost | 5,000
| {"description":"Fires 1 more projectile (all weapons).","cost":"5000","id":"AMOUNT","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"","name":"Amount","release":"27 October 2021","stacking":"Additive","max level":"1","bonus":"+1"}
The Amount PowerUp increases Amount by 1, and only has 1 rank. It initially costs 5,000. 
| Rank | Amount
| 1 | +1
## From items
Amount's associated passive item is the  Duplicator, which provides +1 Amount per level, up to a maximum of +2 Amount at level 2.
-  Academy Badge [DLC] has 6 levels and provides +1 Amount every even level, up to a maximum of +3 Amount.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] has a chance of granting an Amount stat bonus of +0.2 upon activation.
-  Golden Egg can permanently increase Amount by +0.1 for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
-  Beginning (X) provides +3 Amount for the character's starting weapon and its evolution, and +1 Amount for  Bone,  Cherry Bomb,  Carréllo,  Celestial Dusting, and  La Robba.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Gennaro and  Sigma start with +1 Amount.
-  Mortaccio,  Cavallo,  Ramba, and  O'Sole gain +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +3 at level 60.
- Mortaccio, Cavallo, Ramba, and O'Sole gain +1 additional Amount when they morph at level 80 following the collection of their respective Chaos relic.
-  Ambrojoe starts with +10 Amount, every level decreases by -2 until it returns to normal by level 6. He gains +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +3 at level 60.
-  Bats gains +1 amount per revival.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Gav'Et-Oni ★ gains+1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +4 at level 80.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Rottin'Ghoul gains +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +4 at level 80.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate has a chance to gain a random amount of Amount every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Horse gains +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +4 at level 80.
## Operation Guns
-  Lucia gains +0.2 Amount after reviving, with no cap.
-  Simondo gains +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +4 at level 80.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Carrie and  Cornell start with +1 Amount.
-  Blue Crescent Moon Cornell starts with +3 Amount.
-  Trevor gains +1 Amount every 10 levels, up to +3 at level 30.
-  Familiar and  Innocent Devil gain +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +3 at level 60.
-  Cave Troll gains +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +4 at level 80.
-  Hammer starts with +1 Amount and gains +1 Amount every 20 levels, up to +3 at level 60. His Amount is also doubled while invulerable.
-  Leon gains +1 Amount upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
## Tips
- Amount determines not how many projectiles a weapon has, but how many extra projectiles a weapon has. A Level 2  Whip with +1 Amount will produce 3 slashes: 2 from the weapon itself, and 1 from your Amount stat.
- Amount bonuses for some weapons work better than others. Weapons such as the  Axe and  Fire Wand do great when paired with the Duplicator, but weapons such as the  Magic Wand benefit less due to the already high amount of low damage projectiles.

# Antidote
| Antidote
| Allows to buy the Defang PowerUp. Allows to buy the Defang PowerUp.
| Relic
| Release | 19 October 2023 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_ANTIDOTE
| Effects | Unlocks the Defang PowerUp.
| Location | Due south of Whiteout.
| {"description":"Allows to buy the Defang PowerUp.","id":"RELIC_ANTIDOTE","update":"New Content Patch 1.7.0: Whiteout","effects":"Unlocks the Defang PowerUp.","sprite":"Sprite-Antidote.png","name":"Antidote","release":"19 October 2023","icon":"Icon-Antidote.png","location":"Due south of Whiteout."}
The Antidote is a relic located in Whiteout. Once collected, it permanently unlocks the Defang PowerUp.
## Obtaining
The Antidote is found south of the starting area in Whiteout. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. Once the Antidote has been collected, it will not appear again.
Alternatively, the Antidote can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "duraistruth".
## Trivia
- The Antidote and its spell together reference the 1997 game Final Fantasy Tactics. The spell uses the name of the character Orran Durai, who writes the ‘Durai Papers’ that tell the truth of the protagonist’s story to future historians in the game’s framing device. The relic is named for the music track that plays in the battle where he is first met.

| Apoplexy
| Allows to buy the Charm PowerUp. Allows to buy the Charm PowerUp.
| Relic
| Release | 9 February 2023 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_CHARM
| Effects | Unlocks the Charm PowerUp.
| Location | Appears somewhere near the player at the center of a large square of Diamonds at the 9:00 minute mark in Bat Country.
| {"description":"Allows to buy the Charm PowerUp.","id":"RELIC_CHARM","update":"Patch 1.3.0","effects":"Unlocks the Charm PowerUp.","sprite":"Sprite-Apoplexy.png","name":"Apoplexy","release":"9 February 2023","icon":"Icon-Apoplexy.png","location":"Appears somewhere near the player at the center of a large square of Diamonds at the 9:00 minute mark in Bat Country."}
Apoplexy is a relic located in Bat Country. Once collected, it permanently unlocks the Charm PowerUp.
## Obtaining
At minute 9:00 in Bat Country, the Apoplexy will appear somewhere near the player at the center of a large square of Diamonds. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. If not collected quickly enough, it will despawn 1 minute after appearing. Once the Apoplexy has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, the Apoplexy can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "tengillesbalm".
## Trivia
- The Apoplexy and its spell reference the 1997 game Final Fantasy Tactics. The spell uses the name of the character Meliadoul Tengille, and the relic itself is named after the music track that plays in the battle against her.

# Arcanas
Arcanas
"Cards" redirects here. For Vampire Crawlers content, see Crawlers:Cards.
The  Randomazzo relic unlocks access to Arcanas.
Arcana cards are a modifier system in Vampire Survivors. Obtaining the  Randomazzo in Gallo Tower unlocks the first Arcana,  Sarabande of Healing (VI).
The rest of the Arcanas in the base set are unlocked by reaching level 50 with certain characters, or by reaching the 31:00 mark in certain stages. Obtaining the  Darkasso in Room 1665 unlocks an additional set of "Darkanas", allowing even more Arcanas to be unlocked by achieving specific tasks with certain other characters.
## Mechanics
Each Arcana modifies gameplay in a particular way. The player can choose to enable or disable Arcanas in the stage selection screen.
With Arcanas enabled, the player can obtain up to three Arcanas over the course of a run (although through certain means, more than three can be obtained):
- At the beginning of a run, the player is allowed to choose one of any unlocked Arcanas or Darkanas. They are also given the option to select a random one.
- At 11:00 and 21:00, a special boss will spawn that, upon death, who drops an  Arcana Chest. Upon being picked up, the chest will allow the player to choose an additional Arcana. The menu offers four or more randomly chosen Arcanas from the uncollected Arcanas. The player may spend Rerolls to get new choices until all options have been displayed, and unlike normal level ups, previous options will not be offered again.
If the player has three or more Arcanas, the Arcana bosses will instead drop regular chests. If Arcanas are disabled, the Arcana bosses will not appear in the stage.
If the player has unlocked a total of 23 or more Arcanas or Darkanas (which requires unlocking at least one Darkana), Arcana Chests will instead offer six random Arcanas as opposed to four, and each Arcana Chest will allow the player one free Reroll.
In stages where the time limit is not 30:00, the Arcana bosses may appear at different times. In Boss Rash, Bat Country, and Tiny Bridge, the bosses instead spawn at 5:00 and 10:00.
Some characters automatically start with Arcana effects, such as  Avatar Infernas starting with  Heart of Fire (XIX); these Arcana effects do not take up an Arcana slot, and they will still be present if Arcanas are disabled.
## Obtaining more than three Arcanas
There is no limit to the amount of Arcanas a player can have at one time, although more unique methods are required for obtaining more than three.
-  Queen Sigma is allowed to select an additional Arcana at levels 1, 2, 3, 77, and 108. All unlocked Arcanas are available as choices.
- If Inverse mode is active, the  Merchant will sell an Arcana for 20,000, regardless of how many Arcanas the player currently has. All unlocked Arcanas are available as choices.
- With Endless mode enabled, the Merchant will respawn with another for sale at the start of each normal game length cycle (30 minutes in most stages). This can be repeated until all unlocked Arcanas are collected.
- Treasure Chests dropped by bosses spawned by  Moonlight Bolero (VI) can occasionally contain Arcanas. This chance remains until all unlocked Arcanas have been collected.
## Arcanas
There are 22 regular Arcanas total. Each Arcana is represented by a Roman numeral.

## Darkanas
Darkanas were introduced in patch 1.11 and are unlocked by obtaining the  Darkasso found due south in Room 1665. There are currently 12 Darkanas total. Each Darkana is also represented by a Roman numeral. 
While Darkanas often share similar themes to their "counterpart" Arcanas, whether or not they are meant to be the inverse or opposite of Arcana is merely speculation, and has never been confirmed. However, this is likely false, as the patch notes for the same version that introduced Darkana state "The only difference between Arcanas and Darkanas is their theme, functionally they are exactly the same," with no other confirmation toward this intention being given.

## Trivia
- Arcanas can be unlocked by typing the code "randomazzami" into the Secrets menu, granting the player the  Randomazzo and  Sarabande of Healing (VI).
- Darkanas can be unlocked by typing the code "darkassami" into the Secrets menu, granting the player the  Darkasso and  Moonlight Bolero (VI).
- All of the "Reach minute 31 in the [stage]" Arcanas ( Mad Groove (VIII)  Out of Bounds (XII),  Disco of Gold (XV), and  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX)), as well as  Game Killer (0), can be unlocked by typing "aintnobodygottimeforthat" while on the main menu.
- This deck of Arcanas is loosely based on the real Major Arcana used in Tarot card readings. For example,  Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII)'s real counterpart is The Moon (XVIII), which represents mysteries, deception, and illusion.
- In normal stages, the Arcanas are dropped by a  Glowing Bat boss.
- Placeholders for all Arcanas existed in the game files before all Arcana were officially released, with the unreleased ones having placeholder names being musical tracks from the Castlevania series. Most of them were slightly modified before being officially released. For example, what was once place held as "Tragic Prince" has since been released as  Tragic Princess (III).
- The pictures on the Darkanas have similar designs or visual motifs associated with their Randomazzo counterparts. For example,  Gemini (I) and  Sapphire Mist (I) depict the same two figures.
- The correct singular form of the word is "arcanum", whereas "arcana" is already in plural. However, in-game, "arcana" is used to refer to a single card and "arcanas" is used for multiple.
- With Game Killer (0), all treasure chests will always contain at least three rewards, allowing Arcana Chests to contain two additional chest rewards.

# Area
Area
Area is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It increases the area of effect of most weapons.
The player has 100% base Area before modifications. The Area in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base Area, meaning that Area stat of +30% equals to 130% total Area, for example. Area modifications from characters, PowerUps, and items stack additively. Other modifiers, such as Arcanas, stack multiplicatively with the collective bonuses from the aforementioned.
The upper limit of total Area after character, PowerUp and item modifications is 1000%. Bonuses from Arcanas are unaffected by the limit.
Area also affects: 
- Base Damage of  SpellStream [DLC],  SpellStrom [DLC],  Sonic Dash [DLC],  Rapidus Fio [DLC]
- Singularity damage of  SpellStrom [DLC]
- Critical damage of  Rune Sword [DLC]
## PowerUp
| Area
| Augments area of attacks by 5% per rank (max +10%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | AREA
| Max level | 2
| Bonus per level | +5%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +10%
| Initial cost | 300
| {"description":"Augments area of attacks by 5% per rank (max +10%).","cost":"300","id":"AREA","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"","name":"Area","release":"27 October 2021","stacking":"Additive","max level":"2","bonus":"+5%"}
The Area PowerUp increases Area by +5% per rank, with a maximum rank of 2. At rank 2, it provides +10% Area. It initially costs 300.
| Level | Area
| 1 | +5%
| 2 | +10%
## From items
Area's associated passive item is the  Candelabrador, which provides +10% Area per level, up to a maximum of +50% Area at level 5.
-  Silver Ring also provide +5% per level, up to a maximum of +40% at level 9.
-  Torrona's Box provides +4%, then +3% per level, up to a maximum of +25% at level 8.
-  Golden Egg can permanently increase Area by +1% for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
-  Awake (IV) provides +5% Area when chosen per  Revival consumed.
-  Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) provides a multiplier cycling between 0.75 to 1.25 every 10 seconds, and gives +1% Area every level.
## From characters
-  Porta starts with +30% Area.
-  Clerici starts with +400% Area, every level decreases by -100% until it returns to normal by level 5.
-  Concetta gains +1% Area every level, with no cap.
-  Zi'Assunta gains +0.5% Area every level, with no cap.
-  Exdash and  Toastie start with -10% Area.
-  Smith IV starts with -10% Area and gains +0.7% Area every level, with no cap.
-  Minnah's Area cycles between multiplied by 2 and divided by 2 every minute.
-  Leda starts with +10% Area.
-  Peppino starts with -60% Area and gains +10% Area at level 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, and 21.
-  Scorej-Oni starts with +20% Area.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Syuuto starts with -30% Area. He gains +30% Area after he morphs when his  starting weapon evolves.
-  McCoy-Oni's Area cycles between normal and multiplied by 2.5 every second.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% Area per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% Area per level.
## Operation Guns
-  Lucia gains +4% Area after reviving, with no cap.
-  Browny gains 2% Area per -1%  Cooldown up to a maximum of +100% Area.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Charlotte,  Eric,  Isaac,  Barlowe, start with +20% Area.
-  Charlotte & Jonathan starts with +40% Area.
-  Joachim can select to gain +1% Area per level.
-  Blackmore can select to gain +1% Area per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% Area per level.
-  Leon gains +10% Area upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
-  Henry gains up to +100% area depending on the number of enemies in sight.
## Tips
- The larger a weapon is by default, the more it will benefit from Area bonuses. Weapons such as Bloody Tear, Soul Eater, Axe, Death Spiral, Hellfire,Lightning Ring/Thunder Loop, and La Borra are examples of weapons that excel with extra Area.
- Axe and Death Spiral gets an extra multiplier to the character's Area bonus.
- Area stat is additive. The highest possible Area bonus can be attained while playing as Concetta, with the rank 2 Area PowerUp (+10%), rank 5 Omni PowerUp (+10%), level 5 Candelabrador (+50%), level 9 Silver Ring (+40%), and level 8 Torrona's Box (+25%) for a total of +(player level+135)% Area.
- Keep in mind, adding to the Area of King Bible not only increase the size of the books, but also how far away they are from the player.
- Area is important for piercing weapons, as the larger they are, the more enemies they will be able to hit. Consider upgrading Area when using the Runetracer, Hellfire, and Death Spiral.
- Minnah's stat cycling cannot stack with Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII).

# Armor (item)
Armor
| Armor
| Reduces incoming damage by 1. Increases retaliatory Damage by 10%. Reduces incoming damage by 1. Increases retaliatory Damage by 10%.
| Passive item
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | ARMOR
| Player stat | Armor
| Rarity | 100
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | -1
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | -5
| Stage item | Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Lake Foscari, Abyss Foscari
| {"description":"Reduces incoming damage by 1. Increases retaliatory Damage by 10%.","max-level":"5","id":"ARMOR","stage-item":"Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Lake Foscari, Abyss Foscari","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","stacking":"Additive","sprite":"Sprite-Armor.png","max-effect":"-5","name":"Armor","stat":" Armor","release":"27 October 2021","rarity":"100","icon":"Icon-Armor.png","per-level":"-1"}
Armor is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by default.
Armor increases  Armor by 1 per level, reducing all damage dealt to the player by 1. It cannot reduce damage to 0. Armor also increases Retaliatory Damage by 10% additively and it is an additional multiplier on top of  Might. 
Armor can be found as a stage item in Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Lake Foscari, and Abyss Foscari.
## Levels
Armor has 5 levels. At max level, Armor provides +5  Armor and +50% Retaliatory Damage.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Reduces incoming damage by 1. Increases retaliatory damage by 10%."
| Level 2 | Reduces incoming damage by 1.
| Level 3 | Reduces incoming damage by 1.
| Level 4 | Reduces incoming damage by 1.
| Level 5 | Reduces incoming damage by 1.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Runetracer | + | Armor | = | NO FUTURE
| Eskizzibur | + | Max level:Armor | = | Legionnaire
| Sonic Bloom | + | Armor+Weapon Power-Up | = | Wave Beam
| Iron Ball | + | Armor | = | Wrecking Ball
| Keremet Bubbles | + | Max level:Armor | = | Keremet Morbus
## Tips
- Armor bonuses reduce how much damage enemies deal to the player. Even if a single point sounds small, bonuses quickly add up. It is extremely effective with  Crimson Shroud as it caps all incoming damage at 10 already.
- When paired with bonuses to health, such as the  Hollow Heart,  Pummarola, or by playing as  Clerici, damage reduction is even more powerful due to bigger health buffer.
- Armor can allow the player to play much more recklessly, and at high levels, can greatly reduce the risk from bumping into enemies, on accident or on purpose.
- When playing as characters with less Max Health, such as  Poe Ratcho or  Exdash Exiviiq, every point of HP matters even more. If Hollow Heart or Pummarola are unavailable, don't be afraid to take Armor.
- Armor increases retaliatory damage from  NO FUTURE,  Heart of Fire (XIX), and Crimson Shroud.
- It also increases the damage for the explosions of  Out of Bounds (XII); it is unknown if this is intended or an oversight.
- It does not deal retaliatory damage by itself; it only increases retaliatory damage from other sources.
## Trivia
- The Armor sprite had an old design. It is likely inspired from the Iron Plate from the Castlevania series.

# Armor (stat)
Armor (stat)
Armor is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. Each point of Armor the player has will reduce all incoming damage by 1 and increases the damage from retaliatory effects by 10%.
The player has 0 base Armor before modifications, such as the characters bonuses or PowerUps. Armor is additional and the retaliatory damage bonus stack additively with itself and multiplicatively with  Might.
There is no upper limit to how much Armor the player can have, but the retaliatory bonus damage is capped at +500% (50 Armor). Also, regardless the amount of Armor the player has, the damage taken from an enemy will always be at least 1.
Armor also affects Base Damage of  Iron Ball [DLC] and  Wrecking Ball [DLC].
 Probotector [DLC] gains 10% Might per +1 Armor.
## PowerUp
| Armor
| Reduces incoming damage by 1 per rank (max -3).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | ARMOR
| Max level | 3
| Bonus per level | +1
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +3
| Initial cost | 600

The Armor PowerUp increases Armor by 1 per rank, with a maximum rank of 3. At rank 3, it provides +3 Armor. It initially costs 600.

## From items
Armor's associated passive item is  Armor (item), which increases Armor by 1 per level, up to a maximum of +5 Armor at level 5.
-  Centralis Custos [DLC] provides +10 Armor when the player character takes damage from enemies.
-  Trinum Custodem [DLC] provides a passive +10 Armor and an additional +10 Armor when the player character takes damage from enemies.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] has a chance of granting an Armor stat bonus of +0.2 upon activation.
-  Golden Eggs can permanently increase Armor by +0.1 for the character that collected them.
## From arcana
-  Awake (IV) provides +1 Armor per  Revival consumed.
## From characters
-  Antonio and  Bat Robbert starts with +1 Armor.
-  Mortaccio,  Cavallo, and  O'Sole gain +2 Armor after morphing. In Beta 1.14,  Ramba, also has this ability.
-  Divano gains +1 Armor every 5 levels, up to +5 Armor at level 25.
-  Toastie gains +65520 Armor upon reaching level 200.
-  Leda starts with +5 Armor.
-  Peppino,  She-Moon, and  Santa start with +2 Armor.
-  Sigma and  Gyoruntin start with +3 Armor.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Syuuto starts with +2 Armor. He gains +2 Armor after he morphs when his  starting weapon evolves.
-  McCoy-Oni starts with +1 Armor.
-  Megalo Syuuto starts with +2 Armor.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Maruto starts with +1 Armor. He gains +1 Armor after being damaged, resetting every 10 seconds, getting hit again extends the reset timer by 1 second.
-  Je-Ne-Viv starts with +5 Armor.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate has a chance to gain a random amount of Armor every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Guardian starts with +1 Armor and provides +1 Armor to nearby characters.
## Operation Guns
-  Ariana gains +1 Armor per 10%  Move Speed.
-  Lucia gains +0.2 Armor after reviving, with no cap.
-  Browny and  Probotector starts with +1 Armor.
-  Stanley starts with +1 Armor and gains +1 Armor per +10%  Might, with no cap.
-  Bahamut starts with +2 Armor.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Christopher,  Simon,  Rinaldo,  Vincent,  Nathan,  Barlowe,  Stella & Loretta,  Loretta & Stella,  Stella,  Loretta,  Soleil,  Graham,  Joachim,  Carmilla,  Olrox and  Malphas start with +1 Armor.
-  Leon starts with +1 Armor and gains another +1 Armor upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
-  Cornell gains +2 Armor after he morphs upon obtaining  Trinum Custodem.
-  Henry,  Elizabeth Bartley,  Alucard,  Blue Crescent Moon Cornell,  Walter,  Frozenshade,  Galamoth,  Megalo Elizabeth,  Megalo Olrox,  Megalo Death and  Megalo Dracula start with +2 Armor.
-  Keremet starts with +20 Armor.
-  Christopher gains +1 Armor per +23%  Greed temporarily, with no cap.
-  Simon gains +1 Armor per +23%  Growth temporarily, with no cap.
## Tips
- Armor bonuses reduce how much damage enemies deal to you. Even if a single point sounds small, bonuses quickly add up as each point is more valuable than previous thanks to enemies attacking multiple times per second.
- Toastie is the only character capable of having more than 50 Armor, gaining a massive +65520 Armor bonus upon reaching level 200. All other sources of Armor can not exceed +50.
## Trivia
- The Armor sprite had an old design. It is likely inspired from the Iron Plate from the Castlevania series.
- Armor uses its UK spelling, "Armour", in the descriptions of  Stanley,  Probotector, and  Ariana, even though the American spelling is otherwise used in the game.

# Ars Gouda
"Bestiary" redirects here. For a list of all enemies, see Enemies.
| Ars Gouda
| Permanently allows to access the list of defeated enemies from the main menu. Permanently allows to access the list of defeated enemies from the main menu.
| Relic
| Release | 7 July 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_GOUDA
| Effects | Unlocks the Bestiary.
| Location | Due south of Dairy Plant.
| {"description":"Permanently allows to access the list of defeated enemies from the main menu.","id":"RELIC_GOUDA","update":"Patch 0.8.0","effects":"Unlocks the Bestiary.","sprite":"Sprite-Ars Gouda.png","name":"Ars Gouda","release":"7 July 2022","icon":"Icon-Ars Gouda.png","location":"Due south of Dairy Plant."}
Ars Gouda is a relic located in Dairy Plant. Once collected, it permanently unlocks the Bestiary, which is accessed from the Main Menu.
In the Bestiary, the player can view statistics for all the enemies they have killed on their save file. Some entries are colored yellow, which denotes a character unlock being tied to killing a certain number of that enemy.
## Obtaining
The Ars Gouda is located to the far south of the starting area in Dairy Plant. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. Once Ars Gouda has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, the Ars Gouda can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "thistooshouldhavebeenunlockedbydefault".
## Interface
The Ars Gouda/Bestiary in use, particularly opened on the entry of the Pipeestrello.
The Bestiary is divided into three parts: the header, the left panel (which is a list of enemies to the left), and the right panel, which provides more information on a specific enemy.
The header of the Bestiary window indicates two numbers:
- The number of unique enemies ever defeated;
- The maximum number of unique enemies the player can encounter. It can be differentiated from the DLCs the player owns.
The left panel of the Bestiary is a scrollable list of enemies with their bestiary number indicated. If their name is indicated in yellow, this means that they are associated with an unlock (e.g. to unlock  Mortaccio, a total of 3,000 Skeletons must be defeated), otherwise, the enemies are indicated with white-colored names in the list. The currently selected enemy in the Bestiary would have their name highlighted with a yellowish tint and an arrow indicated to the left. An enemy that isn't encountered yet will have their number listed, but their name listed as ---------------.
The right panel of the Bestiary provides information on a selected enemy. From top to bottom, the following information is provided:
- The number of times this enemy has been defeated by the player;
- The looks of the enemy and its variants, shown on the background of a stage that they are from (if they appear in multiple stages, the background will be chosen randomly);
- An entry that describes the enemy;
- The enemy's  HP (how many health points it can have);
- The enemy's  Power (how much damage it can deal to the player);
- The enemy's  Speed (how fast it can be);
- The enemy's resistances;
- The enemy's skills;
- The stages that the enemy appears in;
Clicking on the entry of an enemy that isn't encountered yet will indicate 0 defeated enemies, the image showcasing a question mark on a Mad Forest background, the textual entry being marked as "???", and the values for each stat being marked as "?".
Since the Bestiary is accessed from the Main Menu, there is a red "BACK" button indicated at the top, next to the counter of  Gold Coins. Pressing it will lead back to the Main Menu.
## Trivia
- The name is a pun on the Ars Goetia, the first book of the The Lesser Key of Solomon, a 17th century grimoire which contains descriptions of 72 demons.
- Ars is a Latin word meaning "art" and is often used in ancient book titles, such as "Ars Goetia" meaning the "art of witchcraft". Gouda is a type of cheese, as seen in its sprite having a cheese-like icon and being found in the Dairy Plant.
- The Bestiary entries are written by English-American YouTuber Stephanie Sterling.
- When the player opens the Bestiary, the game will default to opening the first Bestiary entry, which is the  Pipeestrello.
- When the player closes and opens the Bestiary entry of an enemy that appears in multiple stages, the background will be randomly chosen, based on the stages that they appear in. For example, since the Pipeestrello appears in Mad Forest and Gallo Tower, the chosen background in their entry will either by from Mad Forest or from Gallo Tower.
- The icons of an enemy's HP, Power, and Speed, as showcased in its Bestiary entry, are shared with those of the  Max Health/Hollow Heart,  Might/Spinach, and  Move Speed/Wings respectively.

# Astral Stair Map
| Astral Stair Map
| Displays a detailed map when in Astral Stair. Displays a detailed map when in Astral Stair.
| Relic
| Release | 12 June 2023 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_MAP_AS
| Effects | Displays a map of Astral Stair in the pause menu.
| Location | Located in Astral Stair.
| {"description":"Displays a detailed map when in Astral Stair.","id":"RELIC_MAP_AS","update":"Patch 1.5.0","effects":"Displays a map of Astral Stair in the pause menu.","sprite":"","name":"Astral Stair Map","release":"12 June 2023","icon":"","location":"Located in Astral Stair."}
The Astral Stair Map is a relic located in Astral Stair. Once collected, it displays a detailed map of Astral Stair in the pause menu.
## Obtaining
The Astral Stair Map is located to the right from the Astral Stair starting area (where the  Merchant is found), guarded by enemies. It can be located by walking to the bottom-left of the Merchant and entering the closest teleportation door, which will teleport the player to the right. Subsequent doors that lead to the Astral Stair Map can be found on the right of each of the new rooms. The Astral Stair Map's location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. Once the Astral Stair Map has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
The  Milky Way Map must be collected before the Astral Stair Map will function.

# Attractorb
Attractorb
| Attractorb
| Character picks up items from further away. Character picks up items from further away.
| Passive item
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | MAGNET
| Player stat | Magnet
| Rarity | 100
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | See table.
| Stacking | Multiplicative
| Max effect | +300%
| Stage item | Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Tiny Bridge, Mt.Moonspell, Abyss Foscari
| {"description":"Character picks up items from further away.","max-level":"5","id":"MAGNET","stage-item":"Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Tiny Bridge, Mt.Moonspell, Abyss Foscari","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","stacking":"Multiplicative","sprite":"Sprite-Attractorb.png","max-effect":"+300%","name":"Attractorb","stat":"Magnet","release":"27 October 2021","rarity":"100","icon":"Icon-Attractorb.png","per-level":"See table."}
Attractorb is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked upon finding a Vacuum dropped by a light source.
Attractorb gives multiplicative bonuses of varying size to the player's Magnet stat.
Attractorb is required for the evolution of Santa Water, La Borra. It is also required for the evolution of Mirage Robe, J'Odore, in which Attractorb must also be fully leveled.
Attractorb can be found as a stage item in Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Tiny Bridge, Mt.Moonspell, and Abyss Foscari.
## Levels
Attractorb has 5 levels. Unlike other passive items, each level provides a different sized bonus.
| Level | Description | Effect total
| 1 | "Character picks up items from further away." | 1.5x Magnet
| 2 | Pickup range increased by 33%. | 1.995x Magnet
| 3 | Pickup range increased by 25%. | 2.49375x Magnet
| 4 | Pickup range increased by 20%. | 2.9925x Magnet
| 5 | Pickup range increased by 33%. | 3.980025x Magnet
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Santa Water | + | Attractorb | = | La Borra
| Mirage Robe | + | Max level:Attractorb | = | J'Odore
| Diver Mines | + | Attractorb+Weapon Power-Up | = | Atmo-Torpedo
| Dragon Water Whip | + | Attractorb | = | Hydrostormer Tip
| Umbra | + | Max level:Attractorb | = | Vol Umbra
| Screams from the Void | + | Max level:Attractorb | = | Forbidden Siren
## Tips
- Attractorb's bonuses to Magnet apply multiplicatively. Each level will increase your Magnet by a percent of the current total, not the original total. This means that the Attractorb gives you more powerful bonuses as you level it up.
- Each level up approximate to a round multiplier of 1.5x, 2x, 2.5x, 3x, and 4x respectively.
- Attractorb is usually better at increasing experience gain than Crown. While Crown provides small bonuses to the actual value of experience, Attractorb makes experience much easier to collect. Combining the two is the best way to gain experience in the entire game.
- Attractorb also increases the pickup range of other items. This is useful for picking up Floor Chicken and other consumables at large distances, as to avoid putting yourself in a dangerous scenario. Treasure Chests must be walked over no matter how much Magnet one has.
## Trivia
- While the Attractorb and the  Vacuum look very similar, one notable difference between the looks of the two is the fact that the Attractorb has one ring while the Vacuum has two.
=====
# Awake (IV)
| Awake (IV)
| Gives +3 Revivals. Consuming a Revival gives +10% Max Health, +1 Armor, and +5% Might, Area, Duration, and Speed.
| Arcana
| Release | 12 April 2022 (Update)
| Arcana # | 4
| ID | T04_AWAKE
| Icon | 
| Affects | Revival
| Unlocked by | Reach level 50 with Krochi.
| {"notes":"","description":"Gives +3 Revivals. Consuming a Revival gives +10% Max Health, +1 Armor, and +5% Might, Area, Duration, and Speed.","id":"T04_AWAKE","update":"Patch 0.5.0","icon":"","image":"","name":"Awake (IV)","affects":["Revival"],"release":"12 April 2022","unlock":"Reach level 50 with Krochi.","arcana name":"Awake","type":"Arcana"}
Gives +3 Revivals. Consuming a Revival gives +10% Max Health, +1 Armor, and +5% Might, Area, Duration, and Speed.
Awake (IV) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 with  Krochi Freetto.
 Nathan Graves [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Awake (IV) is active, the player gains 3  Revivals. Afterwards, all revives later consumed will also give 1.1x  Max Health, +1  Armor, and +5%  Might,  Area,  Duration, and  Speed. The aforementioned bonus applies to all revives, including those from the  Tirajisú, the Revival PowerUp, or Krochi's passive bonus.
## Tips
- As the player strengthens, it is harder to be killed. It is advised to use your Revivals early to get the stat boost sooner.
-  Phieraggi greatly benefits from this Arcana for getting +3 Base Damage and +3  Amount. It also greatly increases the base damage of the  Meal Ticket [DLC]. Note, however, that using up Revivals will lower the effectiveness of these weapons.
- This Arcana can be used to help characters that start with low health like  Toastie,  Smith IV, and the  Fleaman [DLC] survive.
-  Genevieve Gruyère [DLC] and  Je-Ne-Viv [DLC] trigger a screen-clearing effect once per life; Awake (IV) provides more use out of them.
-  Cosmo Pavone and  Luminaire Foscari [DLC] trigger a Rosary upon reviving. Awake (IV) can enable more uses of this ability.
- Luminaire Foscari has a massive amount of built-in Revives, and gains a powerful temporary bonus every time she revives, scaling with the number of times revived. This makes Awake (IV) a powerful way to scale her over the course of the game.
-  Guardian Pina [DLC] has a 50% chance to gain +1 Revival when reviving. When using Awake (IV) along with items that give Revivals, they can reach a similar level of stat scaling as Luminaire Foscari.
-  Bats Bats Bats gains Amount based on remaining revivals and familiars upon reviving, making Awake a powerful pick.
-  Nameless Fool [DLC] can obtain 3 Darkanas easier with extra Revivals.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Awake", which is the theme song for the Catacombs in Castlevania: Circle of the Moon.
- Awake (IV) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "limperatore", meaning "the emperor" in Italian.
- This makes Awake (IV) a representation of The Emperor Major Arcana card, symbolizing order, power, leadership, responsibility, bravery, fortitude, and discipline.
- This is reflected in its ability to increase the player's stats after using Revivals.
=====
# Banish
Banish
Banish is one of the stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects the amount of times the player can remove an item choice for the rest of the run.
Once an item is banished it can no longer be offered as a level up option. If an owned item is banished or if a banished item is later picked up as a Stage item, it will remain at its current rank, but can no longer be offered upon level ups or upgraded by Treasure Chests. Banished weapons can be offered Limit Break upgrades, once the player's build is fully upgraded if Limit Break is enabled.
There is no upper limit to how many Banishes the player may have.
## PowerUp
| Banish
| Twice per rank, allows you to remove an item from level up choices, for the rest of the run.
| PowerUp
| Release | 25 March 2022 (Update)
| ID | BANISH
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +2
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +10
| Initial cost | 100
| {"description":"Twice per rank, allows you to remove an item from level up choices, for the rest of the run.","cost":"100","id":"BANISH","update":"Patch 0.3.2","image":"","name":"Banish","release":"25 March 2022","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+2"}
The Banish PowerUp increases Banish by 2 per rank, with a maximum rank of 5 after unlocking all Banish achievements. With one rank unlocked it initially costs 100. 
| Rank | Banish
| 1 | +2
| 2 | +4
| 3 | +6
| 4 | +8
| 5 | +10
Each rank of PowerUp is unlocked by these achievements:
- Filling 60 entries in the Collection.
- Filling 70 entries in the Collection.
- Filling 80 entries in the Collection.
- Filling 90 entries in the Collection.
- Filling 100 entries in the Collection.
## From items
Banish does not have an associated passive item.
 Golden Egg permanently increases Banish by 0.2 for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
 Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) provides 3 Banish.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Divano starts with +10 Banish.
-  Sigma starts with +108 Banish.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Menya starts with +8 Banish.
-  Megalo Menya starts with +108 Banish.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Luminaire starts with +5 Banish.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Engineer starts with +20 Banish
## Operation Guns
-  Bill and  Lance start with +4 Banish.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Christopher,  Juste,  Soma and  Lisa start with +5 Banish.
-  Julius,  Dracula,  Charlotte,  Sypha,  Julia,  Carrie,  Yoko,  Rinaldo,  Mina,  Elizabeth,  Vincent,  Shanoa,  Shaft,  Saint Germain,  Barlowe,  Carmilla,  Olrox,  Megalo Elizabeth,  Megalo Olrox,  Megalo Death and  Megalo Dracula start with +10 Banish.
-  Isaac and  Hector start with +20 Banish.
-  Master Librarian starts with +100 Banish.
-  Chaos starts with +65536 Banish.
## Tips
- Banish can be used to limit or prevent undesirable effects from an item's upgrades, such as gaining more  Curse from  Torrona's Box's last upgrade or  Skull O'Maniac when you only need it to evolve  Song of Mana.
- Banishing common items that you are not planning on picking removes them from the item pool and increases the chances to be offered the item you want.
- If you banish an item and pick it up in the level, it cannot be upgraded via level ups.
- As of the 1 April 2026 update picking up a banished item in the level gives the option to "unbanish" it. This allows it to be levelled as usual. You still have the option to pick it up normally, leaving it banished.
- Because banishing an item removes it from the item pool and picking it up in the stage allows it to be unbanished it can be efficient to banish all stage weapons and items in the Collection menu pre-run, get what you want from the item pool and then pick up and unbanish everything in the stage.
## Trivia
- The icon for Banish bears a strong resemblance to the famous Italian hand gesture "Ma cosa vuoi?", literally meaning "Well, what do you want?". The hand gesture can be used in a variety of contexts which can drastically alter its meaning or tone, ranging from lighthearted annoyance to bewildered confusion. The gesture is normally formed with an upward-facing palm and by touching the thumb with the base of the other fingertips, forming a pincer, and accentuated with some slight shaking. Within the context of using Banish on an item, it could be interpreted as signifying an affronted "Well, why are you giving me this?" or "What am I supposed to do with this?". The icon also bears a strong resemblance to the item Vibhuti from Castlevania: Symphony of the Night, though with the hand turned upward instead of down. Combined with the aforementioned reference to the famous hand gesture, this forms a sort of visual pun, taking the Castlevania icon and tweaking it in a humorous way.
=====
# Barrier
DLC Content (Operation Guns)
This article primarily features content only available in DLC, which may not be available in all copies of the game.
| Barrier [DLC]
| Prevents damage from the next incoming attack (max 10 stacks).
| Pickup
| Release | 9 May 2024 (Update)
| ID | FB_BARRIER
| Rarity | 3
| Unlock level | 1
| Affected by luck | Yes
| Effects | Provides a stacking shield that prevents damage form the next attack.
| Notes | Can only appear when playing on Neo Galuga or Hectic Highway, or when playing as a character from the Operation Guns DLC.
| {"notes":"* Can only appear when playing on Neo Galuga or Hectic Highway, or when playing as a character from the Operation Guns DLC.","description":"Prevents damage from the next incoming attack (max 10 stacks).","id":"FB_BARRIER","update":"Vampire Survivors: Operation Guns is out now!","dlc":"og","image":"","name":"Barrier","effects":"Provides a stacking shield that prevents damage form the next attack.","release":"9 May 2024","level":"1","luck":"Yes","rarity":"3"}
Barrier is one of the pickups that has a chance to drop from light sources. Its drop rate is affected by  Luck.
Barrier can only appear when playing in Neo Galuga or Hectic Highway, or as a character from the Operation Guns DLC.  Simondo Belmont is also able to spawn this pickup without the need for light sources.
## Effects
Barrier provides a stacking shield that prevents damage from the next attack. This can stack up to 10 times. After losing the shield, the player will be briefly invulnerable. Unlike the shield from  Laurel and  Crimson Shroud, stacks of the shield can be consumed even during the invulnerable phase.
## Tips
- If possible, try to avoid getting hit rapidly when there are multiple shield stacks. Spreading out the damage taken will allow the player to take full advantage of the shield and invulnerability provided.
=====

The Beast Gate is a relic from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. It is the sixth and final Gate Key that grants access to new areas in the Ode to Castlevania stage, and can be obtained upon defeating  Eligor.
The Beast Gate opens the entrance to the boss chamber of  Beelzebub in the farthest northwestern room of Dracula's Castle and activates the warp gate that teleports the player to the Catacombs.

# Beginning (X)
| Beginning (X)
| Listed weapons get +1 Amount. The character's main weapon and its evolution gain +3 Amount instead.
| Arcana
| Release | 9 June 2022 (Update)
| Arcana # | 10
| ID | T10_BEGINNING
| Icon | 
| Affects | The respective character's starting weapon, its evolutions or unions, as well as:[Bone][Cherry Bomb][Carréllo][Celestial Dusting][La Robba].
| Unlocked by | Reach Level 50 with Antonio.
| {"notes":"","description":"Listed weapons get +1 Amount. The character's main weapon and its evolution gain +3 Amount instead.","id":"T10_BEGINNING","update":"Patch 0.7.2","icon":"","image":"","name":"Beginning (X)","affects":["Bone","Cherry Bomb","Carréllo","Celestial Dusting","La Robba"],"release":"9 June 2022","unlock":"Reach Level 50 with Antonio.","arcana name":"Beginning","type":"Arcana"}
Listed weapons get +1 Amount. The character's main weapon and its evolution gain +3 Amount instead.
Beginning (X) is one of the Arcanas is Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Antonio Belpaese.
 Trevor Belmont [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
When Beginning (X) is active, the character's starting weapon gains +3  Amount. Additionally, the following weapons, if they are not the starting weapon, gain +1  Amount:
-  Bone
-  Carréllo
-  Celestial Dusting
-  Cherry Bomb
-  La Robba
## Tips
- This Arcana is a generically solid first pickup for almost all characters, except those whose starting weapon is not affected by Amount or those who have a very specialized game plan they want to start executing right out of the gate (for example Divine Bloodline builds). It allows most characters to snowball a very powerful early game, although its usefulness later into the game is limited unless the character has an extremely powerful evolved starting weapon.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Beginning", which is the theme song of the Town of Warikiya in Castlevania III: Dracula's Curse.
- Beginning (X) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "laruota", which means "the wheel" in Italian.
- This makes Beginning (X) a representation of the Wheel of Fortune Major Arcana card, symbolizing luck, destiny, karma, prosperity, life cycles, and turning points.
- This is reflected in its ability of granting the player's weapons significantly higher Amount.
- The Chinese character on the arcana is "luck" (運), combined with the name it can form the phrase "Beginner's Luck".
- The old design of Beginning had the Chinese character of "spring" (春) which can tangentially mean beginning.
=====

This pickup is not listed in the Collection.
Big Coin Bag is a pickup item that increases the player's Gold Coins by 25, multiplied by the  Greed stat.
Unlike most pickups, such as  Coin Bag and  Rich Coin Bag, this pickup is not obtained by killing enemies or by destroying light sources. Instead, it is obtained as one of the two options in the level up menu if the player has maxed out the levels of all of the weapons and passives in their possession, assuming that Limit Break is disabled. However, it is still possible to obtain Big Coin Bags through some 3- or 5-item  treasure chests even with Limit Break, though one of the items from the treasure chest would be a  Super Candybox II Turbo.
## Trivia
- Just like the Coin Bag and the Rich Coin Bag, this pickup had an old design.
- The old sprite for the Big Coin Bag is still shown in the Gold Coin counter, though as part of the gold pile icon.

| {"description":"As you can see, this is a Castlevania Black Disk. Cut number one contains 20 more characters, so please do play it.","id":"TP_RELIC_BLACK_DISK","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","dlc":"oc","sprite":"","name":"Black Disk","release":"31 October 2024","location":"Top of the right most tower in Ode to Castlevania, after defeating Death.","icon":"","effects":"Reveals 20 more characters to unlock from Ode to Castlevania, reveals new areas in Ode to Castlevania (stage), and opens the entrance to the Sewers of Dracula's Castle."}
The Black Disk is a relic from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors, which grants the ability to unlock 20 of the DLC's new characters, revealing new locations in the map, and opening the door to the sewers.
## Obtaining
The Black Disk can be obtained by defeating  Death in Ode to Castlevania, which is a boss fight which appears after unlocking  Vlad Tepes Dracula.
## Trivia
- This relic is a reference to a unique secret that occurs if a US-release PlayStation disk of Castlevania: Symphony of the Night is inserted into a CD player. Initially, Alucard will warn the player to not play the disk as it contains the game's computer data, before humorously relenting, which is then followed by it playing a unique "music track". This bonus track is an electronic remix of Dracula's Castle, the main theme of Symphony of the Night.
- Alucard's original message to the player was as follows: "As you can see, this is a PlayStation black disk. Cut number one contains computer data, so please, don't play it. But you probably won't listen to me anyway, will you?" This same message will also be heard when the Black Disk is obtained in Vampire Survivors, but with the word "PlayStation" omitted, due to it being obtained in-game.

The character starts gaining +1% Area every level.","id":"T18_ILLUSIONS","update":"Patch 0.5.2","icon":"","image":"","name":"Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII)","affects":["Area"],"release":"8 May 2022","unlock":"Reach level 50 with Concetta.","arcana name":"Boogaloo of Illusions","type":"Arcana"}
Overall Area continuously changes between -25% and +25% over 10 seconds. The character starts gaining +1% Area every level.
Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Concetta Caciotta.
The  Succubus starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) is active, overall  Area continuously changes between -25% and +25% over 10 second intervals. The player also gains +1% Area each time they level up. The oscillation is done by applying a multiplier on top of everything else ranging from 0.75 to 1.25, which on average, will give an overall net neutral Area bonus.
## Tips
- The Arcana only starts giving stat boosts when the player selects it and does not give extra stats retroactively. If the stats are desirable, select the Arcana early.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Dance of Illusions", which is commonly the theme song for boss battles against Dracula. It was first played during the final boss battle against Dracula in Castlevania: Rondo of Blood.
- Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "laluna", which means "the moon" in Italian.
- This makes Boogaloo of Illusions (XVIII) a representation of The Moon Major Arcana card, symbolizing fear, mysteries, deception, anxiety, darkness, and illusions.
- This is reflected in its ability to amplify the player's Area, making their attacks and projectiles become increasingly larger and more spread out.

| {"description":"Increases projectile speed by 10%.","max-level":"5","id":"SPEED","stage-item":"Gallo Tower, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Lake Foscari.","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","stacking":"Additive","sprite":"Sprite-Bracer.png","max-effect":"+50%","name":"Bracer","stat":"Speed","release":"27 October 2021","rarity":"100","icon":"Icon-Bracer.png","per-level":"+10%"}
Bracer is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by getting King Bible to Level 4.
Bracer increases Projectile Speed by 10% per level.
Bracer is required for the evolution of Knife into Thousand Edge. It is also required for the evolution of Flash Arrow into Millionaire, in which Bracer must be fully leveled.
Bracer can be found as a stage item in Gallo Tower, Moongolow, Boss Rash, and Lake Foscari.
## Levels
Bracer has 5 levels. At max level, Bracer provides +50% Projectile Speed.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Increases projectiles speed by 10%."
| Level 2 | Base speed up by 10%
| Level 3 | Base speed up by 10%
| Level 4 | Base speed up by 10%
| Level 5 | Base speed up by 10%
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Knife | + | Bracer | = | Thousand Edge
| Flash Arrow | + | Max level:Bracer | = | Millionaire
| Short Gun | + | Bracer+Weapon Power-Up | = | Prototype B
| Curved Knife | + | Bracer | = | Bwaka Knife
| Gale Force | + | Max level:Bracer | = | Pneuma Tempestas
| Ammo Appalate | + | Max level:Bracer | = | Gunastrophe
## Tips
- The Collection Page notes that speed is especially important for King Bible, Runetracer, and Bone. This is because the faster the projectiles move, the further they will be able to spin (King Bible) or travel (Runetracer and Bone).
- Speed increases the speed of the bombarding circle of Peachone, Ebony Wings, and Vandalier.
- Speed increases the speed of La Borra's pools, and the speed at which they grow.
- Speed increases the distance that Cross flies before moving in the opposite direction.
- Bracer may be detrimental to weapons that ignore duration and pierce infinitely, such as the Death Spiral or Hellfire. The slow speed of these weapons allow them to pass over more enemies before flying offscreen.

Brave Story is a relic located in Space 54. Once collected, it permanently unlocks Random LevelUp in the stage selection menu. This will prevent level up screens from appearing and instead apply a random upgrade upon leveling up.
## Obtaining
Brave Story will appear north or south of the player at the 18:00 minute mark in Space 54. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. Once the Brave Story has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, the Brave Story can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "lugrianstale".
## Trivia
- The Brave Story’s name, design, and spell all reference Final Fantasy Tactics, which titles its plot as the “Zodiac Brave Story”. The relic’s design of 12 differently colored beads represents the 12 zodiac signs, which affect Tactics’ gameplay. The spell uses the name ‘Lugria’ that is used by protagonist Ramza Beoulve to replace his surname when hiding his identity as a mercenary.
- There was a bug that caused all languages besides English to erroneously display the description of the relic that unlocks  Fever as a PowerUp in the Collection. It has been fixed.

Effect depends on random results and player stats. Luck: improves Treasure Chests and Light Sources. Growth: improves XP Crystals. Greed: improves gold pickups. Curse: enemies gain MoveSpeed[sic] and Health.
Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by ending a run with a  Luck bonus of +999% while playing as  Chula-Reh after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) is active, a small black bar appears under the timer near the top of the screen, cycling through four different symbols much like a slot machine. These slots are rolled every 7 seconds, granting different effects based on which symbol is rolled.
Four possible effects can be rolled, based on the  Growth,  Greed,  Luck, and  Curse stats, each pulsing a translucent aura of their own color and creating an explosion of sprites. The chances for each effect to occur at base are equal. However, the proportion of the player's respective stats affects how commonly an effect will occur. If the same symbol is rolled as the first one with no breaks, the sound effect gets higher in pitch. When four-of-a-kind is rolled, a special effect will happen based on which symbol was rolled, with the fourth pulse being a solid circle of a brighter color and a deep sound effect.
- The exact weight of an effect being rolled is equal to its proportion out of Growth+Greed+Luck+Curse. For example, if Growth = +75%, and all stats total to +200%, then Growth's effect will have a 37.5% chance to be rolled with each roll.
The possible effects are as follows:
-  Growth: Blesses all  Experience Gems on the stage once per symbol rolled, increasing their experience value and oftentimes changing their color. This effect is amplified as more Experience Gems are present. Rolling it pulses cyan, and blue and green Experience Gems explode out.
- Four of a kind: Blesses all Experience Gems an additional 6 times, for a total of 10 instances of the blessing. An ellipsoid consisting of 24  Skelegems is also summoned around the player.
-  Greed: Blesses all  Gold Coins and  Coin Bags present on the stage once per symbol rolled, increasing their gold yield. This effect is amplified as more Gold Coins and Coin Bags are present. Rolling it pulses yellow, and Gold Coins explode out.
- Four of a kind: Blesses all Coins and Coin Bags an additional 6 times, for a total of 10 instances of the blessing. A group of  Anforas and a  Gold Pile are also summoned near the player.
-  Luck: Blesses all visible Light Sources and existing (including off-screen)  Treasure Chests once per symbol rolled, increasing the value of their contents. Rolling it pulses green, and  Little Clovers explode out.
- Four of a kind: Blesses all existing Treasure Chests and Light Sources 6 more times, for a total of 10 instances of the blessing.
- Chests will be improved as they are blessed, scaling from 1 item, to 3 items, to 5 items, to a  Silver Treasure Chest that can evolve weapons, then a  Golden Treasure Chest that grants the player extra weapons, much like those found while playing as  Zi'Appunta Belpaese, while also being able to evolve weapons. The chance for a chest to be upgraded when blessed is 10% if it has been blessed 1-9 times. Upon receiving a 10th blessing, the chest is guaranteed to be upgraded.
- Chests that contain  Arcana, random  Passives, or random  Weapons will not receive the final part of this effect.
- Whenever a light source is blessed, if a  Gold Coin were to drop, it will be replaced with a  Coin Bag. If a Coin Bag were to drop, it will have its gold value increased by 5 times per blessing, with each blessing being additive (i.e., 4 blessings will multiply the gold value by 20 rather than 625). Otherwise, for pickups whose weights are affected by Luck, each blessing to the light source will add an extra +50% Luck when multiply the weights of each pickup by Luck.
- For example, if the player has no extra Luck,  Rosaries from light sources that have been blessed 4 times will have a base weight of 3 from the effective +200% Luck given by blessings.
-  Curse: "Blesses" all currently spawned enemies once per symbol rolled, increasing their Movement Speed and Health by +10%. Rolling it pulses purple, and kill count skulls explode out.
- Four of a kind: Triggers a  Rosary effect, instantly killing all non-instant kill resistant enemies. A  Blinder is also spawned, chasing the player for 30 seconds. If the Blinder is killed, it will drop a Treasure Chest. This Treasure Chest has a chance to be a  Dark Treasure Chest, containing a random passive.
## Tips
- Unlike the actual Curse stat, which increases the number of enemies and therefore the amount of experience the player gains, the Curse effect of Call of a Mad Moon is entirely detrimental unless four-of-a-kind is rolled. Since a high Curse stat skews the odds towards Curse slots but does not guarantee a four-of-a-kind, this Darkana may be detrimental in Curse-centric builds.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Invitation of a Crazed Moon", which is the theme song for the Entrance in Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin.
- Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "etromal", the reverse of "lamorte" or "death" in Italian.
- This makes Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) a representation of the Death Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing stagnation, decay and repetition.
- This is reflected in its ability, which constantly puts the player at risk while also not helping them in any permanent manner.
- The blessing effect exercised by this Darkana for Experience Gems, Gold Coins, and Gold Bags is the same effect as  Luminaire's [DLC] boosts to these pickups besides separating the  Growth and  Greed effect, scaling the same way.
=====

Candelabrador is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by getting Santa Water to Level 4.
Candelabrador increases Area by 10% per level.
Candelabrador is required for the evolution of Axe, Death Spiral. It is also required for the evolution of Four Seasons, Godai Shuffle, in which Candelabrador must also be fully leveled.
Candelabrador can be found as a stage item in Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, and Mt.Moonspell.

Candelabrador has 5 levels. At max level, Candelabrador provides +50% Area.

| Axe | + | Candelabrador | = | Death Spiral
| Four Seasons | + | Max level:Candelabrador | = | Godai Shuffle
| Firearm | + | Candelabrador+Weapon Power-Up | = | Fire-L3GS
| Vibhuti Whip | + | Candelabrador | = | Daybreaker Tip
| Hand Grenade | + | Max level:Candelabrador | = | The RPG
| Incineration | + | Max level:Candelabrador | = | Dust to Dust
## Tips
- The larger a weapon is by default, the more it will benefit from area bonuses. Weapons such as Bloody Tear, Soul Eater, Axe, Death Spiral, Hellfire, and Lightning Ring are examples of weapons that excel when paired with Candelabrador.
- Increasing the Area of King Bible only increases how far away it is from the player.
- Area is important for Area-of-Effect weapons, as the larger they are, the more enemies they will be able to hit. Consider upgrading area when using the Runetracer, Hellfire, and Santa Water.

The character starts gaining +1% projectile Speed every level.","id":"T05_CRASH","update":"Patch 0.5.0","icon":"","image":"","name":"Chaos in the Dark Night (V)","affects":["Speed"],"release":"12 April 2022","unlock":"Reach level 50 with Giovanna.","arcana name":"Chaos in the Dark Night","type":"Arcana"}
Overall projectile Speed continuously changes between -50% and +50% over 10 seconds. The character starts gaining +1% projectile Speed every level.
Chaos in the Dark Night (V) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Giovanna Grana.
 Blackmore [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Chaos in the Dark Night (V) is active, overall  Speed continuously changes between -50% and +50% over 10 second intervals. The player also gains +1% Speed each time they level up. The oscillation is done by applying a multiplier on top of everything else ranging from 0.5 to 1.5, which on average, will give an overall net neutral Projectile Speed bonus. 
This has an interesting interaction with  Peachone,  Ebony Wings, and the  Vandalier, where every 10 seconds the circular zone slows down, stops, changes direction, then accelerates rapidly, and every 10 seconds starting at :05 its speed doubles and halves alternately, in total causing its speed to increase every 20 seconds, causing extremely chaotic movement.
## Tips
- The Arcana only starts giving stat boosts when the player selects it and does not give extra stats retroactively. If the stats are desirable, select the Arcana early.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Into the Dark Night/Crash in the Dark Night", which is the boss battle music of Castlevania: Dawn of Sorrow.
- Chaos in the Dark Night (V) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ilpapa", which means "the pope" in Italian.
- This makes Chaos in the Dark Night (V) a representation of The Hierophant Major Arcana card, symbolizing conformity, righteousness, preservation, and tradition, or in the likelier case of its reversed meaning, unpredictability, challenging the status quo, and rebellion.
- This is reflected in its ability to boost the Projectile Speed of the player's weapons, giving them increasingly more volatile or erratic behavior.
- The eight-pointed symbol in this Arcana is a reference to the Symbol of Chaos, which was first introduced by the literary works of Michael Moorcock, but is more commonly associated with the Warhammer franchise.
=====

Chaos Lazulia is a relic located in Mazerella. Once collected, it permanently allows  Bianca Ramba to morph  Carréllo into  Carrozza! upon reaching level 80.
## Obtaining
In Mazerella, Chaos Lazulia will spawn to the player's left (right in  Inverse Mode with the "Visually Invert Stages" Option enabled) and be promptly stolen by the female dancer. Chasing this dancer to the wooden side room at the far left of the stage and then defeating her will drop the relic on the floor, free for the player to pick up. Once collected, it will not appear again.
Alternatively, Chaos Lazulia can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "shoppingcarttheory".
## Trivia
- Chaos Lazulia,  Chaos Malachite,  Chaos Rosalia, and  Chaos Altemanna are likely references to the Chaos Emeralds from the Sonic the Hedgehog game series. They are gems that hold miraculous powers, one of the most well known being the ability to initiate super transformations.
- The spell "shoppingcarttheory" is a reference to an internet meme about whether someone returns the shopping cart back to the proper place or not is a judgment of their character and ethics.

# Charm
Charm is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It buffs the spawn quantity of enemies.
## Effects
Charm increases the quantity of enemies in each wave by a flat amount; the amount of which corresponds to the current value of the player's Charm stat.
It is added last after other bonuses that affect enemy spawn quantity, such as  Curse. This results in waves always spawning a fixed value of additional enemies which may cause the 500 maximum enemy quantity limit to be reached and constantly filled.
## PowerUp
| Charm
| Increases enemies spawn quantity by 20 per rank (max +100).
| PowerUp
| Release | 9 February 2023 (Update)
| ID | CHARM
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +20
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +100
| Initial cost | 10,000
| {"description":"Increases enemies spawn quantity by 20 per rank (max +100).","cost":"10000","id":"CHARM","update":"Patch 1.3.0","image":"","name":"Charm","release":"9 February 2023","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+20"}
The Charm PowerUp increases the quantity of enemies spawned by 20 per rank, with a maximum rank of 5. At rank 5, it increases enemy quantity by 100. It initially costs 10,000, and is unlocked by collecting  Apoplexy in Bat Country.
| Level | Charm
| 1 | +20
| 2 | +40
| 3 | +60
| 4 | +80
| 5 | +100
## From items
Charm's associated passive item is  Karoma's Mana, which provides +10 Charm per level, up to a total of +50 Charm at level 5.
 Gold Finger sets the player's Charm at +500, overriding the player's pre-existing Charm stat throughout its duration.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Rose starts with +100 Charm.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Elizabeth and  Megalo Elizabeth start with +50 Charm.
## Emerald Diorama
-  Macha Alter Ego starts with +50 Charm.
## Tips
- Charm increases enemies spawned by a flat amount, while  Curse increases it by a percentage. With both, each wave of enemies can spawn almost the max amount allowed on screen most of the time, making it very powerful to increase experience gain.
## Trivia
- Charm,  Fever,  Defang,  Preserve, Invulnerability Time, and Thorns are the only character stats in the game that are not displayed on the stat panel.
- The highest Charm stat that can be attained in a run is +250, which can be done by playing as  Rose De Infernas (+100), with the level 5 Charm PowerUp (+100), and level 5  Karoma's Mana (+50).

| {"description":"Character gets 10% luckier.","max-level":"5","id":"LUCK","stage-item":"Mad Forest, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Lake Foscari","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","stacking":"Additive","sprite":"Sprite-Clover.png","max-effect":"+50%","name":"Clover","stat":"Luck","release":"27 October 2021","rarity":"100","icon":"Icon-Clover.png","per-level":"+10%"}
Clover is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by finding a Little Clover from a light source.
Clover increases Luck by 10% per level.
Clover is required for the evolution of Cross, Heaven Sword. It is also required for the evolution of Santa Javelin, Seraphic Cry in which it must also be fully leveled.
Clover can be found as a stage item in Mad Forest, Moongolow, Boss Rash, and Lake Foscari.
## Levels
Clover has 5 levels. At max level, Clover provides +50% Luck.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Character gets 10% luckier."
| Level 2 | Chance to get lucky up by 10%
| Level 3 | Chance to get lucky up by 10%
| Level 4 | Chance to get lucky up by 10%
| Level 5 | Chance to get lucky up by 10%
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Cross | + | Clover | = | Heaven Sword
| Santa Javelin | + | Max level:Clover | = | Seraphic Cry
| Blade Crossbow | + | Clover+Weapon Power-Up | = | BFC2000-AD
| Platinum Whip | + | Clover | = | Cross Crasher Tip
| Refectio | + | Max level:Clover | = | Sanctuary
## Tips
- In terms of weapons, the Clover's benefits seem weak, but Luck has a large influence on many other game mechanics, such as the drop chances of items in light sources, the quality of Treasure Chests, and the chance of bad events happening (Swarm spawning). These effects can be extremely desirable and a large difference maker in a run.

# Cooldown
Cooldown is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects the length of the delay between attacks done by most weapons.
The player has 100% base Cooldown before modifications, such as the characters bonuses or PowerUps. The Cooldown in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base Cooldown, meaning that a Cooldown stat of -30% equals to 70% total Cooldown, for example. Cooldown modifications stack additively.
The lower limit of total Cooldown is 10%.
 Browny [DLC] gains +2%  Area per -1% Cooldown.
## PowerUp
| Cooldown
| Uses weapons 2.5% faster per rank (max 5%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | COOLDOWN
| Max level | 2
| Bonus per level | -2.5%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | −5%
| Initial cost | 900
| {"description":"Uses weapons 2.5% faster per rank (max 5%).","cost":"900","id":"COOLDOWN","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"","name":"Cooldown","release":"27 October 2021","stacking":"Additive","max level":"2","bonus":"-2.5%"}
The Cooldown PowerUp decreases Cooldown by 2.5% per rank, with a maximum rank of 2. At rank 2, it provides -5% Cooldown. It initially costs 900.
| Rank | Cooldown
| 1 | -2.5%
| 2 | -5%
## From items
Cooldown's associated passive item is the  Empty Tome, which reduces Cooldown by -8% per level, down to a maximum of -40% Cooldown at level 5.
-  Gold Finger provides -100% Cooldown for 10 seconds ( Hail from the Future (X) helps with getting these often).
-  Rapid Fire [DLC] provides -30% Cooldown for 10 seconds, stacking up to -100% total character Cooldown.
-  Centralis Custos [DLC] provides -10% Cooldown when the player character takes damage from enemies.
-  Trinum Custodem [DLC] provides a passive -10% Cooldown and an additional -10% Cooldown when the player character takes damage from enemies.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] has a chance of granting a Cooldown stat bonus of -3% upon activation.
-  Golden Egg can permanently reduce Cooldown by -0.5% for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
-  Tragic Princess (III) decreases Cooldown when moving. It is not limited by the -90% Cooldown cap.
-  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) provides -8% Cooldown when chosen per active weapon slot left empty, for a maximum of -40% Cooldown
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Arca reduces Cooldown by -5% every 5 levels, up to -15% at level 15.
-  Porta starts with -90% Cooldown, every level increases by +30% until it returns to normal by level 4.
-  Christine and  Sigma start with -25% Cooldown.
-  Gallo starts with -15% Cooldown.
-  Exdash and  Toastie both start with +10% Cooldown.
-  Smith starts with +10% Cooldown and reduces Cooldown by -0.25% every level, with no cap.
-  Avatar reduces Cooldown by -0.25% every level, with no cap.
-  Minnah's Cooldown cycles between multiplied by 2 and divided by 2 every minute.
-  Leda starts with -10% Cooldown.
-  Santa starts with -70 Cooldown and increases by 20% at levels 2 and 3 until she has -10% Cooldown.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Menya starts with -10% Cooldown. She gains -20% Cooldown in her boosted state.
-  Babi-Onna is not affected by Cooldown. She attacks by posing and its rate is increased by  Move Speed.
-  Megalo Menya starts with -20% Cooldown.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Keitha starts with -10% Cooldown.
-  Luminaire gains -100% Cooldown temporarily after reviving.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate has a chance to lose a random amount of Cooldown every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Impostor gains -20% Cooldown while in their boosted state.
-  Megalo Impostor starts with -10% Cooldown.
## Operation Guns
-  Lance gains -1% Cooldown at specific kill thresholds up to 25%.
-  Lucia gains -3% Cooldown after reviving, with no cap.
-  Brad gains up to -50% Cooldown based on missing percentage of  Max Health scaling linearly.
-  Sheena gains -1% Cooldown per 2%  Move Speed.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Mina,  Eric and  Nathan start with -5% Cooldown.
-  Richter and  Succubus start with -10% Cooldown.
-  Megalo Elizabeth,  Megalo Olrox,  Megalo Death and  Megalo Dracula start with -20% Cooldown.
-  Saint Germain reduces Cooldown by 0.2% every level, with no cap.
-  Leon reduces Cooldown by 5% upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
-  Sonia reduces Cooldown by 30% for 10 seconds when overhealing by +8.
-  Shaft reduces Cooldown by 20% during his HP-Critical ability.
## Tips
- There is a hard cap on Cooldown reduction which is -90% and it cannot get lower than that. At -90% weapons already fire 10 times as fast. Each reduction will make it exponentially quicker, for example, if reaching -95% Cooldown with a 5% reduction were possible, it would be 20 times quicker, and another 4% reduction would be 100 times quicker. This makes each reduction from any sources very powerful and should not be seen as a simple additive bonus.
- Cooldown reduction stacks additively. Empty Tome can provide -8% Cooldown per level, up to a total of -40%. A maxed Empty Tome combined with the Cooldown PowerUp totals -45% Cooldown. With Silent Old Sanctuary and one weapon, your Cooldown will total -85%.
- Cooldown reduction bonuses are useful for weapons that fire slowly, and less useful for weapons that fire quickly or without Cooldown. For example,  Fire Wand benefits greatly from cooldown reduction, but  Knife may be capped out by pool limit.
