# Crystal Cries (XII)
| Crystal Cries (XII)
| Defeating frozen enemies gives bonus MaxHealth[sic], Recovery, and Growth. Triggers an Orologion when health reaches a critically low level.
| Darkana
| Release | 15 August 2024 (Update)
| Darkana # | 12
| ID | D12_CRYSTAL_CRIES
| Icon | 
| Affects | [Hollow Heart] [Pummarola] [Crown] [Orologion] [Crystallized Soul]
| Unlocked by | Trigger a Starry Heavens with She-Moon Eeta.
| {"notes":"","description":"Defeating frozen enemies gives bonus MaxHealth[sic], Recovery, and Growth. Triggers an Orologion when health reaches a critically low level.","id":"D12_CRYSTAL_CRIES","update":"Patch 1.11: Bug fixes and improvements","icon":"","image":"","name":"Crystal Cries (XII)","affects":["Max Health","Recovery","Growth","Orologion","Crystallized Soul"],"release":"15 August 2024","unlock":"Trigger a Starry Heavens with She-Moon Eeta.","arcana name":"Crystal Cries","type":"Darkana"}
Defeating frozen enemies gives bonus MaxHealth[sic], Recovery, and Growth. Triggers an Orologion when health reaches a critically low level.
Crystal Cries (XII) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by triggering a  Starry Heavens, the upgraded form of an  Orologion granted by  Celestial Voulge, while playing as  She-Moon Eeta after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Crystal Cries (XII) is active, defeating frozen enemies causes them to drop  Crystallized Souls, pickups which increase  Growth,  Recovery, and  Max Health. While individually these bonuses are miniscule, the Crystallized Souls gathered can quickly increase a player's stats. The value of the Crystallized Souls increases the more the player collects because they grant Growth, which in turn increases their own value. There is also a 200-250 pickup limit for Crystallized Souls that can be on the stage at any point.
- The stats provided by  Crystallized Souls are based on the base  Experience value of the enemy that dropped it. The stat bonuses provided by a Crystallized Soul are:
-  Max Health = 0.06 * Base XP Value
-  Recovery = 0.0003 * Base XP Value
-  Growth = 0.03% * Base XP Value
- For example, a  Skelegem, with a Base XP of 3, will drop a Crystallized Soul that gives +0.18 Max Health, +0.0009 Recovery, and +0.09% Growth.
Additionally, Crystal Cries will trigger an HP-Critical  Orologion when the character's Health hits 20%. This ability recovers once the character is healed back to full Max Health.
## Tips
- Because this Darkana affects Recovery and Growth, it pairs well with  Sharp Tongue [DLC]/  Impostongue [DLC],  Star Flail [DLC]/ Moon Rod [DLC],  Wind Whip [DLC]/ Spirit Tornado Tip [DLC], and  Vol Luminatio [DLC], even despite not being freeze-inducing weapons.
- It also synergizes with  Mazo Familiar due to increasing max Health, though not as well as  Divine Bloodline (IX).
- The  Attractorb can be very useful for gathering Crystallized Souls, and it is also the evolution item for the  Mirage Robe [DLC] /  J'Odore [DLC], which synergizes with freezing builds.
-  Hail from the Future (X) synergizes perfectly with Crystal Cries (XII), as  Hail from the Future (X) not only drops  Orologions, but also drops powerful pickups that helps the player clean frozen enemies quickly; killing frozen enemies quickly means dropping more Crystallized Souls, faster increasing Growth (as well as Max Health and Recovery), faster leveling up, and dropping more  Orologions and pickups.
- Due to the limited amount of Crystallized Souls that can be on the stage at a given time,  Sole Solution may be detrimental if combined with Crystal Cries (XII), as collected Crystallized Souls will still count towards the limit during the intangibility effect.
- Due to triggering an  Orologion upon reaching 20% health, this Darkana pairs very well with any Emerald Diorama character, as it will activate at the same time as their Showstopper. This usually will generate enough Crystallized Souls to top off the player's health, thus re-arming the Orologion for another cycle.
- This Darkana and  Heir of Fate (XIX) synergize quite well, as defeating a frozen enemy with a  Fire damage based weapon will drop both a Crystallized Soul and a  Scorched Soul. This grants a player double the health increase, with a bonus to  Might on top of it.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Crystal Teardrops", which is the theme song for the Underground Caverns in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Crystal Cries (XII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "oseppal", the reverse of "l'appeso" or "the hanged man" in Italian.
- This makes Crystal Cries (XII) a representation of The Hanged Man Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing apathy, new insights, and avoiding sacrifices.
- This is reflected in its ability to increase the player's survivability by killing frozen enemies.
- Crystal Cries (XII) is the opposite of  Out of Bounds (XII), as it gives freezing a utility function (increasing Recovery, Max Health, and Growth) while Out of Bounds (XII) gives it an offensive function (makes freezing generate damaging explosions).
=====

# Curse
Curse
Curse is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It buffs the speed, health, spawn frequency, and quantity of enemies.
The player has 100% base Curse before modifications. The Curse in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base Curse, meaning that Curse stat of +30% equals to 130% total Curse, for example. Curse modifications from character bonuses, PowerUps and items stack additively.
There is no upper limit to how much Curse the player may have.
## Effects
Curse increases the frequency and quantity of enemy waves, as well as their speed and health, by the percentage of Curse. The multipliers to health, speed, and enemy minimum stack multiplicatively with other effects of the same kind.
The new spawn interval with any amount of Curse can be calculated as follows:
effectiveSpawnInterval=spawnIntervaltotalCurse
When Curse is gained during a run, the health multiplier effect is immediately applied to all newly spawned enemies. All other effects are applied at the start of the next minute.
Total enemy quantity allowed on screen is capped at 500, regardless of Curse and  Charm value.
Curse also affects:
- Base Damage of  Hex [DLC]
- Instant-killing chance of  Sonic Whip [DLC]
- Base Damage of  108 Bocce [DLC] if Curse is higher than  Luck
- Damage of explosion summoned by  Twilight Requiem (II)
 Baal'Thasar [DLC],  Baal'Thamut [DLC],  Colonel Bahamut [DLC],  Vlad Tepes Dracula [DLC], and  Megalo Dracula [DLC] gain +1%  Might per +1% Curse.

## From items
Curse's associated passive item is  Skull O'Maniac, which provides +10% Curse per level, up to a maximum of +50% Curse at level 5.
 Gold Ring and  Metaglio Right both provide +5% per level, up to +40% each at level 9.
 Torrona's Box also provides +100% at level 9.
 Golden Egg can permanently increases Curse by 1% for the character that collected it.
 Road to Heaven [DLC] decreases curse as it levels up, to a maximum of -90% at level 9.
 Nightmare [DLC] creates green plague balls that increases Curse by 100% when stepping in (which can stack up from each plague balls).
## From arcana
-  Hidden Anathema (III) may increase Curse when a food pickup is collected.
-  Wicked Season (XIII) doubles Curse in certain 10 second intervals, and gives +0.5% Curse every level.
-  Victorian Horror (XVIII) generates  Cursed Souls when killing enemies that can be collected to slightly increase  Max Health,  Greed and Curse.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Lama starts with +10% Curse and gains +5% Curse every 10 levels, up to +20% at level 40. In total, she can have +30% Curse.
-  Concetta,  Zi'Assunta,  Marrabbio, and  Sigma start with +10% Curse.
-  Gyorunton and  Gyoruntin gain +1% Curse every level, with no cap.
-  Avatar gains 0.5% Curse every level, with no cap.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Menya gains +50% Curse when she is invincible.
-  Syuuto and  Megalo Syuuto start with +50% Curse.
## Legacy of the Bloodmoon
-  Megalo Sargon starts with +50% Curse and gains +0.5% Curse every level, with no cap.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Maruto and  Keitha start with +10% Curse.
-  Je-Ne-Viv gains +0.5% Curse every level, with no cap.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% Curse per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% Curse per level.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Axe Armor starts with +10% Curse and gains +5% Curse every 10 levels, up to +20% at level 40. In total, it can have +30% Curse.
-  Shaft gains +1% Curse every level, with no cap. He also gains +50% Curse during his HP-Critical ability.
-  Joachim can select to gain +1% Curse per level.
-  Blackmore can select to gain +1% Curse per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% Curse per level.
-  Barlowe gains +1% Curse per 24 health of overhealing, with no cap.
-  Stone Skull starts with +50% Curse.
## Emerald Diorama
-  Malevolent Door Spirit starts with +100% Curse.
## Tips
- Upon completing a run of at least 20 minutes with at least 10% Curse active, it will unlock the character  Lama Ladonna.
- While increasing the stats of the enemies may not seem like the best idea, more enemies and higher frequency of waves greatly increases the amount of enemies you can kill, which means more  Experience Gems.
- Curse stat is additive.

# Cursed Soul
This pickup is not listed in the Collection.
| Cursed Soul
| Pick up to get a minor bonus to MaxHealth[sic], Greed, and Curse.
| Pickup
| Release | 28 October 2025 (Update)
| ID | BONUS_CURSEDSOUL
| Rarity | 0
| Unlock level | 1,000,000
| Affected by luck | No
| Effects | Increases Max Health, Greed, and Curse by a minuscule amount that is multiplied by the Base XP Value of the enemy that dropped it.
| {"rarity":"0","id":"BONUS_CURSEDSOUL","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","effects":"Increases Max Health, Greed, and Curse by a minuscule amount that is multiplied by the Base XP Value of the enemy that dropped it.","image":"","name":"Cursed Soul","release":"28 October 2025","level":"1000000","luck":"No","description":"Pick up to get a minor bonus to MaxHealth[sic], Greed, and Curse."}
The Cursed Soul is a pickup item in Vampire Survivors. It is dropped by defeating enemies with  Insatiable or Cursed World Eater provided by  Victorian Horror (XVIII).
The stat bonuses provided by a Cursed Soul are based on the Base XP value of the enemy that dropped it:
-  Max Health = 0.1 * Base XP
-  Greed = 0.05% * Base XP
-  Curse = 0.05% * Base XP
For example, a  Skelegem, with a Base XP of 3, will drop a Cursed Soul that grants +0.3 Max Health, +0.15% Greed, and +0.15% Curse.
## Trivia
- This pickup's design combines the inner shading of the  Pummarola (but it is purple instead) and the outer shape of the  Hollow Heart.

# Darkasso
This article is about the relic. For other uses, see Darkasso (disambiguation).
| Darkasso
| Enables the unlocking and activation of Darkanas. Enables the unlocking and activation of Darkanas.
| Relic
| Release | 15 August 2024 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_DARKASSO
| Effects | Unlocks Darkanas.
| Location | Due south of Room 1665.
| {"description":"Enables the unlocking and activation of Darkanas.","id":"RELIC_DARKASSO","update":"Patch 1.11: Bug fixes and improvements","effects":"Unlocks Darkanas.","sprite":"Sprite-Darkasso.png","name":"Darkasso","release":"15 August 2024","icon":"Icon-Darkasso.png","location":"Due south of Room 1665."}
Darkasso is a relic located in Room 1665. Once collected, it permanently extends the Arcana system to contain Darkanas, and awards  Moonlight Bolero (VI).
## Obtaining
After unlocking Room 1665, the player must progress down through each of the rooms by killing enemies, where the Darkasso is located in the center of the last room after killing 1665 enemies in total. Walking towards the Darkasso plays an animation, causing it to be automatically collected.
Alternatively, the Darkasso can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "darkassami".
## Notes
- In the items panel at the bottom of the Darkasso, currently obtained items are indicated through a yellow circle and are positioned before unobtained weapons/items.

# Defang
Defang is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It renders certain enemies unable to harm the player.
## Effects
Defang turns a percentage of enemies from each spawn interval unable to deal damage to the player. Enemies affected by Defang are visually identified by a green appearance, which the player can disable in the settings.
Some enemies cannot be Defanged. This includes, but is not limited to: wave bosses, enemies from wave events, and special enemies such as Atlanteans.
## PowerUp

| 3% of enemies spawn unable to deal damage per rank (max 15%).
| PowerUp

| ID | DEFANG

| Bonus per level | +3%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +15%
| Initial cost | 10

The Defang PowerUp increases the quantity of enemies unable to deal damage by 3% per rank, with a maximum rank of 5. At rank 5, 15% of enemies are defanged. It initially costs 10, and is unlocked by collecting  Antidote in Whiteout.
| Level | Defang
| 1 | +3%

| 5 | +15%
## From items
The weapons  Time Warp,  Gaze of Gaea, and  Embrace of Gaea have a chance to Defang enemies. The chance of it happening is affected by  Luck.
## From characters
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Astarte starts with +15% Defang.
## Trivia
- Defang,  Charm,  Fever,  Preserve, Invulnerability Time, and Thorns are the only character stats in the game that are not displayed on the stat panel.

| {"notes":"Big Coin Bags from Treasure Chests and level ups are also affected.","description":"Picking up coin bags from the floor triggers Gold Fever. Obtaining gold restores as many HP.","id":"T15_GOLD","update":"Patch 0.5.2","icon":"","image":"","name":"Disco of Gold (XV)","affects":["Vicious Hunger","Greed","Coin Bag","Big Coin Bag","Rich Coin Bag","Treasure Chest","Gold Coin (pickup)"],"release":"8 May 2022","unlock":"Reaching minute 31 in the Inlaid Library.","arcana name":"Disco of Gold","type":"Arcana"}
Picking up coin bags from the floor triggers Gold Fever. Obtaining gold restores as many HP.
Disco of Gold (XV) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching the 31:00 mark in Inlaid Library. 
 Vincent Dorin [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Disco of Gold (XV) is active, collecting any amount of gold will heal the player by the same amount, and scales up with Greed. Additionally, collecting  Coin Bags and  Rich Coin Bags will trigger Gold Fever. Affected gold sources include: 
-  Vicious Hunger
-  Gold Coins
-  Coin Bags
-  Rich Coin Bags
-  Treasure Chests
-  Big Coin Bags obtained from Treasure Chests and level ups when all of the player's weapons and passive items are fully maxed out.
## Tips
- Disco of Gold synergises perfectly with effects based on overhealing, like  Guardian's Targe [DLC].
- As Gold Fevers will generate a lot of coins and healing by killing enemies, pairing up with  Sarabande of Healing (VI) will double the healing and turn them into damaging pulses, fueling the killing spree even more.
- Pairing up with  Wandering the Jet Black (XXI) converts the healing from gold into more damage when it retaliates.
- This Arcana synergizes well with  Max Health to make use of the excess amount of healing it can generate. As one good Gold Fever can even heal level 100+  Toastie to full.
- With  Greed, it further increases the effectiveness of the healing and benefits Gold Fever significantly. It also boost Big Coin Bags from leveling up enough to be a better source of healing than  Floor Chicken while also giving the player more gold.
- However,  Recovery cannot multiply the healing from the coins. Other means that directly multiplies the healing effect like Sarabande of Healing (VI) or  Miang Moonspell [DLC]'s passive multiplier based on Recovery can increase it besides Greed.
- Starting a Gold Fever much easier greatly benefits  Millionaire [DLC] as the generated coins can create a lot of ammo for the weapon.
- With  Vicious Hunger, Disco of Gold and enough Greed, a character can become nearly immortal.
- This Arcana is extremely useful in The Bone Zone as it provides a source of healing in a level where Floor Chicken does not spawn, but coin bags are plentiful due to light sources spawning nothing but gold.
- This Arcana is a must-have on  Sammy and makes it nearly immortal, though  Wicked Season (XIII) provides more levels.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Dance of Gold", which is the theme song of the Alchemy Laboratory in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Disco of Gold (XV) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ildiavolo", which means "the devil" in Italian.
- This makes Disco of Gold (XV) a representation of The Devil Major Arcana card, symbolizing personal vices, lust, addiction, temptation, instant gratification, and greed.
- This is reflected in its ability to benefit the player's own Greed by triggering more Gold Fevers and healing off gold coins.
- The card for Disco of Gold was originally mislabeled as 'XIV' instead of 'XV'. This was corrected with the addition of  Jail of Crystal (XIV).

# Duplicator
Duplicator
| Duplicator
| Weapons fire more projectiles. Weapons fire more projectiles.
| Passive item
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | AMOUNT
| Player stat | Amount
| Rarity | 50
| Max level | 2
| Bonus per level | +1
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +2
| Stage item | Cappella Magna, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Mt.Moonspell
| {"description":"Weapons fire more projectiles.","max-level":"2","id":"AMOUNT","stage-item":"Cappella Magna, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Mt.Moonspell","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","stacking":"Additive","sprite":"Sprite-Duplicator.png","max-effect":"+2","name":"Duplicator","stat":"Amount","release":"27 October 2021","rarity":"50","icon":"Icon-Duplicator.png","per-level":"+1"}
Duplicator is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by getting the Magic Wand to Level 7.
Duplicator increases Amount by +1 per level.
Duplicator is required for the evolution of Lightning Ring, Thunder Loop. It is also required for the evolution of Summon Night, Echo Night, in which Duplicator must also be fully leveled.
Duplicator can be found as a stage item in Cappella Magna, Moongolow, Boss Rash, and Mt.Moonspell.
## Levels
Duplicator has 2 levels. At max level, Duplicator provides +2 Amount.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Weapons fire more projectiles."
| Level 2 | Fires 1 more projectile.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Lightning Ring | + | Duplicator | = | Thunder Loop
| Summon Night | + | Max level:Duplicator | = | Echo Night
| Homing Miss | + | Duplicator+Weapon Power-Up | = | Multistage Missiles
| Fulgur | + | Max level:Duplicator | = | Tenebris Tonitrus
| Trident | + | Max level:Duplicator | = | Gungnir-Souris
| Ashella | + | Max level:Duplicator | = | Bloodlust
## Tips
- Duplicator is a great passive item, as it boosts the effectiveness of most weapons by a large amount. Extra projectiles leads to extra damage and extra protection from enemies.
- Amount bonuses work for some weapons better than others. High-damage weapons with a low rate of fire, such as the  Axe and  Fire Wand, perform better when paired with the Duplicator, but weapons such as the  Magic Wand benefit less due to the already high amount of low-damage projectiles.
## Trivia
- Duplicator and  Tirajisú are the only passive items with a max level of 2 instead of the usual 5.
- Duplicator is likely a reference to the Duplicator accessory of the same name from Castlevania: Symphony of the Night, which allows items to be used an infinite number of times, at the cost of decreased stats and being an extremely expensive item purchaseable only after finishing the game once beforehand.

# Duration
Duration
Duration is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects how long the effects of certain weapons last.
The player has 100% base Duration before modifications. The Duration in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base Duration, meaning that Duration stat of +30% equals to 130% total Duration, for example. Duration modifications from characters, PowerUps, and items stack additively. Other modifiers, such as Arcanas, stack multiplicatively with the collective bonus from the aforementioned.
The upper limit of total Duration after character, PowerUp and item modifications is 500%. Bonuses from Arcanas are unaffected by the limit.
Duration also affects:
- Base Damage of  Four Seasons [DLC],  Godai Shuffle [DLC] and  Hydro Storm [DLC]
- Critical Damage of  Rune Sword [DLC]
- Amount of  Santa Javelin
## PowerUp
| Duration
| Effects from weapons last 15% longer per rank (max +30%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | DURATION
| Max level | 2
| Bonus per level | +15%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +30%
| Initial cost | 300
| {"description":"Effects from weapons last 15% longer per rank (max +30%).","cost":"300","id":"DURATION","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"","name":"Duration","release":"27 October 2021","stacking":"Additive","max level":"2","bonus":"+15%"}
The Duration PowerUp increases Duration by +15% per rank, with a maximum rank of 2. At rank 2, it provides +30% Duration. It initially costs 300.
| Level | Duration
| 1 | +15%
| 2 | +30%
## From items
Duration's associated passive item is the  Spellbinder, which provides +10% Duration per level, up to a maximum of +50% Duration at level 5.
-  Silver Ring also provides +5% per level, up to a maximum of +40% at level 9.
-  Torrona's Box provides +4%, then +3% per level, up to a maximum of +25% at level 8.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] has a chance of granting a Duration stat bonus of +4% upon activation.
-  Golden Egg can permanently increase Duration by +1% for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
-  Awake (IV) provides +5% Duration when chosen per  Revival consumed.
-  Lost & Found Painting (XVII) provides a multiplier cycling between 0.5 to 1.5 every 10 seconds, and gives +1% Duration every level.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Dommario starts with +40% Duration.
-  Poppea gains +1% Duration every level, with no cap.
-  Gallo starts with +15% Duration.
-  Zi'Assunta gains +0.5% Duration every level, with no cap.
-  Exdash and  Toastie start with -10% Duration.
-  Smith starts with -10% Duration and gains +0.7% Duration every level, with no cap.
-  Minnah's Duration cycles between multiplied by 2 and divided by 2 every minute.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate has a chance to gain a random amount of Duration every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% Duration per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% Duration per level.
## Operation Guns
-  Lucia gains +4% Duration after reviving, with no cap.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Juste,  Charlotte,  Barlowe and  Olrox start with +20% Duration.
-  Sypha,  Sara and  Charlotte & Jonathan start with +40% Duration.
-  Shaft starts with +50% Duration.
-  Joachim can select to gain +1% Duration per level.
-  Blackmore starts with +40% Duration and can select to gain +1% Duration per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% Duration per level.
## Tips
- Duration stat is additive. The highest possible Duration bonus can be attained while playing as Poppea, with the rank 2 Duration PowerUp (+30%), rank 5 Omni PowerUp (+10%), level 5 Spellbinder (+50%), level 9 Silver Ring (+40%), and level 8 Torrona's Box (+25%) for a total of +(player level+155)% Duration.
- Duration is not used by many weapons, but the weapons it does benefit, benefit greatly from it
- Weapons which benefit from duration King Bible, Santa Water, Runetracer, Peachone, Ebony Wings, Gatti Amari, Song of Mana, Shadow Pinion, Clock Lancet, Vento Sacro, Bone, Cherry Bomb, Celestial Dusting, La Robba, Greatest Jubilee, Bracelet, Flames of Misspell, Silver Wind, Four Seasons, Summon Night, Mirage Robe, Mille Bolle Blu, SpellString, SpellStream, Shadow Servant
- Evolutions which benefit from duration Unholy Vespers, La Borra, NO FUTURE, Vicious Hunger, Mannajja, Valkyrie Turner, Infinite Corridor, Bi-Bracelet, Tri-Bracelet, Ashes of Muspell, Anima of Mortaccio, Yatta Daikarin, Festive Winds, Godai Shuffle, Echo Night, J'Odore, Boo Roo Boolle, Legionnaire, Ophion
- Unions which benefit from duration Vandalier, Fuwalafuwaloo, SpellStrom
- It is a great early pick for Pasqualina Belpaese and Mortaccio, due to their starting weapons and abilities.
- Minnah's stat cycling cannot stack with Lost & Found Painting (XVII).
## Trivia
- Duration's old design is very similar to whip upgrades from Castlevania: Harmony of Dissonance, especially the bullet tip upgrade.

Also reveals their corresponding Megalo forms as secret characters."}
The Ebony and Crimson Stones are a relic from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors which grants  Vlad Tepes Dracula [DLC],  Elizabeth Bartley [DLC],  Count Olrox [DLC], and  Death [DLC] significantly more powerful transformations under certain conditions and allows for unlocking their secret Megalo variants.
## Obtaining
The Ebony and Crimson Stones can be obtained by defeating  Legion in Ode to Castlevania to the farthest southeast of Dracula's Castle, which only appears after obtaining the  Black Disk. However, the door to Legion's boss fight can only be opened after obtaining all of the gate keys, including the  Serpent Gate and the  Beast Gate.
## Morphs
| Character | | Relic | | Condition | | Evolution
| Dracula [DLC] | + | [Ebony and Crimson Stones] | + | Reach level 80 | = | Megalo Dracula [DLC]
| Elizabeth [DLC] | + | [Ebony and Crimson Stones] | + | Reach certain kill thresholds | = | Megalo Elizabeth [DLC]
| Olrox [DLC] | + | [Ebony and Crimson Stones] | + | Reach certain kill thresholds | = | Megalo Olrox [DLC]
| Death [DLC] | + | [Ebony and Crimson Stones] | + | Reach level 80 | = | Megalo Death [DLC]

Allows to vacuum Treasure Chests, Light Sources, and Weapons.","id":"D08_EDGE_OF_THE_EARTH","update":"Vampire Survivors: Legacy of the Bloodmoon is coming this summer!","icon":"","image":"","name":"Edge of the Earth (VIII)","affects":["Wings","Vacuum","Light source","Treasure Chest"],"release":"6 June 2026","unlock":"Trigger a Lavatrix Machina with Para Kooleo.","arcana name":"Edge of the Earth","type":"darkana"}
Gives +3 Thorns. Fixes MoveSpeed[sic] to +100%. Allows to walk through walls. Allows to vacuum Treasure Chests, Light Sources, and Weapons.
Edge of the Earth (VIII) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by triggering a  Lavatrix Machina, the upgraded version of a Vacuum, granted by  Penshin Fatcha after it has evolved once, while playing as  Para Kooleo after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Edge of the Earth (VIII) is active, the player will:
- Gain +3 Thorns, dealing 30 damage to enemies they come in contact with as retaliatory damage.
- Have their  Move Speed stat locked to +100%, regardless of other sources of it.
- Their final speed can still be affected by multipliers such as that from hyper mode and  Stone Skull [DLC].
- Gain the ability to ignore most walls, akin to flight and wall clipping.
- Gain the ability to attract  Treasure Chests, light sources, and weapons and passives on the floor with their  Magnet.
- Chest-opening animation will be skipped when attracting  Treasure Chests.
- Gain the ability to destroy light sources upon contact.
The maximum number of light sources available on the stage will also be doubled while this Darkana is present.
Additionally, unlocking this Darkana will allow the player to toggle the "Quick Treasures" option during a run. This will cause all treasure chests to skip playing an animation, and instead grant their rewards directly to the player in the overworld.
## Tips
- This Darkana is fantastic for characters that are normally incapable or nearly incapable of moving, including  Peppino, who also has an enhanced Magnet radius, and  Keremet, who has extremely high armor and as such is fantastic at using the Thorns provided by Edge of the Earth, especially in combination with a  Divine Bloodline (IX).
- Conversely, it can also be used to reset the speed of characters that have grown too fast to control via Golden Eggs or passive bonuses.
- It's also a great Arcana for characters with abilities that scale off the  Move Speed stat, such as  Babi-Onna and  Jiangshi, allowing them to massively increase this stat in the extreme early game.
- Flight and high speed allows for the easy navigation of stages that can otherwise be difficult to traverse, like Lake Foscari or Ode to Castlevania. This is especially valuable when trying to find relics and secrets. Flight also allows a character to go out of bounds, which can be a way of greatly reducing pressure from enemies.
- This Darkana can sometimes be a double-edged sword by attracting chests before the player is ready to take them (for example, while trying to get a weapon ready for evolution before grabbing an evolution chest).
- This Darkana would allow for the player to pickup the Dommario survarot and the Archon Ascia enemah without worrying of decreased move speed.
- Likewise, the Krochi Freetto survarot will not provide a movement speed buff and only be beneficial through its skills. Furthermore, the Archon Ascia enemah would only decrease  Max Health.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Edge of the Sky", which is the theme song for the Tristis Pass in Castlevania: Order of Ecclesia.
- Edge of the Earth (VIII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "azrofal", the reverse of "laforza" or "strength" in Italian.
- This makes Edge of the Earth (VIII) a representation of the Strength Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing sloth, vulnerability, and a lack of confidence or self-control.
- This is reflected in its ability to benefit characters with lackluster movement speed and high amounts of Magnet, but comes with the downside of recklessly pulling items towards them that would be better used at more opportune moments, such as treasure chests.

Empty Tome is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by holding 6 different weapons at once. 
Empty Tome reduces the Cooldown of weapons by 8% per level.
Empty Tome is required for the evolution of Magic Wand, Holy Wand.
Empty Tome can be found as a stage item in Inlaid Library, Moongolow, Boss Rash, and Abyss Foscari.
## Levels
Empty Tome has 5 levels. At max level, Empty Tome provides -40% Cooldown.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Reduces weapons cooldown by 8%."
| Level 2 | Cooldown reduced by 8.0%
| Level 3 | Cooldown reduced by 8.0%
| Level 4 | Cooldown reduced by 8.0%
| Level 5 | Cooldown reduced by 8.0%
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Magic Wand | + | Empty Tome | = | Holy Wand
| Phas3r | + | Max level:Empty Tome | = | Photonstorm
| Spread Shot | + | Empty Tome+Weapon Power-Up | = | Prototype C
| Shuriken | + | Empty Tome | = | Yagyu Shuriken
| Globus | + | Max level:Empty Tome | = | Nitesco
## Tips
- Empty Tome is an almost universal benefit to all weapons and characters, with the sole exception of Babi-Onna.
## Trivia
- The name of this passive item is a reference to the song "An Empty Tome", the theme of Shanoa in Castlevania: Order of Ecclesia.

# Experience Gem
| Experience Gem
| Increases experience.
| Pickup
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | GEM
| Rarity | 0
| Unlock level | 100,000
| Affected by luck | No
| Effects | Gives 50 XP (as a light source drop).
| Notes | Amount depends on gem type. Amount of XP gained is multiplied by Growth.
| {"notes":"* Amount depends on gem type.\n* Amount of XP gained is multiplied by Growth.","description":"Increases experience.","id":"GEM","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Experience Gem","effects":"Gives 50 XP (as a light source drop).","release":"27 October 2021","level":"100000","luck":"No","rarity":"0"}
Experience Gems are pickups that can be dropped by killed enemies. Collecting enough experience allows the player to level up. The amount of experience gained is affected by the Growth stat.
## Mechanics
Experience Gems are pulled in by the player when they get in the player's Magnet radius.
 Vacuum pickup, and  Gorgeous Moon can be used to collect all Experience Gems from the ground.  Summon Spirit Tornado [DLC]can also collect Experience Gems.
 Luminaire [DLC] can increase the value of the Experience Gems.
Experience Gems have varying value depending on their color. Blue Experience Gems grant up to 2 XP, green Experience Gems up to 9 XP and red gems contain anything beyond. If there are more than 400 Experience Gems on the ground at a time, no more gems can be dropped and any experience gained is instead accumulated into a single red Experience Gem. During the pulsing and chiming phase of Gorgeous Moon, it produces a total of 20 moon beams which erase enemies and generate uniquely colored Experience Gems (orange, yellow, magenta) that yield 1 experience next to their usual gem drop. Luminaire can randomize the color of the gems.
Enemies killed by the  Emerald Wave [DLC] will always drop red gems.
## Level up
Main article: Level up
When the player collects enough experience, they will gain a level up. Each successive level up requires more experience than the previous. Upon level up the game is paused and the player is given 3 or 4 options consisting of weapons and passive item to choose from.
## Notes
- Players can pick up a green Experience Gem, containing 5 experience, near the starting areas in Inlaid Library and Dairy Plant.
- A total of 15 blue Experience Gems can be found surrounding 3  Little Clovers in Lake Foscari.
## Trivia
- This item, along with the  Gold Coin (pickup) and the  Friendship Amulet, are the only items that are unlocked in the Collection menu before the player starts their first round in the game.
- When the player levels up, there is an animation showcasing blue, green, and red gems falling from the top side of the screen.
- There is also a similar animation that appears from the top bar of poncle's website.
=====
# Fever
Fever is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It increases the duration of  Gold Fever and  Gold Finger.
## Effects
Fever multiplies the duration of Gold Fever and Gold Finger, making them last longer and potentially achieve a higher coin count or better rewards respectively.
Note that this multiplier is separate from the Gold Fever cap and duration bonus of  Big Trouser and  Sammy [DLC].
## From Items
Fever's associated passive item is  Karoma's Mana, which provides +10% Fever per level, up to a total of +50% Fever at level 5.
## From Survarot
-  FOIL Skill: Overhealing by +16 provides +1% Fever.
## Trivia
- Fever,  Defang,  Charm,  Preserve, Invulnerability Time, and Thorns are the only character stats in the game that are not displayed on the stat panel.
- Fever, Invulnerability Time, and Thorns are the only stats without a respective PowerUp.
- Fever was accidentally leaked as a stat in all languages besides English for the erroneous description of  Brave Story, displaying the description of the relic that unlocks it as a PowerUp.
=====

The Floor Chicken is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors that has a chance to drop from light sources. It restores 30 Health to the player upon being picked up, and its drop rate depends on the Luck stat.
Collecting five Floor Chickens will unlock the  Garlic.
Collecting 500 Floor Chickens will unlock The Coop stage.
Floor Chicken can also be gained upon level up in the late game. It is offered as a choice when your inventory is full and all items are maxed out.

## Trivia
- Floor Chicken is a direct reference to "Wall Chicken", which is a long-running meme related to Castlevania series.
- This is also very likely a reference to Gauntlet food pickups.
- In older versions, the Floor Chicken used to be called "Pot Roast."

| Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane

| Relic

The Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane are a relic located in The Bone Zone. Once collected, it permanently unlocks the secret menu, which offers hints to find secret characters, and allows the player to cast spells.
## Obtaining
The Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane are obtained from defeating the  Sketamari in The Bone Zone, which appears after the player has obtained the Yellow Sign, at with point it will be marked with a question mark on the  pause menu map. Once the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane have been collected, it can no longer be dropped by the Sketamari, though the Sketamari will still spawn thereafter.
It should also be noted that as long as the player is not within viewing distance or has not gotten close enough to the Sketamari, it will not have absorbed any enemies, and can be faced at a much later level, instead of being taken down right away, making obtaining the scroll much easier and doable with any character.
- For a more extensive guide to unlocking this relic, see Guide:Defeating the Sketamari.
Alternatively, the Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane can have its features accessed by tapping the Yellow Sign in the Collection seven times.
## Secrets
Main article: Secrets
## Spells
Main article: Spells
## Audio
 Temptation of the Occult
Track that plays when the player accesses the Secrets menu.
## Trivia
- The Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane are a reference to Morbane's Book of Monsters & Magic; a collection of stories or tales about various legendary and or mythical monsters and spells, collected on cards, and published in installments in a magazine in early 1992. The magazine was "The Ancestral Trail," a valuable collector's item in its own right, if a player can find or put together a full collected set.

# Friendship Amulet
Friendship Amulet
| Friendship Amulet
| Every player gets a level up for one of their existing weapons.
| Pickup
| Release | 17 August 2023 (Update)
| ID | FRIENDSHIP
| Rarity | 0
| Unlock level | 1,000,000
| Affected by luck | No
| Effects | Gives a level up to all players for a random weapon in their inventory in a co-op mode game.
| Notes | Exclusive to Co-op mode. Is a hidden item which doesn't appear from light sources. May be offered as an option when leveling up to level 30 or above. For this purpose, it has a rarity weighting of 30. Disappears from level-up choices after all players' weapons have been maxed out, even if Limit Break is enabled.
| {"notes":"* Exclusive to Co-op mode.\n* Is a hidden item which doesn't appear from light sources.\n* May be offered as an option when leveling up to level 30 or above. For this purpose, it has a rarity weighting of 30.\n* Disappears from level-up choices after all players' weapons have been maxed out, even if Limit Break is enabled.","description":"Every player gets a level up for one of their existing weapons.","id":"FRIENDSHIP","update":"Version 1.6: New engine & Local co-op","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Friendship Amulet","effects":"Gives a level up to all players for a random weapon in their inventory in a co-op mode game.","release":"17 August 2023","level":"1000000","luck":"No","rarity":"0"}
Friendship Amulet is a special item exclusive to Co-op mode, that gives all players in a co-op stage a level-up for one of their equipped weapons. 
Players in a co-op stage may choose the Friendship Amulet as a random choice after leveling up, starting at level 30, and it may continue to appear in subsequent level-up choices even if one of the players has previously selected it. It disappears from the level-up selection after all players reach the maximum level on all of their weapons, even if Limit Break is enabled in the run.
Friendship Amulet is unlocked by default.
## Trivia
- Although the only way to obtain a Friendship Amulet during a run is through the item selection after leveling up, it falls into the same category as all pickup items in the game's code. Specifically, the class of items that may drop by breaking light sources.
- A similar instance of this is the Big Coin Bag, which is unobtainable as a pickup and can only appear as a choice after a player fills up their inventory and maxes out their items. Like the Friendship Amulet, it is categorized as a pickup item in the code.
- If one player has reached the maximum level on all their weapons but the other players have not, the Friendship Amulet will continue to appear. If chosen in this circumstance, the player with no weapons left to level up will receive a level-up for one of their equipped passives instead.
- This item, along with the  Gold Coin (pickup) and the  Experience Gem, are the only items that are unlocked in the Collection menu before the player starts their first round in the game.

| Pickup

| {"notes":"* Collecting Gold Coins, Coin Bags and Rich Coin Bags increase the duration of Gold Fever.","description":"Gathers all Gold left on the ground and starts a Gold Fever.","id":"GILDED","update":"Patch 0.5.2","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Gilded Clover","effects":"Collects all Gold Pickups on screen and starts a Gold Fever.","release":"8 May 2022","level":"30","luck":"Yes","rarity":"1"}
Gilded Clover is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors that has a chance to drop from light sources.
## Gold Fever
Picking up Gilded Clover gathers all the  gold left on the ground to the character and starts a Gold Fever that lasts for 10 seconds. When an enemy is killed, there is a 75% chance it drops a Gold Coin that has a value of 0.5, which then is multiplied by  Greed and Stage Modifiers. Enemies from Il Molise will divide the value by 5 to 0.1.
Its duration can be extended by collecting  Gold Coins,  Coin Bags, and  Rich Coin Bags by 0.5, 5 and 10 seconds respectively. Coins generated from killing enemies during Gold Fever and  Vicious Hunger extend the duration by 0.01 and 0.05 seconds respectively. The timer is capped at 10 seconds.
 Fever increases Gold Fever duration, which can be obtained with  Karoma's Mana and some  Suravorts.
During an active  Orologion, the duration decay is halved to counteract enemies not moving in, turning them into Starry Heavens with  Celestial Voulge make them actively helpful by extending the duration without slowing down the kill rate.
With  Big Trouser, Gold Fever lasts for 15 seconds and all duration extensions from collecting coins are also multiplied by 1.5; With  Sammy [DLC] and  Master Librarian [DLC], Gold Fever lasts for 12.5 seconds and duration extensions are multiplied by 1.25.
Sammy triggers a Gold Fever at level 30.
 Power of Lire [DLC] triggers a Gold Fever every attack, which Master Librarian have a hidden weapon version.
With  Disco of Gold (XV), collecting both types of Coin Bags also starts a Gold Fever.

Defeat as many enemies as possible to win a prize.","id":"GOLDFINGER","update":"Patch 1.5.0","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Gold Finger","effects":"Grants the player invincibility, Cooldown, and Charm bonuses for 14 seconds before dropping a prize based on defeated enemies.","release":"12 June 2023","level":"30","luck":"No","rarity":"0.02"}
Gold Finger is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors. It starts out exclusive to Astral Stair. After  Trisection is obtained, Gold Fingers become available in all stages. It is also the rarest drop from destroyed light sources.
Picking up 5 Gold Fingers will unlock the Space 54 stage.
## Effects
Picking up Gold Finger grants the character a 14 second period of invulnerability and 10 seconds of set enemies spawned to 500 and -100%  Cooldown, and offers variable rewards after said period ends.
 Karoma's Mana increases Gold Finger duration by 10% per level, up to +50% at max, making a total duration of 21 seconds.
The enemies killed threshold of each tier and its reward being:
- Bronze: 0 + Player Level; Healing (Pet command) + bouncy weapon ( Bone,  Cherry Bomb,  Carréllo,  Celestial Dusting).
- Silver: 500 + Player Level x 2; 32 Gold Coins + healing + Little Clover + random pickup between  Gilded Clover,  Nduja Fritta Tanto, or  Rosary.
- Gold: 1000 + Player Level x 3; 64 Gold Coins +  Floor Chicken +  Treasure Chest (cannot evolve, 3% chance to have 5 items, 33% to have 3)
- Demon: 1500 + Player Level x 4; 32 Gold Coins + [Treasure Chest 2] Treasure Chest (all five slots can evolve, 3% chance to have 5 items, 33% to have 3)
- Cosmic: 2500 + Player Level x 5; 64 Gold Coins + [Treasure Chest 2] Treasure Chest (same as above) + random weapon:
-  Greatest Jubilee,  Torrona's Box, or  Victory Sword; including  Candybox if the player has less than 6 weapons; including anima weapon ( Anima of Mortaccio,  Yatta Daikarin,  Carrozza!,  Profusione D'Amore) if the player has less than 7 weapons and does not already have an anima weapon.
## Notes
- If a Gold Finger is a stage item (like in Astral Stair or in Space 54), it cannot be attracted by the  Mad Groove (VIII).
- However, it can be attracted by the  Lavatrix Machina.

Gold Ring increases  Curse by 5% per level.
Gold Ring, alongside  Silver Ring, is required for the evolution of  Clock Lancet,  Infinite Corridor.
Gold Ring cannot be obtained from level ups, but can be found as a stage item in every stage, except Tiny Bridge and Eudaimonia Machine. Generally, it will spawn south of the starting area. It will always be guarded by a Sun Atlantean. In Bat Country, all nine spawn and they are spinning.
## Levels
Gold Ring has 9 levels. At max level, Gold Ring provides +40% Curse.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | ... With ... Lancet
| Level 2 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 5%.
| Level 3 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 5%.
| Level 4 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 5%.
| Level 5 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 5%.
| Level 6 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 5%.
| Level 7 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 5%.
| Level 8 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 5%.
| Level 9 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 5%.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Clock Lancet | + | Max level:Gold Ring+Silver Ring | = | Infinite Corridor
## Tips
- Gold Ring does not provide any stats at level 1.
## Trivia
- Together with the description of  Silver Ring, it forms "Wear with Clock Lancet", which is a reference to "Wear in Clock Tower", the combined description of the Silver Ring and Gold Ring in the original English translation of the game Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Gold Ring, Silver Ring,  Metaglio Left,  Metaglio Right,  Torrona's Box, and  Outer Saboteur [DLC] are the only passive items with max level 9 instead of the usual 5.
=====
# Golden Egg
This article is about the pickup. For the enemy, see Egge.
| Golden Egg
| Permanently improves a random stat for the current character, by a tiny amount.
| Pickup
| Release | 23 May 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_GOLDENEGG
| Rarity | 0
| Unlock level | 1,000,000
| Affected by luck | No
| Effects | Permanently increases a random stat.
| Notes | Drops from certain enemies including The Reaper after obtaining the Yellow Sign.
| {"notes":"* Drops from certain enemies including The Reaper after obtaining the Yellow Sign.","description":"Permanently improves a random stat for the current character, by a tiny amount.","id":"RELIC_GOLDENEGG","update":"Patch 0.6.1","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Golden Egg","effects":"Permanently increases a random stat.","release":"23 May 2022","level":"1000000","luck":"No","rarity":"0"}
The Golden Egg is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors that permanently increases a random stat of the player's current character by a small amount. 
## Obtaining
Golden Eggs can be purchased repeatedly from the  Merchant or the  Eggman, who appears when the player has at least 5,000 Golden Eggs on their save file and is currently holding at least 1,000,000, for 10,000. The Eggman also has the option to buy max eggs, using all of the player's gold. Additionally, the following special enemies can drop a Golden Egg when killed:
-  Moon Atlantean
-  Sun Atlantean
-  City Atlantean
-  Volcano Atlantean
-  Moongolow Atlanteans
-  The Reaper will drop five Golden Eggs when killed if the player is holding the  Infinite Corridor or  Crimson Shroud.
The amount of Golden Eggs a single character can have is hard capped at 3.402823E+38, which is the 32-bit floating point maximum.
Unlike all other characters,  Queen Sigma is completely unaffected by Golden Eggs. When playing as her, Golden Eggs in the Merchant's shop are replaced with the  Candybox, and Golden Eggs dropped by Atlanteans or The Reaper will be thrown away when picked up.
## Effects
Each Golden Egg will provide a small permanent boost to one of the 19 stats available by the following amount:
- +1  Max Health
- +0.1  Recovery,  Armor,  Amount,  Revival,  Reroll
- +1%  Move Speed,  Might,  Speed,  Duration,  Area,  Luck,  Growth,  Greed,  Curse
- -0.5%  Cooldown
- +0.3  Magnet
- +0.2  Skip,  Banish
Golden Eggs boosts the character's starting stats. It does not, however, increase the character stats while in-game. As a result, any Golden Eggs obtained in-game will take effect for the next game session.
There is a hard cap on certain stats, even if visually the number displayed is higher. The following are stats with hard caps:
- +50  Armor (with the exception of  Toastie)
- +900%  Might
- +400%  Speed
- +400%  Duration
- +900%  Area
- -90%  Cooldown
- +10  Amount
## Removal
The  Healer will appear in Moongolow when a character has at least 5,000 Golden Eggs. The Healer can remove 100 Golden Eggs at once, or, when that same character has at least 25,000 Golden Eggs, all eggs that grant Move Speed.
=====
# Gracia's Mirror
| Gracia's Mirror
| Permanently allows to access a harder version of any stage. Permanently allows to access a harder version of any stage.
| Relic
| Release | 20 October 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_MIRROR
| Effects | Unlocks Inverse Mode.
| Location | Given after entering Eudaimonia Machine and choosing the "Too Easy" option. If the "Too Hard" option had been chosen instead, start and quit a run in endless mode, go back to Eudaimonia Machine and it will be given.
| {"description":"Permanently allows to access a harder version of any stage.","id":"RELIC_MIRROR","update":"Patch 1.0","effects":"Unlocks Inverse Mode.","sprite":"Sprite-Gracia's Mirror.png","name":"Gracia's Mirror","release":"20 October 2022","icon":"Icon-Gracia's Mirror.png","location":"Given after entering Eudaimonia Machine and choosing the \"Too Easy\" option. If the \"Too Hard\" option had been chosen instead, start and quit a run in endless mode, go back to Eudaimonia Machine and it will be given."}
Gracia's Mirror is a relic found in Eudaimonia Machine. Once collected, it permanently unlocks inverse mode in the stage selection menu for all stages.
Inverse mode increases the stage's Gold Bonus by +200% and Luck Bonus by +25%, but causes enemies to start with +200% HP and gain +5% HP and +0.5 Move Speed per minute. If the "Visual Invert Stages" option in the Gameplay section of the settings is enabled, it also causes the stage's layout to be flipped upsided down. While inverse mode is enabled, the  Merchant will also sell additional  Skips,  Banishes,  Rerolls, and an additional Arcana card.
## Obtaining
Upon entering Eudaimonia Machine for the first time, approach  The Directer. It will then ask the player a question, and if they pick "Too Easy", it will give them the Gracia's Mirror.
If the player picks the other option and obtains the  Seventh Trumpet, the player will need to start and end a run with endless mode enabled, and then reeneter Eudaimonia Machine and interact with The Directer a second time to unlock the Gracia's Mirror.
Alternatively, the Gracia's Mirror can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "freezearrow".
=====
# Great Gospel
| Great Gospel
| Permanently allows to level up weapons beyond their limit. Can be enabled in stage selection. Permanently allows to level up weapons beyond their limit. Can be enabled in stage selection.
| Relic
| Release | 7 July 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_GGOSPEL
| Effects | Unlocks Limit Break.
| Location | Dropped by the final enemy in Cappella Magna (requires the Yellow Sign).
| {"description":"Permanently allows to level up weapons beyond their limit. Can be enabled in stage selection.","id":"RELIC_GGOSPEL","update":"Patch 0.8.0","effects":"Unlocks Limit Break.","sprite":"Sprite-Great Gospel.png","name":"Great Gospel","release":"7 July 2022","icon":"Icon-Great Gospel.png","location":"Dropped by the final enemy in Cappella Magna (requires the Yellow Sign)."}
The Great Gospel is a relic located in Cappella Magna. Once collected, it permanently unlocks Limit Break in the stage selection menu, which allows weapons to be leveled up beyond their usual cap, granting them additional stats per level.
## Obtaining
The Great Gospel is obtained by defeating  The Ender in Cappella Magna, who is encountered after surviving past the 30:00 mark in the stage for the first time after obtaining the Yellow Sign. Once the Great Gospel has been collected, White Hand will arrive to kill the player and The Ender will no longer appear.
Alternatively, the Great Gospel can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "icanhearthecriesofcaptainplanet".
## Audio
 Unlock Jingle
A version of the Character Reveal jingle that plays when obtaining Great Gospel.
## Trivia
- The Great Gospel is named after Great Gospel, Aerith Gainsborough's final Limit Break in Final Fantasy VII.
- The spell to unlock the Great Gospel is a reference to "You Can Hear the Cry of the Planet", the song which plays in the Forgotten Capital in Final Fantasy VII. The code has the "the" replaced with "captain", referencing the animated television series Captain Planet.

# Greed
Greed
Greed is one of the stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects the amount of  Gold Coins that are gained from pickups and  Treasure Chests.
The player has 100% base Greed before modifications. The Greed in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base Greed, meaning that Greed stat of +30% equals to 130% total Greed, for example. Greed modifications from character bonuses, PowerUps and items stack additively. Stage bonuses to gold gained do not count as Greed and stack multiplicatively with the collective bonus from the aforementioned.
There is no upper limit to how much Greed the player may have.
Greed also affects Base Damage of  Night Sword [DLC],  Muramasa [DLC] and  Victorian Horror (XVIII).
 Colonel Bahamut [DLC] gains +1%  Might per +1% Greed.
## PowerUp
| Greed
| Gains 10% more Gold per rank (max +50%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 2 January 2022 (Update)
| ID | GREED
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +10%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +50%
| Initial cost | 200
| {"description":"Gains 10% more Gold per rank (max +50%).","cost":"200","id":"GREED","update":"Patch 0.2.5: new stage and achievements","image":"","name":"Greed","release":"2 January 2022","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+10%"}
The Greed PowerUp increases Greed by 10% per rank, with a maximum rank of 5. At rank 5, it provides +50% Greed. It initially costs 200.
| Rank | Greed
| 1 | +10%
| 2 | +20%
| 3 | +30%
| 4 | +40%
| 5 | +50%
## From items
Greed's associated passive item is the  Stone Mask, which provides +10% Greed per level, up to a maximum of +50% Greed at level 5.
 Pearl magatama [DLC] will provide -7% Greed per level, up to a maximum of -35% Greed at level 6.
 Velvet Dodecahedron [DLC]will provide +20% Greed per even level, up to a maximum of +60% Greed at level 6.
 Golden Egg permanently increases Greed by 1% for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
 Wicked Season (XIII) doubles Greed in certain 10 second intervals, and gives +0.5% Greed every level.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Ambrojoe starts with +20% Greed.
-  Gallo and  Divano reduce Greed by -50%.
-  Leda and  Marrabbio reduce Greed by -80%.
-  Trouser starts with +20% Greed and gains +1% Greed every level, with no cap.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Megalo Syuuto starts with +50% Greed.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Genevieve and  Je-Ne-Viv start with +50% Greed.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% Greed per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% Greed per level.
## Operation Guns
-  Lucia gains +1% Greed after reviving, with no cap.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Soma starts with +50% Greed.
-  Dracula,  Elizabeth,  Stella & Loretta,  Loretta & Stella,  Stella,  Loretta,  Brauner,  Graham,  Walter,  Carmilla,  Olrox,  Succubus,  Galamoth,  Megalo Elizabeth,  Megalo Olrox,  Megalo Death and  Megalo Dracula start with +100% Greed.
-  Master Librarian starts with +1000% Greed.
-  Vincent starts with +20% Greed and gains Greed when overhealing, with diminishing returns. His Greed is also doubled while invulnerable.
-  Joachim starts with +100% Greed and can select to gain +1% Greed per level.
-  Blackmore starts with +100% Greed and can select to gain +1% Greed per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% Greed per level.
## From stages
## Base Game
- Dairy Plant multiply gold gain by 1.2.
- Gallo Tower, Bat Country, and Tiny Bridge multiply gold gain by 1.3.
- Cappella Magna multiply gold gain by 1.4.
- The Bone Zone multiply gold gain by 1.5.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
- Mt.Moonspell multiply gold gain by 1.5.
The hyper modes of all stages, except Holy Forbidden and Eudaimonia Machine, give a +50% gold gain bonus additively.
The inverse modes of all stages, except Holy Forbidden and Eudaimonia Machine, give a +200% gold gain bonus additively.
## Tips
- Greed is not vital for winning a run, but rather getting the most money out of it. If you find yourself unable to finish a run, it is advised to invest into other passives and PowerUps over taking the Stone Mask or ranking up the Greed PowerUp.
- Bonuses to Greed apply additively. For example, 50% Greed makes  Rich Coin Bags give 150 coins instead of 100. Stage bonuses, however, do not count as Greed, but are an additional multiplier. The +50% Gold bonus from Mad Forest's hyper mode would increase the 150 coins from previous example to 225.
- For the highest gold multiplier, you need the level 5 Stone Mask (+50%) and the Greed PowerUps (+50%) for a total of +100% Gold earning, and play The Bone Zone in hyper mode multiplying overall gold gain by 2x to total of 4x the original amount.

Green Eyes Vizard is found by purchasing it with 60,000 from  Big Troubler in The Lycaeum. Once Green Eyes Vizard has been purchased, it cannot be purchased again.

Grenade can only appear when playing on Neo Galuga or Hectic Highway, or as a character from the Operation Guns DLC.  Simondo Belmont is also able to spawn this pickup without the need for light sources.

# Grim Grimoire
Grim Grimoire
"Grimoire" redirects here. For the real-world book, see Vampire Survivors: The Grimoire.
| Grim Grimoire
| Permanently allows to peek at discovered weapon evolutions and unions from the pause menu. Permanently allows to peek at discovered weapon evolutions and unions from the pause menu.
| Relic
| Release | 23 June 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_GRIMOIRE
| Effects | Unlocks the "Open Grimoire" menu in the pause menu.
| Location | Due west of Inlaid Library.
| {"description":"Permanently allows to peek at discovered weapon evolutions and unions from the pause menu.","id":"RELIC_GRIMOIRE","update":"Patch 0.7.3","effects":"Unlocks the \"Open Grimoire\" menu in the pause menu.","sprite":"Sprite-Grim Grimoire.png","name":"Grim Grimoire","release":"23 June 2022","icon":"Icon-Grim Grimoire.png","location":"Due west of Inlaid Library."}
Grim Grimoire is a relic located in Inlaid Library. Once collected, it permanently unlocks Grimoire in the pause menu, where the player can view all the evolutions and unions that have been discovered on their save file, as well as what weapons and passive items are needed to obtain them.
## Obtaining
Grim Grimoire is located to the far west of the starting area in Inlaid Library. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. It is protected by 2 patrolling  Undead Witches that slow the player down using a beam attack while firing bullets at them. Once Grim Grimoire has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, Grim Grimoire can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "thisshouldhavebeenunlockedbydefault".
## Accessing
If the player has obtained this relic without having obtained the  Milky Way Map beforehand, by default, Grim Grimoire will become immediately accessible upon pausing the game, with options becoming accessible in a different way, where the player would have to pause the game and then click the "Options" button, instead of only clicking the pause button.
If the player has both Grim Grimoire and the Milky Way Map, the player would have to access Grim Grimoire by pressing the pause button and then the "Open Grimoire" button, just to the left of the "RESUME" button.
## Interface
Grim Grimoire in use.
Grim Grimoire shows evolutions that have been obtained before, and hides any evolutions that have yet to be encountered. However, it does show whether a set of weapons and passives result in an evolution, even if the weapons or passives have yet to be discovered. An exception is made for evolutions that require the Yellow Sign, which are hidden until said relic is obtained.
Once a player obtains a weapon and/or a passive item, its sprite in Grim Grimoire will be highlighted with a yellow-greenish square. If the player has evolved a weapon (for example, evolved the  Garlic and the  Pummarola to get the  Soul Eater), the equation indicating the evolution in Grim Grimoire will be entirely highlighted. This is also the case where the player has selected a character that starts with an evolved weapon, such as  Peppino, who starts with the  Soul Eater, even if they have not obtained the  Garlic or the  Pummarola beforehand.
If the player has obtained the maximum number of weapons and/or passives, the evolution equations that would remain completely visible in Grim Grimoire are the ones where an evolution has already occurred, the ones where all of the items needed for the evolution are equipped, or the ones where the player has at least one of the required items needed for the evolution while having the other required item(s) obtainable through leveling up and not through a weapon selector, such as the  Candybox or the  Arma Dio. The rest of the evolution equations, where an evolution is no longer possible by obtaining the remaining required item(s) by leveling up, will look more faded.
The list of evolutions is separated by pagination, depending on how many evolutions the player has discovered.
## Trivia
- The name “Grim Grimoire” may be a reference to GrimGrimoire, a game by Vanillaware.
- The Grim Grimoire does not highlight evolutions and unions available to secondary characters.
=====
# Growth
Growth
Growth is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects how much experience  Experience Gems are worth.
The player has 100% base Growth before modifications. The Growth in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base Growth, meaning that Growth stat of +30% equals to 130% total Growth, for example. Growth modifications from character bonuses, PowerUps and items stack additively.
At level 20 and 40, all characters gain +100% Growth to offset a formula change that makes leveling up harder by making the increased experience requirement for each level higher, which applied retroactively and makes it especially hard at the first level it changes. This bonus is reverted at level 21 and 41, applying a -100% Growth penalty.
There is no upper limit to how much Growth the player may have.
Growth also affects: 
- Base Damage of  Wind Whip [DLC]
- Base Damage, Speed, Area and Duration of  Vol Luminatio [DLC]
- Charge ability of  Charlotte Aulin [DLC]
## PowerUp
| Growth
| Gains 3% more experience per rank (max 15%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | GROWTH
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +3%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +15%
| Initial cost | 900
| {"description":"Gains 3% more experience per rank (max 15%).","cost":"900","id":"GROWTH","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"","name":"Growth","release":"27 October 2021","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+3%"}
The Growth PowerUp increases Growth by 3% per rank, with a maximum rank of 5. At rank 5, it provides +15% Growth. It initially costs 900.
| Rank | Growth
| 1 | +3%
| 2 | +6%
| 3 | +9%
| 4 | +12%
| 5 | +15%
## From items
Growth's associated passive item is  Crown, which provides +8% Growth per level, up to a maximum of +40% Growth at level 5.
 Academy Badge [DLC] has 6 levels and provides -3% Growth every level after 1, for a maximum of -15% Growth.
 Golden Egg permanently increases Growth by 1% for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
 Game Killer (0) multiplies experience gain by 0, effectively halting level ups. Growth is not directly affected and can be used for other calculations like the experience gem damage dealt with this Arcana.
 Wicked Season (XIII) doubles Growth in certain 10 second intervals, and gives +0.5% Growth every level.
## From darkana
 Crystal Cries (XII) generates  Crystallized Souls when killing frozen enemies that can be collected to slightly increase  Max Health,  Recovery and Growth.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Imelda gains +10% Growth every 5 levels, up to +30% at level 15.
-  Gallo gains +10% Growth every 5 levels, up to +50% at level 25.
-  Gains gains +2% Growth every level, with no cap.
-  Sigma gains +1% Growth every level, with no cap.
-  Rose starts with -80% Growth. She gains +10% Growth every 12 levels from level 36 to 84 and +15% Growth on levels 96 and 108.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  McCoy-Oni starts with +20% Growth.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Eleanor starts with 25% Growth.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate has a chance to gain a random amount of Growth every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% Growth per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% Growth per level.
## Operation Guns
-  Ariana starts with +10% Growth.
-  Lucia gains +5% Growth after reviving, with no cap.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Blackmore can select to gain +1% Growth per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% Growth per level.
-  Chaos starts with -99% Growth.
-  Fleaman starts with  Game Killer (0), making him having effectively no Growth.
-  Hector starts with +20% Growth.
-  Joachim can select to gain +1% Growth per level.
-  Leon gains +10% Growth upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
-  Lisa gains +1% Growth every level, with no cap.
## From stages
The hurry mode of all stages, except Holy Forbidden and Eudaimonia Machine, give a +25% experience gain.
- Bat Country has a -75% experience gain penalty.
## Tips
- Bonuses to Growth apply additively. The highest possible Growth in the game can be achieved by playing as Gains, with 5 levels of the Growth PowerUp (+15%) and 5 levels of Crown (+40%) for +(2 x player level + 55)%.
- If you find yourself being underleveled in games, it can be wise to try taking bonuses to  Magnet as well as bonuses to Growth. While Growth can provide powerful boosts to experience gains, collecting all of the experience gems dropped faster can help the player leveling up earlier to be more powerful.
- At level 20 and 40, players can take advantage of  Experience Gems coalescing into a red one with the value of all the uncollected gems, getting to the highest Growth with Crown, PowerUp, Wicked Season (XIII), and collect it at those levels. It will give much more experience than usual, making level ups a breeze.
=====

| {"notes":"","description":"Receive one of the listed items when levelling up.","id":"D10_FROM_THE_FUTURE","update":"Patch 1.11: Bug fixes and improvements","icon":"","image":"","name":"Hail from the Future (X)","affects":["Candybox","Arma Dio","Rosary","Orologion","Vacuum","Gilded Clover","Gold Finger","Nduja Fritta Tanto","Sorbetto","Rerollo","Coin Bag","Rich Coin Bag"],"release":"15 August 2024","unlock":"Trigger a Weird Souls Purifier with Santa Ladonna.","arcana name":"Hail from the Future","type":"Darkana"}
Receive one of the listed items when levelling up.
Hail from the Future (X) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by triggering a  Weird Souls Purifier, the upgraded version of a  Rosary granted by  Seraphic Cry, while playing as  Santa Ladonna after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Hail from the Future (X) is active, every time the player receives a level up, a red twenty-sided die falls from the sky in a semicircle below the player, dropping one of the following items. A single item will also fall when Hail from the Future (X) is first picked up. 
-  Candybox
-  Arma Dio
-  Rosary
-  Orologion
-  Vacuum
-  Gilded Clover
-  Gold Finger
- Cannot drop until the player collects the  Trisection.
-  Nduja Fritta Tanto
-  Rerollo
-  Sorbetto
-  Coin Bag and  Rich Coin Bag
The weights for each pickup dropping are the same as they are in light sources. Any multiplier to such weights will also apply to Hail from the Future (X), including its own multiplier for  Gold Fingers, multiplying their weight by 10.
A maximum of seven Candyboxes and Arma Dios can be generated by Hail from the Future (X) in a single run. The base chances of a Candybox and Arma Dio are 2.5% each. When either item is summoned, this chance is divided by the total number of that item spawned. These chances are affected by  Luck.
- For example, if 3 Candyboxes have been summoned, then the 4th will have a base chance of ~0.625% to be granted.
Up to 5 dice, or drops, can be summoned per level. A random value is multiplied by the higher value between the player's level divided by 40, and  Amount. Then, the lower value between this final value and 5 is chosen for the number of drops. Thus, as the player levels up and accumulates Amount, they will be more likely to receive extra drops.
- Possessing  Beginning (X) will add 3 to this Amount.
## Tips
- Hail from the Future (X) synergizes extremely well with  Crystal Cries (XII), as Hail from the Future (X) not only drops  Orologions and  Sorbettos, but also drops powerful pickups that helps the player kill frozen enemies faster. Killing frozen enemies drops  Crystallized Souls, which increase  Growth (as well as  Max Health and  Recovery), enabling faster leveling which leads to more Orologions/Sorbettos and other pickups.
- It also synergizes with  Hidden Anathema (III), because the  Nduja Fritta Tantos and  Sorbettos are dropped fairly frequently, causing additional stat boosts that then increase the rate of level ups and more subsequent drops.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Hail from the Past", which is the theme song for the Sandy Grave in Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin.
- Hail from the Future (X) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "atoural", the reverse of "laruota" or "the wheel" in Italian.
- This makes Hail from the Future (X) a representation of the Wheel of Fortune Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing bad luck, a lack of control, and unwelcome changes.
- The Chinese character on this Darkana is "winter" (冬). This is likely a joke based on the usage of the word "hail" in its name, which also refers to a type of winter weather.
- The art depicts a geometric shape resembling a 20-sided icosahedron. This is a common shape for dice, likely in reference to the Darkana giving the player a random item.
- As Hail from the Future (X) is unlocked by playing as Santa Ladonna, the effect of being given an item is likely in reference to Santa Claus giving children gifts during Christmas.

The Heart Refresh is an item pickup from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. The Heart Refresh increases Recovery by +5.0 for a duration of 10 seconds, which stacks with other picked-up Heart Refreshes.
Heart Refreshes are permanently unlocked upon collecting the  Stallion Gate, which is dropped by defeating the Giant Bat in the Ode to Castlevania stage. Once unlocked, they only appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage or while playing as a character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in any other stage.
Additionally, picking up a total of 7 Heart Refreshes or more unlocks  Sonia Belmont as a playable character.

Defeating enemies with FIRE gives bonus MaxHealth[sic] and Might. Triggers an[sic] Nduja Fritta when health reaches a critically low level.
Heir of Fate (XIX) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by surviving 15 minutes in Boss Rash with Inverse Mode, Random Events, and Random Level Ups enabled after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Heir of Fate (XIX) is active, defeating enemies with  Fire damage causes them to drop  Scorched Souls, pickups which increase  Max Health and  Might. While individually these bonuses are miniscule, the Scorched Souls gathered can quickly increase a player's stats. There is also a 200-250 pickup limit for Scorched Souls that can be on the stage at any point. Whenever the player collected a Scorched Soul, a fiery explosion will occur around them, damaging enemies in its radius.
- The stats provided by  Scorched Souls are based on the base  Experience value of the enemy that dropped it. The stat bonuses provided by a Scorched Soul are:
-  Max Health = 0.1 * Base XP Value
-  Might = 0.01% * Base XP Value
- For example, a  Skelegem, with a Base XP of 3, will drop a Scorched Soul that gives +0.3 Max Health and +0.03% Might.
Additionally, Heir of Fate will trigger an HP-Critical  Nduja Fritta Tanto when the character's Health hits 20%. This ability recovers once the character is healed back to full Max Health.
## Tips
- Weapons that can deal Fire damage will be marked with an indicator on the right side of their selection icon when the character levels up.
-  Heart of Fire (XIX) works great with this arcana, as the explosions it creates count as  Fire damage. However, some weapons affected by Heart of Fire do not count as Fire for the purposes of Heir of Fate and vice-versa, for example, Heir of Fate does not count  Vanitas Whip as a fire weapon even though it is affected by Heart of Fire, meanwhile, Heart of Fire does not improve  Santa Water despite it counting for Heir of Fate. This can render weapon selection complicated when trying to build around both of these Arcana.
- It is a great Arcana for  Dario Bossi as it works with most of the weapons he wants to be using.
- This darkana and  Crystal Cries (XII) synergize quite well, as defeating a frozen enemy with a Fire based weapon will drop both a Scorched Soul and a  Crystallized Soul. This grants the player double the health increase, with a bonus to  Growth and  Recovery on top of it.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Successor of Fate", which is the theme song for Juste Belmont in Castlevania: Harmony of Dissonance.
- Heir of Fate (XIX) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "elosli", the reverse of "ilsole" or "the sun" in Italian.
- This makes Heir of Fate (XIX) a representation of The Sun Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing inflated or misguided optimism, pride, pessimism, and conceitedness.
- This is reflected in its ability to boost the power of the player by burning enemies, using them as fuel to prop them up.
