| {"notes":"","description":"Food pickups grant cumulative bonuses to random stats based on their rarity or healing value.","id":"D03_BREAD_ANATHEMA","update":"Vampire Survivors: Emerald Diorama is free and out now","icon":"","image":"","name":"Hidden Anathema (III)","affects":["Meal Ticket","Recovery","Floor Chicken","Nduja Fritta Tanto","Sorbetto","Wall Chicken"],"release":"10 April 2025","unlock":"Eat as much as possible with Gazebo.","arcana name":"Hidden Anathema","type":"Darkana"}
Food pickups grant cumulative bonuses to random stats based on their rarity or healing value.
Hidden Anathema (III) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching the stat bonus cap from consuming food pickups while playing as  Gazebo after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Hidden Anathema (III) is active, food pickups such as  Floor Chicken,  Nduja Fritta Tantos,  Sorbettos,  Wall Chicken [DLC], as well as food created by the  Meal Ticket [DLC] will add random bonus stats to the player based upon the rarity of the pickup for the rest of the current run.
Floor Chicken provide 1 stat bonus at base. Nduja Fritta Tanto and Sorbetto provides 2 stat bonuses. Wall Chicken also provides 2 stat bonuses at base. Floor Chicken and Wall Chicken can provide up to 5 stats when their healing is boosted by  Recovery or  Sarabande of Healing (VI). Food from the Meal Ticket has a 20% chance to provide a single stat increase, multiplied by  Luck. This Darkana works functionally like Gazebo's inherent passive but without a hard cap.
- The number of stats granted by Floor and Wall Chicken is equal to the healing provided, divided by 30, then rounded down. At the standard +0.50 Recovery, and without Sarabande of Healing (VI), Floor Chickens will provide 1 stat bonus while Wall Chickens will provide 3.
Different food pickups provide a different amount of stat bonuses. The first and second time the player gains a specific stat, the value is higher than subsequent times for that specific stat.
| Stats | Initial Bonus | Second Bonus | Subsequent Bonus
| Max Health | +50 | +25 | +1
| Recovery | +0.5 | +0.1 | +0.01
| Armor | +1 | +0.5 | +0.05
| Move Speed | +20% | +10% | +1%
| Might | +20% | +10% | +1%
| Speed | +20% | +10% | +1%
| Duration | +20% | +10% | +1%
| Area | +20% | +10% | +1%
| Cooldown | -5% | -2% | -1%
| Amount | +1 | +0.25 | +0.05
| Revival | +1 | +0.5 | +0.05
| Magnet | +0.5 | +0.5 | +0.5
| Luck | +20% | +10% | +1%
| Growth | +20% | +10% | +1%
| Greed | +20% | +10% | +1%
| Curse | +20% | +10% | +1%
## Tips
- The quickest way to unlock Hidden Anathema (III) is to take  Gazebo into The Coop or Lake Foscari stages.
- Fire weapons such as the  Fire Wand,  Flames of Misspell, and  Firearm [DLC], along with the explosions from  Heart of Fire (XIX), have a chance to instantly kill or "roast"  Chickenfantry into  Floor Chicken.
- The player can also use  Paranormal Scan [DLC] to eventually overheal enough times to reach Gazebo's maximum bonus, while also making it easier for other sources to overheal thanks to the  Recovery bonus this weapon provides.
- Another, slower, option is the stage Bat country, as the diamonds that appear can spawn chickens.
-  Greatest Jubilee can generate light sources for more food drops.
-  Meal Ticket [DLC] also synergizes with Hidden Anathema as each food item will grant 1 stat bonus.
-  Luck increases the chance of getting pickups (such as food) other than Gold Coins or Coin Bags when destroying light sources.
- Sarabande of Healing (VI) will increase the amount of stats provided by Floor Chicken and Wall Chicken, as it increases healing output.
- Certain stages have unique circumstances or enemies that drop food:
-  Sammies [DLC] drop  Birthday Cakes when killed.
- Inputting the spell "popthecorn" causes the player to start a run in Green Acres with special  Corn pickups.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "The Hidden Curse", which is the theme song for the Forest of Doom in Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin.
- Hidden Anathema (III) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ecirtarepmil", the reverse of "limperatrice" or "the empress" in Italian.
- This makes Hidden Anathema (III) a representation of The Empress Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing neglect, overreliance on others, and a lack of willpower or progress.
- This is reflected in its ability to gain stats from pickups that can only be found from light sources and enemies outside of the player's control.
- Hidden Anathema (III) depicts a cornucopia, a hollow, horn-shaped wicker basket filled with fruits and vegetables that is commonly a symbol of abundance, fertility, and bountiful harvests.
- The shape the displayed stat bonuses take as more are obtained is the spiral found in relation to the golden ratio. Since this value is irrational, and the game uses a truncated version for the placement of each stat bonus above the character, it is not a perfect replica of this spiral.

| {"description":"Augments Max Health by 20%.","max-level":"5","id":"MAXHEALTH","stage-item":"Mad Forest, Moongolow, Boss Rash","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","stacking":"Multiplicative","sprite":"Sprite-Hollow Heart.png","max-effect":"+149%","name":"Hollow Heart","stat":" Max Health","release":"27 October 2021","rarity":"90","icon":"Icon-Hollow Heart.png","per-level":"+20%"}
Hollow Heart is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by surviving 1 minute as any character.
It multiplies  Max Health by 1.2 per level and can be found as a stage item in Mad Forest, Moongolow, and Boss Rash.
## Levels
Hollow Heart has 5 levels. Each of them multiplies the current value of  Max Health by 1.2.
| Level | Description | Effect total
| 1 | "Augment max health by 20%." | 1.2x Max Health
| 2 | Max health increases by 20% | 1.44x Max Health
| 3 | Max health increases by 20% | 1.728x Max Health
| 4 | Max health increases by 20% | 2.0736x Max Health
| 5 | Max health increases by 20% | 2.48832x Max Health
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Whip | + | Hollow Heart | = | Bloody Tear
| Pako Battiliar | + | Max level:Hollow Heart | = | Mazo Familiar
| Metal Claw | + | Hollow Heart+Weapon Power-Up | = | Big Fuzzy Fist
| Vanitas Whip | + | Hollow Heart | = | Aurablaster Tip
| Mace | + | Max level:Hollow Heart | = | Stamazza
## Tips
- Hollow Heart's health bonus stacks multiplicatively. This means that each level will increase Max Health by 20%; however, it multiplies the current total, not the original total. Characters and builds that gain a big amount of Max Health later (like  Toastie at level 100) will lose out on the benefits if it is applied early.
- Hollow Heart does not heal to fill up the newly increased Max Health.
## Trivia
- The Hollow Heart is inspired by Heart Containers from the Legend of Zelda series, or simply video games at large, increasing the cap of max health.

Listed weapon projectiles gain up to 3 bounces and might pass through enemies and walls.
Iron Blue Will (VII) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Gennaro Belpaese.
 John Morris [DLC] acquires this Arcana automatically at level 10.
## Overview
When this Arcana is active, the following weapons' projectiles gain up to three bounces (triggers by hitting an enemy, mostly), and the ability to pass through enemies and ignore walls:
-  Knife,  Thousand Edge and  Million Cut [DLC]
-  Axe,  Death Spiral (Death Spiral +1 bounces off from screen) and  Legacy of Death: Soul River [DLC]
-  Phiera Der Tuphello and  Eight The Sparrow
-  Carréllo (+3 bounces off from screen)
- The arrow projectile of  Flash Arrow [DLC] and  Millionaire [DLC] (+3 bounces off from screen)
- Drone projectiles of  Prototype A [DLC],  Prototype B [DLC], and  Prototype C [DLC](+1 bounce)
-  Javelin [DLC] and  Long Inus [DLC]
-  Iron Ball [DLC] and  Wrecking Ball [DLC]
-  Curved Knife [DLC] and  Bwaka Knife [DLC]
-  Shuriken [DLC] and  Yagyu Shuriken [DLC]
Instead of bouncing on enemies 3 times before using up their own pierce like other weapons, Death Spiral bounces off the edges of the screen once; Flash Arrow and Millionaire 3 times; and Carréllo 3 more times. They already have nearly infinite Pierce and benefits more from staying on screen longer. 
The drones' projectiles of Prototype A, B, and C gains +1 bounce instead of 3, while their main weapon will not benefit.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Iron Blue Intention", which is the theme song of The Munitions Factory in Castlevania: Bloodlines.
- Iron Blue Will (VII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ilcarro", which means "the chariot" in Italian.
- This makes Iron Blue Will (VII) a representation of The Chariot Major Arcana card, symbolizing willpower, determination, confidence, conviction, physical dexterity, and overcoming challenges.
- This is reflected in its ability to give physical projectile weapons the power to pierce through obstacles and last for longer periods of time.
- Iron Blue Will (VII) is very similar to  Waltz of Pearls (XI) with how both Arcanas give weapons extra bounces, but the two Arcanas differ mainly by applying to different weapon sets.
-  Carréllo is affected by both Arcanas.
=====

Listed weapon projectiles have a chance to freeze enemies.
Jail of Crystal (XIV) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Pasqualina Belpaese.
 Sypha Belnades [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Jail of Crystal (XIV) is active, the following weapons gain a chance (or increased chance) to freeze enemies with their projectiles:
-  Magic Wand,  Holy Wand and  Spirit of Light [DLC]
-  Runetracer,  NO FUTURE and  Carnage Heart [DLC]
-  Eight The Sparrow and  Twice Upon a Time
-  Cygnus
-  Bracelet,  Bi-Bracelet,  Tri-Bracelet and  Lapiste Tepisto [DLC]
-  Penshin Fatcha, all of its morphs, and  Miracle of Multiplication.
-  Prismatic Missile [DLC] and  Luminaire [DLC]
-  C-U-Laser [DLC] and  Pronto Beam [DLC]
-  Prism Lass and  Prism Damsel [DLC](Increases freeze chance from 5% to 25%)
-  Dragon Water Whip [DLC] and  Hydrostormer Tip [DLC] (10% freeze chance)
-  Ice Fang [DLC],  Speculo Ice Fang [DLC], and  Cocytus [DLC]
-  Icebrand [DLC] and  Ninth Circle [DLC] (Increases freeze chance from 5% to 35%)
-  Fleuret [DLC],  Dress Sword [DLC], and  Espada Ropera [DLC]
-  Hydra Cannon [DLC] and  Hyperion Bazooka [DLC]
-  Spirit Rings [DLC] and  Rings of Calamity [DLC]
All these weapons have a 25% chance to freeze enemies, multiplied by  Luck. The freeze duration is based on the weapon's individual  Duration.
 Prism Lass [DLC],  Prism Damsel [DLC], and  Icebrand [DLC] are already able to freeze enemies and instead gain a greatly increased chance to freeze enemies.
## Tips
- With  Out of Bounds (XII), inflicting freeze onto an enemy will also create an explosion dealing damage to them and other nearby enemies.
- With  Crystal Cries (XII), defeating frozen enemies will drop  Crystallized Souls that grant  Growth,  Recovery, and  Max Health when collected.
- The freeze from the listed weapons cannot freeze  The Reaper.
- With +300%  Luck, the listed weapons are guaranteed to freeze enemies. This is possible by playing as  Exdash Exiviiq,  Toastie, or  Smith IV, the  Clover, and a few  Little Clovers.  Keitha Muort [DLC] and  Cosmo Pavone can reach +300% Luck through leveling.  Wicked Season (XIII) can also be used to reach +300% Luck.
- Pairing up with  Garlic or  Soul Eater can make some enemies that are normally immune susceptible to freeze.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Jail of Jewel", which is the theme song of the Great Stairway in Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin.
- Jail of Crystal (XIV) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "latemperanza", which means "temperance" in Italian.
- This makes Jail of Crystal (XIV) a representation of the Temperance Major Arcana card, symbolizing patience, moderation, frugality, balance, tranquility, humility, and higher learning.
- This is reflected in its ability to provide water, ice, and many other blue-colored weapons the power to freeze enemies.
- There was a bug where Jail of Crystal (XIV) does not affect the  Dragon Water Whip [DLC] or the  Hydrostormer Tip [DLC]. It has since been fixed.
- Despite being closely related to the freeze mechanic and the evolved form even has an innate chance to freeze enemies, both  Glass Fandango and  Celestial Voulge does not benefit from it.

The Joker Disk is a relic located in Moongolow. Once collected, it allows entrance to the Ante Chamber stage.
## Obtaining
The Joker Disk can be purchased for 50,000 from  Xanthia (the merchant who usually appears in Planar Bazaar), who can be found on the left side of Moongolow.
Alternatively, the Joker Disk can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "differenttypeofstake".

# Karma Coin
DLC Content (Ode to Castlevania)
This article primarily features content only available in DLC, which may not be available in all copies of the game.
| Karma Coin [DLC]
| Has a 50% chance to trigger a Soul Steal, which destroys all enemies in line of sight and turns them into hearts.
| Pickup
| Release | 31 October 2024 (Update)
| ID | TP_KARMA_COIN
| Rarity | 2
| Unlock level | 12

The Karma Coin is an item pickup from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. 
Upon being picked up, the Karma Coin causes the character to create a speech bubble that displays "heads" or "tails". If the character says "tails", then nothing happens, but if they say "heads", following a short delay, all enemies on the screen are killed instantly and turned into  Little Hearts , an effect similar to that of the  Soul Steal. The Karma Coin's success rate is 50%, which can be increased by the Luck stat.
Karma Coins are permanently unlocked upon collecting the  Scorpion Gate, which is dropped by defeating the  Puppet Master in the Ode to Castlevania stage. Once unlocked, they only appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage or while playing as a character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in any other stage.
Additionally, picking up a total of 7 Karma Coins or more unlocks  Hector as a playable character.

Karoma's Mana is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by collecting it in any stage.
Karoma's Mana increases  Charm by 10 and  Fever by 10% per level.
Karoma's Mana can be found as a stage item on Westwoods, Ode to Castlevania, and Emerald Diorama.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Silver Revolver | + | Karoma's Mana | = | Jewel Gun
| Optical Shot | + | Max level:Karoma's Mana | = | Acerbatus

## Trivia
- Its name is a reference to Karona, a godlike being from the card game Magic: The Gathering, who acted as the manifestation of all five colors of magic on the plane of Dominaria. The color of the gems is the same as each type of mana (White, Blue, Black, Red, Green). Its codename is DOMINION, a reference to the plane's name.
- It was made available for the base game alongside the release of the Ode to Castlevania DLC, and later made fully unlockable by finding it in Westwoods.
- While the unlocks menu states that it must be found in Westwoods, it simply needs to be obtained once to unlock, much like  Arma Dio.

# Lavatrix Machina
| Lavatrix Machina
| 
| Pickup
| Release | ? (edit)
| ID | ? (edit)
| Rarity | 2
| Unlock level | 12
| Affected by luck | Yes
| Effects | All pickups, light sources, and stage items are pulled to the player. Spins enemies around the screen.
| Notes | It takes time for all pickups to reach the player.
| {"notes":"* It takes time for all pickups to reach the player.","description":"","id":"","update":"","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Lavatrix Machina","effects":"All pickups, light sources, and stage items are pulled to the player. Spins enemies around the screen.","release":"","level":"12","luck":"Yes","rarity":"2"}
Lavatrix Machina is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors that has a chance to drop from light sources after  Penshin Fatcha has been evolved the first time. It can only drop after the character reaches level 12, and its drop rate is affected by  Luck. It is an improved version of  Vacuum.
## Effects
Picking up Lavatrix Machina creates a blue circular aura with sparkles for a brief moment, and constantly has blue rings pulling in with fainter sparkles occasionally. It spins and slowly pulls all the  Experience Gems,  Treasure Chests, light sources, stage items, and pickups lying on the ground of the entire stage towards the player's character and collects them. It also spins enemies around the screen.
## Tips
- Since Lavatrix Machina indiscriminately vacuums everything but the  Master Librarian [DLC], this effect can be counter-intuitive when attempting to upgrade Treasure Chests with the  Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) or evolve weapons, especially once Lavatrix Machinas become more common the longer the run lasts.
- With  Greatest Jubilee and enough Luck (which can be obtained from character bonuses and/or  Wicked Season (XIII)), Lavatrix Machina can keep a character safe from all enemies, including  The Reaper, indefinitely, as Greatest Jubilee constantly spawns new light sources, which produce Vacuums, which the Lavatrix Machina absorbs, and which in turn refresh the duration of Lavatrix Machina.
- Lavatrix Machina can be used to obtain all nine  Gold Rings at once while bypassing the  Sun Atlanteans, as long as the player is not near the original location of the Gold Rings, when the Sun Atlanteans would otherwise show up.
- Lavatrix Machina can be used to attract stage item  Gold Fingers that wouldn't otherwise by attracted by the  Mad Groove (VIII).
- Lavatrix Machina synergizes well with the  Hail from the Future (X), given that this arcana provides easier access to this item as a pickup.
## Trivia
- While Lavatrix Machina and  Attractorb look very similar, one notable difference between the looks of the two is the fact that Lavatrix Machina has two rings while the Attractorb has one.

| {"notes":"Stacks multiplicatively with other sources of Duration.","description":"Overall Duration continuously changes between -50% and +50% over 10 seconds. The character starts gaining +1% Duration every level.","id":"T17_PAINTING","update":"Patch 0.5.0","icon":"","image":"","name":"Lost & Found Painting (XVII)","affects":["Duration"],"release":"12 April 2022","unlock":"Reach level 50 with Poppea.","arcana name":"Lost & Found Painting","type":"Arcana"}
Overall Duration continuously changes between -50% and +50% over 10 seconds. The character starts gaining +1% Duration every level.
Lost & Found Painting (XVII) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Poppea Pecorina.
 Keremet [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Lost & Found Painting (XVIII) is active, overall  Duration continuously changes between -50% and +50% over 10 second intervals. The player also gains +1% Duration each time they level up. The oscillation is done by applying a multiplier on top of everything else ranging from 0.5 to 1.5, which on average, will give an overall net neutral Duration bonus.
## Tips
- The Arcana only starts giving stat boosts when the player selects it and does not give extra stats retroactively. If the stats are desirable, select the Arcana early.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "The Lost Portrait/Painting", which is the theme song of several areas in the Reverse Castle in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Lost & Found Painting (XVII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "lastella", which means "the star" in Italian.
- This makes Lost & Found Painting (XVII) a representation of The Star Major Arcana card, symbolizing hope, faith, generosity, encouragement, inspiration, growth, and perseverance.
- This is reflected in its ability to amplify the Duration of the player's weapons, allowing them to remain active for increasingly longer periods of time.
=====
# Luck
Luck
Luck is one of the stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects various random chance events, giving the player higher odds at positive outcomes.
The player has 100% base Luck before modifications, such as the character's bonuses or PowerUps. The Luck in the stat panel is given as the difference from base Luck, meaning that a Luck stat of +30% equates to 130% total Luck. Luck modifications stack additively.
There is no upper limit to how much Luck the player may have.
## Effects
Luck increases the following:
- The chance of getting a fourth option to choose from when leveling up
- The chance of getting pickups other than  Gold Coins or  Coin Bags when destroying light sources
- The respawn rate of light sources
- The chance of getting more than one reward from a  Treasure Chest
- The chance of positive weapon effects triggering, which includes:
- Dealing critical hits
- Freezing, slowing down or defanging enemies
-  Cherry Bomb exploding
-  Gatti Amari attacking, starting a scuffle and activating certain pickups on the player instead of wasting them or causing a detrimental effect on themselves
-  Vicious Hunger turning enemies and pickups into Gold Coins
- The chance of firing more Amount of  Phas3r and  Photonstorm
- The damage of  108 Bocce [DLC] if Luck is higher than  Curse
- The spawn chance of light sources and the critical hit damage of  Greatest Jubilee's light show finisher
-  Sapphire Mist (I) chance to fire weapons multiple times
- The chance of spawning additional stage items
- The chance to be offered weapons already in your inventory
- The cooldown of traps in Dairy Plant and Gallo Tower
Luck reduces the following:
- The chance of map events, such as Swarms and Flower Walls, taking place
- The chance of negative weapon effects triggering, which includes:
-  Gatti Amari to attack the player
-  Pentagram destroying  Experience Gems, Treasure Chests and Pickups
- The amount of attacks by  Lucky Swipe [DLC]
- The odds of obtaining  Gold Finger
## PowerUp
| Luck
| Chance to get lucky goes up by 10% per rank (max +30%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | LUCK
| Max level | 3
| Bonus per level | +10%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +30%
| Initial cost | 600
| {"description":"Chance to get lucky goes up by 10% per rank (max +30%).","cost":"600","id":"LUCK","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"","name":"Luck","release":"27 October 2021","stacking":"Additive","max level":"3","bonus":"+10%"}
The Luck PowerUp increases Luck by 10% per rank, with a maximum rank of 3. At rank 3, it provides +30% Luck. It initially costs 600.
| Rank | Luck
| 1 | +10%
| 2 | +20%
| 3 | +30%
## From items
Luck's associated passive item is the  Clover, which provides +10% Luck per level, up to a maximum of +50% Luck at level 5.
Additionally,  Little Clover pickup increases Luck by 10% when collected. 
 Golden Egg permanently increases Luck by 1% for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
 Wicked Season (XIII) doubles Luck in certain 10 second intervals, and gives +0.5% Luck every level.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Ambrojoe starts with +20% Luck.
-  Exdash start with +100% Luck, and gains +10% Luck every 10 levels, up to +50% at level 50.
-  Toastie start with +100% Luck, and gains +20% Luck every 10 levels, up to +100% at level 50.
-  Smith start with +100% Luck, and gains +0.7% Luck every level, with no cap.
-  Divano and  Santa start with +30% Luck.
-  Cosmo starts with +20% Luck, and gains +1% Luck every level, with no cap.
-  Sigma and  Avatar start with +50% Luck.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Menya and  Megalo Menya start with +10% Luck.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Keitha starts with +10% Luck, and gains +1% Luck every level, with no cap.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Ghost starts with +100% Luck.
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% Luck per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% Luck per level.
## Operation Guns
-  Lucia gains +5% Luck after reviving, with no cap.
-  Browny starts with +20% Luck.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Sonia starts with +10% Luck.
-  Grant starts with +40% Luck.
-  Young Maria starts with +80% Luck.
-  Sniper starts with +100% Luck and gains +1% Luck every level, with no cap.
-  Joachim can select to gain +1% Luck per level.
-  Blackmore can select to gain +1% Luck per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% Luck per level.
-  Leon gains +10% Luck upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
-  Henry gains up to +100% Luck depending on the number of enemies in sight.
## From stages
The hyper modes of all stages, except Mad Forest, Holy Forbidden, The Bone Zone, and Eudaimonia Machine, introduce a Luck bonus:
- +10% from Inlaid Library, Dairy Plant, Gallo Tower, Cappella Magna, Green Acres, Boss Rash, Bat Country, and Tiny Bridge.
- +20% from Il Molise and Moongolow.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
- +10% from Mt.Moonspell.
The inverse modes of all stages, except Holy Forbidden and Eudaimonia Machine give a +20% luck bonus additively.
## Mechanics
## TotalLuck
TotalLuck is Internal variable used for the following formulas, and is equal to the displayed luck percentage plus one hundred.
- For example, most characters start with +30% Luck with the Luck Powerup maxed out, which corresponds to a TotalLuck variable of 130.
## Fourth level up option
It is possible to sometimes gain four item choices upon leveling up. This depends on the character's Luck. The chance to receive a fourth option is:
chanceFourth=1−(1totalLuck)
## Higher rarity pickups
Luck increases the chance to get pickups other than  Gold Coins and  Coin Bags from light sources by increasing their weight (="rarity") in the item pool:
itemWeight=rarity⋅totalLuck
## Respawn rate of light sources
Each stage introduces a chance of spawning light sources just out of sight every second, which is increased by Luck:
destructibleSpawnChance=mapDestructibleChance⋅totalLuck
## Treasure Chest level
Each Treasure Chest has a base level, ranging from 1-3 uniquely for each chest and carries 3 separate chances for a level 3, 2, and 1 treasure, these chances are also unique to each chest. The game checks for each level of treasure, starting from level 3 (5 item chest) proceeding to a lower level if the previous check fails. If all attempts fail, the chests base level is used. Luck increases the chance of each check succeeding:
chanceWithLuck=levelChance⋅totalLuck
## Chance based weapon effects
Weapon effects, such as critical hits from Bloody Tear or Pentagram's item erasing, that are based on a chance are affected by Luck. The probability of these effects triggering is:
chanceWithLuck=positiveEffectChance⋅totalLuck
## Additional stage items
Some stage items can spawn in multiples in Mad Forest and Inlaid Library. The chance of additional items spawning is increased by Luck and can be calculated as:
spawnChance=1−chancetotalLuck
Note, that this check is performed by only once at the start of the run and is thus only affected by your starting Luck from character, PowerUp and stage bonuses.
## Owned items on level up
The chance to be offered items already in your inventory is affected by Luck as follows:
ownedChance=1+0.3x−1totalLuck
where x=2 if the player's level is even and x=1 if the level is odd. The check is performed twice and, if successful, an item already owned by the player will be randomly selected to appear as an option for each successful check. However, if the second roll selects the same item as the first it discarded and a new item is pulled from the pool of all items.
## Skipping events
Most map events feature a chance for it to happen, which is unique to each event. Luck affects the chance as follows:
chanceWithLuck=eventChancetotaLluck
## Trap cooldown
Luck increases the cooldown of traps as follows:
effectiveCooldown=trapGlobalCooldown⋅totalLuck
## Tips
- Luck bonuses stack additively. The highest possible Luck stat without picking up any Little Clovers is (player level + 200)%. It can be obtained by playing as Alamaric Sniper, who starts with +100% and gains +1% per level, with 3 levels of the Luck PowerUp (+30%), 5 levels of Clover (+50%), and playing on Il Molise or Moongolow on hyper mode (+20%).
- In terms of dealing damage and surviving, Luck benefits seem weak, but it has a large influence on many other game mechanics. These effects are extremely desirable and can be a large difference maker in a run.
- Due to how Luck's effect is calculated, some chance based events can become guaranteed if the player has enough Luck. For example, +54% Luck would be enough to completely eliminate the chance of Pentagram erasing items past level 7.
=====
# Mad Groove (VIII)
| Mad Groove (VIII)
| Every 2 minutes attracts all standard stage items, pickups, and light sources towards the character.
| Arcana
| Release | 9 June 2022 (Update)
| Arcana # | 8
| ID | T08_MAD_FOREST
| Icon | 
| Affects | All Stage Items
| Unlocked by | Reach minute 31 in the Mad Forest.
| {"notes":"","description":"Every 2 minutes attracts all standard stage items, pickups, and light sources towards the character.","id":"T08_MAD_FOREST","update":"Patch 0.7.2","icon":"","image":"","name":"Mad Groove (VIII)","affects":["Passive item"],"release":"9 June 2022","unlock":"Reach minute 31 in the Mad Forest.","arcana name":"Mad Groove","type":"Arcana"}
Every 2 minutes attracts all standard stage items, pickups, and light sources towards the character.
Mad Groove (VIII) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching the 31:00 mark in Mad Forest. 
The  Master Librarian [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Mad Groove (VIII) is active, upon acquiring the Arcana and at every even minute on the in-game timer afterwards, all stage items,  Treasure Chests, pickups (including  Experience Gems), and a maximum count of light sources are immediately moved into a circle around the player.
Mad Groove does not attract relics. It also does not attract  Gold Fingers if they are stage items like in Astral Stair or in Space 54.
## Tips
- The attraction effect does not trigger every two minutes after the arcana is picked up, it is instead based on the in-game timer.
- However, once it is selected, it will be immediately triggered, then triggered after every 2-minute interval, based on the timer (for example, if a player picked up this arcana when the timer says 13:35, the arcana will be triggered at that moment, then at 14:00, then at 16:00, etc.)
- The  Gold Ring,  Silver Ring,  Metaglio Left, and  Metaglio Right will only spawn their corresponding Atlantean if their associated item is picked up.
- Mad Groove allows (or more precisely, is the only way) to legitimately obtain the Stage items in Boss Rash, including the secret items from the Yellow Sign. Otherwise these items remain out of reach from both "wall clipping" and the Flight-attribute due to the grandstand / auditorium ends having been hardcoded to be impenetrable to both clipping methods.
- Pairing this arcana with a high Magnet stat will allow for the player to attract and obtain Experience Gems or even some other pickups without the need to move.
- This arcana can be used to collect  Treasure Chests in The Lycaeum.
## Trivia
- The code name of this stage is a reference to the song "Mad Forest", which is the theme song of the Forest of Darkness in Castlevania III: Dracula's Curse. This is also referenced in the stage Mad Forest.
- Mad Groove (VIII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "laforza", which means "strength" in Italian.
- This makes Mad Groove (VIII) a representation of the Strength Major Arcana card, symbolizing spiritual strength, inner peace, perseverance, magnanimity, and managing one's emotions and instincts.
- This is reflected in its ability to collect scattered items around the map and gather them into an accessible location around the player.
- It is one of the few arcanas to have their own sound effects.
=====
# Magic Banger
| Magic Banger
| Permanently allows to change music in Stage Selection. Permanently allows to change music in Stage Selection.
| Relic
| Release | 8 May 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_BANGER
| Effects | Unlocks music selection.

| {"description":"Permanently allows to change music in Stage Selection.","id":"RELIC_BANGER","update":"Patch 0.5.2","effects":"Unlocks music selection.","sprite":"Sprite-Magic Banger.png","name":"Magic Banger","release":"8 May 2022","icon":"Icon-Magic Banger.png","location":"Due northeast of Green Acres."}
Magic Banger is a relic located in Green Acres. Once collected, it permanently unlocks a music selection in the stage select menu. The music selection allows the player to play any music and add a modifier to it in any stage.
## Obtaining
The Magic Banger is located to the north-east of the starting area in Green Acres. Its location can be tracked by following a green arrow on the player's screen. Once the Magic Banger has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, the Magic Banger can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "thankelrond".
## Interface and accessing
The Magic Banger is split into two interfaces:
- A music selection panel, which is present on the right side of the stage selection menu (after having selected a character);
- An advanced music selection window, which is accessed by clicking the blue "ADVANCED" button found on the regular music selection panel.
## Music selection panel
The music selection panel with the track The Beginning being selected. The music modifier that is set here is Hyper.
Being one part of the interface of the Magic Banger, it allows for the player to quickly set a desired unlocked track, without the need to access the advanced music selection window. The regular music selection panel simply displays the track name, its icon, and the currently set speed.
By default, unless if the player selects a specific character that has their own theme, the music selection panel will change the currently set song, depending on what stage the player is choosing. However, in any case, by using the regular music selection panel, the player can navigate through the left and right arrows, which are near the track icon, to change the currently set track. They can also use the lower set of arrows to modify a track's speed and tempo.
The order of the tracks in the music selection panel are not the same as the ones shown in the albums in the advanced music selection window. For base game tracks in particular, instead of going through the chronological album/track list order, the regular music selection panel selects music from all base game albums. Furthermore, the general order of the tracks has to do with a significant part of the chronological order of the stages, then character tracks (such as Red & Blue), then tracks having to do with some parts of the game's interface (such as Dust Elementals), then there are a few extra base game tracks after those, such as Before Concession X. After that, players can get to access DLC tracks, where even the order of the tracks of the regular music selection panel and the advanced music selection window may not be the same in some cases.
In any case, if the player prefers to have more options and/or easier access to modifying the track they want to set, they can head over to the advanced music selection window by clicking the blue "ADVANCED" button on the bottom of the regular music selection panel.
## Advanced music selection window
The advanced music selection window, with Forest Night Fever being selected.
TBA
## List of music
Main article: Music
## Music modifiers
When inverse mode is selected, themes of Stages 1-5, Moongolow, Bone Zone, and Boss Rash will switch to their alternate "Side B" track.
There are three music modifiers for each theme: 
- Normal
- Hyper: the theme will have higher key and tempo
- Forsaken: the theme will have lower key and tempo
## Trivia
- When playing as  Giovanna Grana, her theme starts out slower and will gradually increase when leveling up the  Gatti Amari to the normal tempo.
- The Magic Banger used to be located to the far west of the starting area in Inlaid Library. This was changed in patch 0.7.3.
- What was formerly "The Green Acres Challenge" has been renamed to "No Mortals Allowed" in patch 0.9.0.
- The Bone Zone used to play the Forsaken variation of "Iron Green Intent". This was replaced with "Unholy Invocation" in patch 0.10.0.
- Il Molise used to play "Song of Mana". This was replaced with "Vempair Survaivors" in patch 1.0.
- Boss Rash used to play "No Mortals Allowed". This was replaced with "Needs More Fighting" in patch 1.0.
## External links
- Vampire Survivors Original Soundtrack Playlist

# Magnet
Magnet
Magnet is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It determines the radius inside which  Experience Gems and other pickups will be collected by the player's character.
The base value of Magnet is 30. It is only shown after starting the game, and not in the Character Selection menu. Magnet modifications stack multiplicatively.
There is no upper limit to how much Magnet the player may have.
 Blood Astronomia (XXI), as well as  Victorian Horror (XVIII), turns the character's Magnet area into a zone to damage enemies.
Magnet also affects Base Damage, Speed, Area and Duration of  Vol Umbra [DLC].
## PowerUp
| Magnet
| Items Pickup range +25% per rank (max +50%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | MAGNET
| Max level | 2
| Bonus per level | +25%
| Stacking | Multiplicative
| Max effect | +56.25%
| Initial cost | 300
| {"description":"Items Pickup range +25% per rank (max +50%).","cost":"300","id":"MAGNET","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"","name":"Magnet","release":"27 October 2021","stacking":"Multiplicative","max level":"2","bonus":"+25%"}
The Magnet PowerUp increases Magnet exponentially, multiplying it by 1.25 per rank, with a maximum rank of 2. At rank 2, it multiplies Magnet by 1.5625. It initially costs 300.
| Rank | Magnet
| 1 | 1.25x
| 2 | 1.5625x
The calculation of initial Magnet is done differently from other stats, similarly to that of  Max Health:
Magnet=30*Character bonus*PowerUp multiplier
For example,  Poe starts with 1.25x Magnet, so using Poe with the PowerUp at rank 2, Magnet would be:
30*1.25*1.5625=58.59375
## From items
Magnet's associated passive item is the  Attractorb, which provides varying multiplicative bonuses to Magnet, up to a maximum of 3.98x Magnet at level 5.
 Golden Egg can permanently increases Magnet base value by 0.3 for the character that collected it. So the calculation will be:
Magnet=(30+Eggs bonus)*Character bonus*PowerUp multiplier
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Poe starts with +25% Magnet.
-  Ambrojoe starts with +20% Magnet.
-  Peppino,  Queen Sigma, and  Avatar start with +100% Magnet
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Genevieve and  Je-Ne-Viv start with +50% Magnet.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate has a chance to gain a random amount of Magnet every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
## Operation Guns
-  Bahamut starts with +25% Magnet.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Hector and  Isaac start with +25% Magnet.
-  Carrie and  Yoko start with +50% Magnet.
-  Graham,  Brauner, and  Persephone start with +100% Magnet.
-  Chaos starts with +200% Magnet.
## Notes
- In the Character Selection menu the displayed Magnet value only shows the percentage applied at the start of the game instead of the actual value. Also, the shown percentage doesn't increase exponentially, which is incorrect. These two factors combined make it difficult to tell how much Magnet the player actually has at the start.
- The Magnet value shown in the stats panel, both in the Character Selection menu and in-game, is rounded to the closest whole number. The actual value of Magnet is a decimal number.
- To calculate Magnet initial in-game value using the value shown in the Character Selection menu is a little difficult as each egg bonus adds 1% to it, which is an additive bonus, not multiplicative. To do it, the following formula can be used: Magnet=(((Menu%Value−Character%Value−PowerUp%Value)*0.3)+30)*Character bonus*PowerUp multiplier
- For example, Poe with no Eggs Bonus: Magnet=(((75−25−50)*0.3)+30)*1.25*1.5625=58.59375
- Note that 0.3 is the value added by the Golden Egg in the Magnet base value, so the purpose of the formula is to find the bonus acquired with Golden Eggs using the %  showed in the menu as each one increases it by 1%, to sum it with the base value as showed in the previous formula and then to multiplying by the character and PowerUp bonuses.
## Tips
- Bonuses to Magnet apply multiplicatively, meaning each bonus will provide a percent of the current total, not the original total. This means that the Attractorb, PowerUp, and playing as Poe provide more powerful bonuses as the stat gets increased.
- With no bonuses to Magnet, it will go from 30, to 45, to 60, to 75, to 119.
- With two levels into the Magnet PowerUp (which add an additional +50% to the stat), it will start at 47 and increase to 70, 94, 117, 140, and finally 187. The reason it starts at 47 and not 45 is because each +25% bonus from the PowerUp is applied separately, first taking 25% of 30 to give 37.5 and then 25% of 37.5 to give 47. Rounding is involved in the process.
- For gold farming purposes an initial value of about 120 with a fully upgraded Magnet bringing it to 478 is required to pull gold bags from off screen, but for it to be effective the player will need slightly more.
- With 2 levels of Magnet and Peppino's +100% bonus, Magnet will cap out at 373 which is around 4 times larger than the default pickup range and 120 points higher than other characters' reach.
- Poe's range 1.56 times larger than default range and about 30 points higher.

# Masquerade
| Masquerade
| Unlocks Party Mode Unlocks Party Mode
| Relic
| Release | 28 October 2025 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_PARTY
| Effects | Grants access to Party Mode
| Location | Due west of Westwoods.
| {"description":"Unlocks Party Mode","id":"RELIC_PARTY","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","dlc":"","sprite":"","name":"Masquerade","release":"28 October 2025","location":"Due west of Westwoods.","icon":"","effects":"Grants access to Party Mode"}
Masquerade is a relic located in Westwoods. Once collected, it unlocks Party Mode. 
Party Mode functions similarly to co-op, with the same levelling, max weapon, and evolution/passive sharing systems. However, instead of having to play with others, the player is able to choose the character and behavior of up to 3 CPU companions. 
Despite Party Mode and local co-op sharing the same menu, Party Mode does not replace the latter. The player is still able to play with others locally by connecting extra controllers. 
- Sealing Masquerade in the collection will make this menu local co-op only.
## Obtaining
Masquerade is found by collecting it at the far western end of Westwoods. Once Masquerade has been collected, it will not appear again.
- To progress through Westwoods, the player must repeatedly win the "Spin the Wheel" minigame at the Giocare merchant.
Alternatively, the Masquerade can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "arefriendselectric".
## Party Mode CPU Behavior
In Party Mode, CPUs have 8 available behaviors:
- [Aspect of Moon] Defensive
- CPUs stay close to the player. Multiple CPUs with this behavior will try to evenly defend the player in a ring formation.
- [Aspect of Sun] Aggressive
- CPUs stay close to enemies, but attempt to avoid damage.
- [Aspect of Volcano] Chaotic
- CPUs move randomly.
- [Aspect of Seawinds] Mirrors your movements.
- CPUs perform the opposite inputs the player does. This will impede progress, as the screen must fit every player or CPU.
- [Aspect of City] Follows you.
- CPUs repeat the player's inputs after a delay.
- [Aspect of Stone] Greedy for pickups.
- CPUs collect pickups when available, including  Experience Gems.
- [Aspect of Black] Stands still...?
- CPUs stand still. This will also impede progress.
- [Skull] Actively trying to get damaged.
- Similar to the Aggressive Behavior, but CPUS will not avoid enemies and keep receiving damage.
There is a Share Passives option on Stage Selection, which enables or disables the sharing of passive items for evolution purposes.
## Restrictions
- Much like Co-op Mode, the player is subject to Weapon and Passive Slot restrictions depending on Party Size:
- With 2 total party members, each member will have 4 Weapon and Passive Slots.
- With 3 total party members, each member will have 3 Weapon and Passive Slots.
- With 4 total party members, each member will have 2 Weapon and Passive Slots.
- Golden Eggs will be disabled.
=====
# Max Health
Max Health
Max Health is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It determines how much health the player's character can have in total.
The base value of Max Health is 100 before modifications, such as the characters' bonuses or PowerUps. The characters' Max Health modification is additive. Max Health modifications from PowerUps and items are calculated after the character modification and stack multiplicatively.
There is no upper limit to how much Max Health the player can have.
Max Health also affects:
- Base Damage and Amount of  Mazo Familiar
- Damage of explosions created by  Vanitas Whip [DLC]
The amount of missing Health affects Cooldown of  Metal Claw [DLC].
With  Divine Bloodline (IX) activated, all weapons gain +0.1 flat damage per missing Health, uncapped.
## PowerUp
| Max Health
| Augments Max Health by 10% per rank (max +30%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | MAXHEALTH
| Max level | 3
| Bonus per level | +10%
| Stacking | Multiplicative
| Max effect | +33.1%
| Initial cost | 200
| {"description":"Augments Max Health by 10% per rank (max +30%).","cost":"200","id":"MAXHEALTH","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","image":"","name":"Max Health","release":"27 October 2021","stacking":"Multiplicative","max level":"3","bonus":"+10%"}
The Max Health PowerUp increases Max Health exponentially, multiplying it by 1.1 each rank up to a maximum rank of 3. At rank 3, it multiplies Max Health by 1.331. It initially costs 200.
| Rank | Max Health
| 1 | 1.1x
| 2 | 1.21x
| 3 | 1.331x
The calculation of initial Max Health is done differently from other stats, similarly to that of Magnet:
Max Health=(100+character bonus)*PowerUp multiplier
For example,  Poe reduces Max Health by 30, so using Poe with the PowerUp at rank 3, Max Health would be:
(100−30)*1.331=93.17
## From items
Max Health's associated passive item is  Hollow Heart, which multiplies Max Health by 1.2 each time it is leveled up. Its maximum level is 5. At level 5 it provides a 2.49x multiplier to Max Health.
-  Metaglio Left multiplies Max Health by 1.05 each time it is leveled up. Its maximum level is 9. At level it provides a 1.477x multiplier to Max Health.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] has a chance of granting a Max Health stat bonus of +20 upon activation.
-  Golden Eggs can permanently increase Max Health by +1 for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
-  Awake (IV) provides +10% Max Health per  Revival consumed.
-  Divine Bloodline (IX) provides +0.5 Max Health per enemy killed by retaliatory damage.
## From darkana
-  Crystal Cries (XII) generates crystals when killing frozen enemies that can be collected to slightly increase Max Health,  Recovery and  Growth.
## From survarot
- Survarot of Antonio Belpaese provides +80 Max Health. Survarot of Antonio Belpaese provides +5 Max Health every level.
- Survarot of Gennaro Belpaese provides +30 Max Health every 5 levels (Max 5 times).

-  Exdash starts with -23 Max Health.
-  Toastie starts with -99 Max Health and gains +9998 Max Health upon reaching level 100.
-  Smith starts with -93 Max Health.
-  Cosmo starts with -80 Max Health.
-  Gyorunton starts with +200 Max Health.
-  Red Death starts with +155 Max Health.
-  Sigma starts with +233 Max Health.

-  Scorej-Oni starts with +8 Max Health.
-  Gyoruntin starts with +35 Max Health.
-  Robbert gains +16% Max Health, up to a maximum of +400, when he falls below 20% Max Health and triggers his CriticalHP ability.
## Legacy of the Moonspell

-  Menya starts with -20 Max Health, can be invincible throughout the run.
-  Syuuto starts with +50 Max Health. He gains +100 Max Health after he morphs when his  starting weapon evolves.

## Emergency Meeting

-  Ghost starts with +156 Max Health.
-  Impostor starts with +20 Max Health.
-  Megalo Impostor starts with +80 Max Health.
## Operation Guns
-  Bill and  Lance start with +50 Max Health.
-  Lucia gains +20 Max Health after reviving, with no cap.
-  Brad,  Probotector,  Stanley, and  Bahamut start with +100 Max Health.
-  Browny and  Newt start with +40 Max Health.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Julia starts with +8 Max Health.
-  Axe Armor starts with +10 Max Health.
-  Christopher,  Simon,  Rinaldo,  Shanoa,  Albus,  Nathan and  Soleil start with +20 Max Health.

-  Maxim and  Reinhardt start with +40 Max Health.
-  Walter and  Olrox start with +50 Max Health.
-  Julius starts with +80 Max Health.
-  Cornell gains +100 Max Health after he morphs upon obtaining  Trinum Custodem.
-  Elizabeth,  Alucard,  Blue Crescent Moon Cornell and  Carmilla start with +100 Max Health.
-  Death starts with +155 Max Health.
-  Galamoth starts with +156 Max Health.
-  Barlowe starts with +200 Max Health.
-  Megalo Elizabeth,  Megalo Olrox and  Megalo Death start with +300 Max Health.
-  Dracula and  Megalo Dracula start with +566 Max Health.
-  Leon starts with +20 Max Health and gains another +80 Max Health upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
-  Chaos starts with +2097052 Max Health.
-  Fleaman starts with -99 Max Health.
## Notes
- Max Health value shown in the Character Selection menu doesn't increase exponentially, which is incorrect.
- Max Health value shown in the stats panel, both in the Character Selection menu and in-game, is rounded to the closest whole number. The actual value of Max Health is a decimal number.
- Negative Max Health makes the character invulnerable.
-  White Hand kills the character by setting Max Health to 0 (even if Max Health is set to be negative).
## Tips
- Max Health bonuses are simultaneously more useful and less useful for characters that have less Max Health: The Hollow Heart provides them with especially-important survivability, but it will not scale as fast or as high as other characters. On the opposite side, Max Health Bonuses are especially potent while playing as  Clerici.
## Trivia
- A recurring spelling error in the descriptions of some items is Max Health being spelled as "MaxHealth." This typo can be found in the descriptions of the  Crystal Cries (XII) arcana and arcana pickups, such as the  Cursed Soul or the  Crystallized Soul.

Metaglio Left is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked after obtaining Yellow Sign. It cannot be found via level ups, and must be found as a stage item.
Metaglio Left increases  Recovery by 0.1 and multiplies  Max Health by 1.05 per level.
Metaglio Left, alongside  Metaglio Right, is required for the evolution of  Laurel,  Crimson Shroud.
Metaglio Left cannot be obtained from level ups, but can be found as a stage item in every stage, except Bat Country, Tiny Bridge, and Eudaimonia Machine. Generally, it will spawn west of the starting area. It will always be guarded by a City Atlantean.
## Levels
Metaglio Left has 9 levels. Each of them multiplies the current value of Max Health by 1.05. At max level, Metaglio Left provides +0.8 Recovery.
| Level | Description | Effect total
| Level 1 | Channels dark powers to protect the bearer. | -
| Level 2 | Health recovery increases by 0.1 HP per second. Max Health increases by 5%. | 0.1 Recovery, 1.05x Max Health
| Level 3 | Health recovery increases by 0.1 HP per second. Max Health increases by 5%. | 0.2 Recovery, 1.1025x Max Health
| Level 4 | Health recovery increases by 0.1 HP per second. Max Health increases by 5%. | 0.3 Recovery, 1.157625x Max Health
| Level 5 | Health recovery increases by 0.1 HP per second. Max Health increases by 5%. | 0.4 Recovery, 1.21550625x Max Health
| Level 6 | Health recovery increases by 0.1 HP per second. Max Health increases by 5%. | 0.5 Recovery, 1.2762815625x Max Health
| Level 7 | Health recovery increases by 0.1 HP per second. Max Health increases by 5%. | 0.6 Recovery, 1.340095640625x Max Health
| Level 8 | Health recovery increases by 0.1 HP per second. Max Health increases by 5%. | 0.7 Recovery, 1.40710042265625x Max Health
| Level 9 | Health recovery increases by 0.1 HP per second. Max Health increases by 5%. | 0.8 Recovery, 1.4774554437890625x Max Health
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Laurel | + | Max level:Metaglio Left+Metaglio Right | = | Crimson Shroud
## Tips
- Metaglio Left does not provide any stats at level 1.
## Trivia
- Metaglio Left,  Metaglio Right,  Silver Ring,  Gold Ring,  Torrona's Box, and  Outer Saboteur [DLC] are the only passive items with max level 9 instead of the usual 5.
- When both Metaglio Left and Metaglio Right are obtained, they move to the first two slots of the passive weapons and form a cross-shape on a wand.
- Metaglio is a portmanteau of the Italian words "metà" meaning "half" and "taglio" meaning "cut", literally "cut in half". This word first appeared in a sketch by the comedian Maccio Capatonda.

# Might
Might
Might is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects the damage output of all weapons.
The player has 100% base Might before modifications, such as the characters bonuses or PowerUps. The Might in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base Might, meaning that Might stat of +30% equals to 130% total Might, for example. Might modifications from characters, PowerUps, passive items, and arcanas stack additively.
The upper limit of total Might after character, PowerUp, and item modifications is 1000%, or +900% in the stats panel.
- Might has a further multiplier of 1.25x on  SpellStrike [DLC].
- Might also affects the charge ability of  Quincy Morris [DLC].
-  Stanley [DLC] gets +1  Armor per +10% Might.
## PowerUp

| Raises inflicted damage by 5% per rank (max +25%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | POWER
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +5%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +25%
| Initial cost | 200

The Might PowerUp increases Might by 5% per rank, with a maximum rank of 5. At rank 5, it provides +25% Might. It initially costs 200.

## From items
Might's associated passive item is  Spinach, which provides +10% Might per level, up to a maximum of +50% Might at level 5.
-  Torrona's Box also provides +4%, then +3% per level, up to a maximum of +25% at level 8.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] has a chance of granting a Might stat bonus of +4% upon activation.
-  Golden Eggs can permanently increase Might by +1% for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
-  Awake (IV) provides +5% Might when chosen per  Revival consumed.
-  Divine Bloodline (IX) provides +1 flat damage (displayed as Might in stats menu) for every 10 health less than the  Max Health.
-  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) provides +20% Might when chosen per active weapon slot left empty.
## From characters
-  Antonio gains +10% Might every 10 levels, up to +50% at level 50.
-  Arca starts with +10% Might.
-  Lama starts with +10% Might. She gains +5% every 10 levels, up to +20% at level 40. In total, she can have +30% Might.
-  Christine starts with -35% Might.
-  Pugnala gains +1% Might every level, with no cap.
-  Zi'Assunta gains +0.5% Might every level, with no cap.
-  Exdash and  Toastie start with -10% Might.
-  Smith starts with -10% Might and gains +0.7% Might every level, with no cap.
-  Marrabbio and  Red Death start with +20% Might.
-  Minnah start with -70% Might and gain +10% Might every 3 levels, up to +70% at level 24. Their Might also cycles between multiplied by 2 and divided by 2 every minute.
-  Leda starts with +100% Might.
-  Gyorunton starts with +30% Might.
-  Sigma starts with +50% Might and gains +1% Might every level, with no cap.
-  Avatar starts with +50% Might and gains 0.5% Might every level, with no cap.
-  Rose starts with -90% Might. She gains +10% Might every 12 levels until level 108.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Menya starts with +10% Might. She gains +200% Might while in her boosted state.
-  Syuuto starts with +50% Might. He gains +1% Might every level, with no cap, after he morphs when his  starting weapon evolves.
-  Megalo Menya starts with +100% Might.
-  Megalo Syuuto starts with +50% Might and gains +1% Might every level, with no cap.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Maruto starts with +20 Might.
-  Luminaire gains +200% Might temporarily after reviving.
-  Je-Ne-Viv starts with +50% Might. She gains +1% Might every level, with no cap.
-  Sammy starts -60% Might. They gains an additional +10% Might on levels 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, and 21.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate has a chance to gain a random amount of Might every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Ghost starts with -100% Might and cannot be increased by any means.
-  Impostor starts with +20% Might and gains +200% Might while in their boosted state.
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% Might per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% Might per level.
-  Megalo Impostor starts with +80% Might.
## Operation Guns
-  Bill gains 1% Might at specific kill thresholds, with no cap.
-  Lucia gains +4% Might after reviving, with no cap.
-  Brad and  Stanley start with +20% Might.
-  Probotector starts with +20 Might and gains +10% Might per +1  Armor, with no cap.
-  Newt gains +1% Might per +1%  Speed, with no cap.
-  Bahamut starts with +50% Might and gains +1% Might per +1%  Greed or +1%  Curse, with no cap.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Juste,  John,  Jonathan,  Reinhardt,  Eric,  Carmilla,  Olrox,  Malphas and  Death start with +20% Might.
-  Julius starts with +40% Might.
-  Rinaldo and  Elizabeth start with +50% Might.
-  Galamoth starts with +100% Might.
-  Megalo Elizabeth,  Megalo Olrox and  Megalo Death start with +250% Might.
-  Axe Armor starts with +10% Might and gains +5% Might every 10 levels, up to +20% at level 40. In total, it can have +30% Might.
-  Soma,  Shanoa and  Blue Crescent Moon Cornell gain +1% Might every level, with no cap.
-  Dracula starts with +100% Might and gains +1% Might every level and +1% Might per +1%  Curse, with no cap.
-  Megalo Dracula starts with +250% Might and gains +1% Might every level and +1% Might per +1% Curse with no cap.
-  Christopher gains +1% Might per +1%  Greed temporarily, with no cap.
-  Simon gains +1% Might per +1%  Growth temporarily, with no cap.
-  Barlowe gains +1% Might per 24 health of overhealing, with no cap.
-  Maria Renard starts with +25% Might and gains +1% Might per 24 health of overhealing, with no cap.
-  Joachim can select to gain +1% Might per level.
-  Blackmore can select to gain +1% Might per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% Might per level.
-  Leon gains +10% Might upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
-  Maxim gains Might while Invulnerable. This additional Might scales with baseline Might.
-  Albus gains a bonus of up to 40% Might depending on how many enemies are in sight.
-  Shaft gains +200% Might during his HP-Critical ability.
## Tips
- Since Might is percentage based, it is more effective with weapons that have high Base Damage, such as the Death Spiral and the Hellfire.

# Milky Way Map
Milky Way Map
| Milky Way Map
| Permanently enables the map in the pause menu. Permanently enables the map in the pause menu.
| Relic
| Release | 2 March 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_MAP
| Effects | Unlocks the map in the pause menu.
| Location | Due south of Dairy Plant.
| {"description":"Permanently enables the map in the pause menu.","id":"RELIC_MAP","update":"Patch 0.3.0","effects":"Unlocks the map in the pause menu.","sprite":"Sprite-Milky Way Map.png","name":"Milky Way Map","release":"2 March 2022","icon":"Icon-Milky Way Map.png","location":"Due south of Dairy Plant."}
The Milky Way Map is a relic located in Dairy Plant. Once collected, it permanently unlocks a viewable map in the pause menu. The map displays the player's location as well as the locations of pickups, stage items, relics,  coffins, and  Treasure Chests in any stage.
A "Show Guides"/"Hide Guides" button is also added to the pause menu, which, when toggled on, displays an arrow pointing to each item with that item's icon.
## Obtaining
The Milky Way Map is located to the far south of the starting area in Dairy Plant. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. Once the Milky Way Map has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, the Milky Way Map can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "leadmetothecheese".
## Interface
The Milky Way Map in use in Mad Forest.
TBA
## Trivia
- The spell "leadmetothecheese" is a reference to the  Cheese in Dairy Plant, with its location only being revealed if the player has the Milky Way Map and leads to another secret that unlocks a secret character.
- A square or rectangle depicted in the map is called a tileset.
=====
# Mindbender
| Mindbender
| Permanently allows to change character appearance (where applicable), maximum weapon loadout, and favorite characters. Permanently allows to change character appearance (where applicable), maximum weapon loadout, and favorite characters.
| Relic
| Release | 23 June 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_NOSEGLASSES
| Effects | Unlocks cosmetic options when clicking on certain characters in the character selection menu and allows to set the maximum number of weapons.
| Location | Fill 50 entries in the Collection.
| {"description":"Permanently allows to change character appearance (where applicable), maximum weapon loadout, and favorite characters.","id":"RELIC_NOSEGLASSES","update":"Patch 0.7.3","effects":"Unlocks cosmetic options when clicking on certain characters in the character selection menu and allows to set the maximum number of weapons.","sprite":"Sprite-Mindbender.png","name":"Mindbender","release":"23 June 2022","icon":"Icon-Mindbender.png","location":"Fill 50 entries in the Collection."}
Mindbender is a relic that permanently unlocks three additional options that can be accessed from the character selection menu: the maximum number of weapons, the character skin system, and the ability to favorite characters. It is automatically obtained by filling out 50 entries in the Collection.
## Obtaining
Unlike other relics, the Mindbender does not need to be collected off the ground in a certain stage. Rather, it is automatically awarded after the player fills 50 entries in the Collection.
The Mindbender can also be unlocked by typing "teleportustomars", either on the main menu or in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane as a spell.
Since skin selection is required to progress through Emerald Diorama, the Mindbender can alternatively be obtained by fully evolving  Fleuret [DLC] into  Espada Ropera [DLC].
## Options
This article or section contains information that is out of date.
Reason: Discuss interface changes from 1.15
You can help improve this page by editing it.
## Maximum number of weapons
"Max weapons" option, being set to 6.
Being one of the two options in the character menu that is unlocked upon obtaining the Mindbender, it allows for the player to set their desired maximum number of weapons per round to be between 1 and 6. However, in any case, there are ways to obtain beyond the set number of maximum weapons, such as using the  Candybox.
The way to navigate this option is by clicking on the button. Doing so will increment the maximum number of weapons for each click until it reaches 6, where clicking it afterwards will set the number to 1.
By default, and before obtaining the Mindbender, the maximum number of weapons a player can obtain is 6.
This option does not modify the maximum number of passive items a player can obtain, which is fixed at 6 passive items.
## Character skin system
Retired Poe Ratcho selected as the current skin, as indicated in the bottom panel of the character selection menu.
The character skin system is the other option that is unlocked upon obtaining the Mindbender. It allows for the player to change the appearance of certain characters that have other skins available. Players can change a character skin by clicking on the character sprite on the bottom panel of the character selection menu, though not every character has selectable skins.
There are cases where skins are unlocked with a character itself, and other cases where they can be unlocked separately, such as completing an adventure or by other means. They can also be unlocked by using spells.
In some cases, such as the legacy or Halloween skins, character skins are completely cosmetic. However, there are many more cases where character skins are not cosmetic. The different types of skins where this is the case are:
- Adventure skins: Generally, adventure skins modify the appearance, amplify stats, and make the player start with the maximum level passive item that corresponds to evolving the character's starting weapon. For example, Retired Poe Ratcho starts with 100 Max Health instead of 70 and 5 Recovery instead of 0. Furthermore, he starts with a level 5  Pummarola, which can be used to evolve Poe's starting weapon,  Garlic, into the  Soul Eater.
- Skins with different starting weapons: There are characters that have different skins where the character appearance is not modified, but instead, the starting weapon can be altered, which is especially the case for characters in Ode to Castlevania, such as  Leon Belmont and  Eric Lecarde.
- Solo/non-solo skins: There are a few characters, particularly in Emerald Diorama, such as  Tsunanori Mido, who have solo and non-solo skin variants, where they are either paired with followers or another character with their non-solo variants.
- Multi-variant skins: There are also a few characters who come in multiple distinct forms with different starting weapons but the same stats, such as the  Familiar or the  Kugutsu.  Diva No. 5, in particular, has several distinct forms, all with different starting stats, but with the same starting weapon, which is the  Super Missile (except for  Virginia which starts with  Twin Dragon instead).
If a player attempts to preemptively unlock skins before obtaining the Mindbender, they will have the skin unlocked, but won't be able to use it until they have unlocked the Mindbender.
## Interface
The character selection menu, where options included from the Mindbender are highlighted in light green.
The options of the Mindbender can be accessed solely from the character selection menu. The character skin or the set maximum number of weapons cannot be changed outside of the character menu.
## Maximum number of weapons
The setting of the maximum number of weapons is indicated on the bottom left panel of the character selection menu, directly below the stats panel on the left. The button to selecting the maximum number of weapons is located to the left of the checkbox of enabling  Golden Eggs.
Weapons panel with the max number of weapons set to 4.
If the player has chosen to have a maximum of less than 6 weapons, during a round, the excluded weapon slots will be indicated with an [A sprite icon representing Seal I with no link] mark on the top left weapons panel. For example, if the maximum number of weapons is set to 4, the weapons panel will indicate an [A sprite icon representing Seal I with no link] mark on the last two weapon slots. However, this does not mean that players cannot go beyond that limit, such as by using the  Candybox after having obtained 4 other weapons beforehand.
## Character skin system
The character skin system is integrated onto the character description panel, which is below the main character selection panel. The number of squares below the character sprite indicates the number of available unlocked skins for a particular character, and the yellow square indicates the currently selected skin. If there are no squares, then this means the character only has one skin.
Upon clicking the character sprite in this panel, the character skin will be switched. Depending on what the skin may do, it may simplify modify the character's appearance, the starting weapon, and/or even their description.

Mini Crewmate is a passive item from the Emergency Meeting DLC of Vampire Survivors.
Mini Crewmate is added to the level up item pool after Report! reaches level 2. It is consumed upon Report! evolving into Emergency Meeting (weapon).
Mini Crewmate can be found as a stage item on Polus Replica.
## Effects
Mini Crewmate mirrors the effects of Garlic, acting as a lesser version of the weapon. It also spawns a miniature, non-functional Crewmate Dino that flies around the player's character. Each Mini Crewmate level corresponds to the same level for Garlic.
Once it is consumed after evolving Report!, the Garlic effect disappears, although the flying Mini Crewmate would still remain.

# Mirror of Truth
"Mirror" redirects here. For the relic, see Gracia's Mirror.
DLC Content (Ode to Castlevania)
This article primarily features content only available in DLC, which may not be available in all copies of the game.
| Mirror of Truth [DLC]
| Temporarily doubles Amount. Activates all equipped weapons at once when picked up.
| Pickup
| Release | 31 October 2024 (Update)
| ID | TP_MIRROR_OF_TRUTH
| Rarity | 2
| Unlock level | 12

| Effects | Doubles Amount and activates all the character's weapons for a short duration.

| {"notes":"* Can only appear when playing on Ode to Castlevania, or when playing as a character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC.","description":"Temporarily doubles Amount. Activates all equipped weapons at once when picked up.","id":"TP_MIRROR_OF_TRUTH","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","dlc":"oc","image":"","name":"Mirror of Truth","effects":"Doubles Amount and activates all the character's weapons for a short duration.","release":"31 October 2024","level":"12","luck":"Yes","rarity":"2"}
The Mirror of Truth is an item pickup from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. 
The Mirror of Truth temporarily multiplies Amount by 2× and activates all of the character's weapons for a duration of about 10 seconds, which stacks with other picked-up Mirrors of Truth.
Mirrors of Truth are permanently unlocked upon collecting the  Capra Gate, which is dropped by defeating  Paranoia in the Ode to Castlevania stage. Once unlocked, they only appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage or while playing as a character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in any other stage.
Additionally, picking up a total of 7 Mirrors of Truth or more unlocks  Charlotte Aulin as a playable character.
## Trivia
- Mirrors of Truth function differently than they do in the original Castlevania games, where they instantly kill all enemies on the screen when picked up.
## External links
- Mirror of Truth on the Castlevania Wiki

# Moonlight Bolero (VI)
| Moonlight Bolero (VI)
| An extra treasure boss spawns every minute. The treasure it carries might occasionally contain arcanas and random passive weapons.
| Darkana
| Release | 15 August 2024 (Update)
| Darkana # | 6
| ID | D06_BOLERO
| Icon | 
| Affects | [Treasure Chest][Treasure Chest 2][Treasure Chest 5][Treasure Chest 4]
| Unlocked by | Be embraced by the Darkasso.
| {"notes":"","description":"An extra treasure boss spawns every minute. The treasure it carries might occasionally contain arcanas and random passive weapons.","id":"D06_BOLERO","update":"Patch 1.11: Bug fixes and improvements","icon":"","image":"","name":"Moonlight Bolero (VI)","affects":["Treasure Chest","Treasure Chest","Treasure Chest","Treasure Chest"],"release":"15 August 2024","unlock":"Be embraced by the Darkasso.","arcana name":"Moonlight Bolero","type":"Darkana"}
An extra treasure boss spawns every minute. The treasure it carries might occasionally contain arcanas and random passive weapons.
Moonlight Bolero (VI) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked upon obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Moonlight Bolero (VI) is active, a special boss that is guaranteed to drop a  Treasure Chest will spawn every minute. The chance of each boss spawning is as follows:
- 10% chance for a  Levatee Darkasso (Blue), which drops an  Arcana Chest.
- If the player has met the maximum number of Arcana they can have, normally being 3, this chance drops to 5%. Sources of Arcana outside of Treasure Chests, like the  Merchant, will increase the player's maximum number of Arcana, effectively making this decreased chance only happen when the player has obtained 2 Arcanas from Treasure Chests.
- 20% chance for a  Levarsee Darkasso, which drops a  Dark Chest.
- If the player has the maximum number of passive items, which is normally 6, or more, this chance jumps up to 60%.
- If both rolls fail, then a  Levatee Darkasso (Green) is spawned, which will drop a  Silver Chest.
These chests can always provide new passive items and Arcanas, even if the player already has three or four Arcanas, or their passive item slots are full. If a run continues for long enough with this Darkana active, the player could eventually obtain every passive item and Arcana.
The Levatee Darkasso bosses will constantly move in a straight line across the screen. It cannot despawn and will return to the screen eventually else if it gets too far from the player.
The Levarsee Darkasso boss emits smoke and will move towards the player like normal enemies and bosses. The black chests dropped by these bosses have a chance to give a random passive item the player does not have, including the passive items from the Emergency Meeting and Operation Guns DLCs if the player has their respective weapons. A new passive item is not guaranteed, however, and there is a 50% chance the chest will instead level up a random weapon or another passive already in a slot.
## Tips
- This Arcana can be rerolled at the start of the run by exiting and re-entering a run, allowing a player to guarantee the highly valuable Arcana chest at time 0:00.
- Typically, without rerolling, the player is expected to get 1 extra Arcana Chest.
- This Arcana is generally a good pick if the player is using  Game Killer (0), as the presence of more treasure chests is very useful.
- Additionally, Game Killer (0)'s effect of making all treasure chests contain at least three items can cause one of the random passive item chests to provide multiple new passive items at once.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Moonlight Nocturne" from Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Moonlight Bolero (VI) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "darkassami", the same spell that unlocks the Darkasso.
- Moonlight Bolero (VI) is a representation of The Lovers Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing conflict, disharmony, imbalance, and selfishness.
- This is reflected in its ability to spawn more enemies for the player's benefit.

# Move Speed
This article is about movement speed. For projectile speed, see Speed.
Move Speed (also known as Movement Speed, originally known as Speed) is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects how fast the character moves in relation to the default speed.
The player has 100% base MoveSpeed before modifications. The MoveSpeed in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base MoveSpeed, meaning that MoveSpeed stat of +30% equals to 130% total MoveSpeed, for example. MoveSpeed modifications from characters, PowerUps, and passive items stack additively. Stage bonuses stack multiplicatively with the collective bonus from the aforementioned.
There is no upper limit to how much MoveSpeed the player may have.
MoveSpeed also affects:
- Cooldown of  Shadow Pinion and  Valkyrie Turner
- Cooldown of  Valmanway [DLC]while moving
-  Tragic Princess (III)'s effectiveness
-  Babi-Onna's effectiveness on triggering weapons
 Ariana [DLC] gains +1  Armor per +10% MoveSpeed.
 Sheena Etranzi [DLC] gains -1%  Cooldown per +2% MoveSpeed.
## PowerUp

| Character moves 5% faster per rank (max 10%).
| PowerUp

| Bonus per level | +5%
