The Move Speed PowerUp increases MoveSpeed by 5% per rank, with a maximum rank of 2. At rank 2, it provides +10% MoveSpeed. It initially costs 600.
| Rank | Move Speed
| 1 | +5%
| 2 | +10%
## From items
Move Speed's associated passive item is  Wings, which provide +10% Move Speed per level, up to a maximum of +50% Move Speed at level 5.
-  Sonic Dash [DLC] increases Move Speed by +10% per level per activation stack, up to a total of +80% MoveSpeed per stack at level 8. The MoveSpeed bonus is capped at +500%.
-  Rapidus Fio [DLC] increases Move Speed by +80% per activation stack. The MoveSpeed bonus is capped at +500%.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] has a chance of granting a Move Speed stat bonus of +8% upon activation.
-  Golden Egg can permanently increase Move Speed by +1% for the character that collected it.
## From characters
-  Lama starts with +10% MoveSpeed and gains +5% MoveSpeed every 10 levels, up to +20% at level 40. In total, she can have +30% MoveSpeed.
-  Dommario starts with -40% MoveSpeed.
-  Krochi,  Christine,  Marrabbio,  Cosmo, and  Trouser start with +30% MoveSpeed.
-  Pugnala,  Giovanna,  Poppea,  Concetta,  Zi'Assunta,  Toastie,  Smith,  Rose,  Robbert, and  She-Moon start with +20% MoveSpeed.
-  Exdash starts with -10% MoveSpeed.
-  Leda and  Scorej-Oni starts with -20% MoveSpeed.
-  Peppino starts with -100% MoveSpeed
-  Red Death starts with +100% MoveSpeed.
-  Sigma starts with +50% MoveSpeed.
-  Avatar gains 2% MoveSpeed every level, with no cap.
-  Space Dude starts with +40% MoveSpeed.
-  Gyoruntin starts with 80% MoveSpeed.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Miang,  Megalo Syuuto, and  Gav'Et-Oni ★ starts with +20% MoveSpeed.
-  Menya gains up to a total +300% MoveSpeed while in her boosted state.
-  Megalo Menya starts with +100% MoveSpeed
-  Syuuto starts with -20% MoveSpeed. He gains +40% MoveSpeed after he morphs when his  starting weapon evolves.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Eleanor and  Genevieve start with +20% MoveSpeed.
-  Keitha starts with +40% MoveSpeed.
-  Luminaire starts with +40% MoveSpeed. She gains up to a total of +300 MoveSpeed temporarily after reviving.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate starts with 20% MoveSpeed and has a chance to gain a random amount of MoveSpeed every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Engineer,  Guardian, and  Horse start with 20% MoveSpeed.
-  Shapeshifter starts with +10% MoveSpeed.
-  Impostor starts with +20% MoveSpeed and gains +70% MoveSpeed while in their boosted state.
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% MoveSpeed per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% MoveSpeed per level.
-  Megalo Impostor starts with +80% MoveSpeed.
## Operation Guns
-  Ariana starts with +20% MoveSpeed.
-  Lucia starts with +20% MoveSpeed and gains +8% MoveSpeed after reviving, with no cap.
-  Brad gains up to +10% MoveSpeed based on missing percentage of  Max Health scaling linearly.
-  Browny and  Sheena start with +40% MoveSpeed.
-  Newt starts with +50% MoveSpeed.
-  Bahamut starts with +80% MoveSpeed.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Trevor,  Juste,  Richter,  Quincy,  Henry,  Soma,  Charlotte,  Julia,  Mina,  Eric,  Sara,  Lisa,  Familiar,  Innocent Devil,  Wind,  Charlotte & Jonathan,  Stella & Loretta,  Loretta & Stella,  Stella,  Loretta,  Brauner,  Graham,  Walter,  Olrox,  Cave Troll and  Frozenshade start with +20% MoveSpeed.
-  Shaft starts with +30% MoveSpeed.
-  Sonia,  Sypha,  Carrie,  Yoko,  Maria,  Albus and  Blue Crescent Moon Cornell start with +40% MoveSpeed.
-  Elizabeth,  Alucard,  Shanoa,  Megalo Elizabeth,  Megalo Olrox and  Megalo Dracula start with +50% MoveSpeed.
-  Grant start with +60% Movespeed.
-  Maxim,  Young Maria,  Carmilla,  Succubus,  Sniper and  Malphas start with +80% MoveSpeed.
-  Saint Germain,  Fleaman,  Death and  Megalo Death start with +100% MoveSpeed.
-  Keremet starts with -100% MoveSpeed.
-  Master Librarian starts with -100% MoveSpeed and gains +2% MoveSpeed every level, with no cap.
-  Axe Armor starts with +10% Movespeed and gains +5% MoveSpeed every 10 levels, up to +20% at level 40. In total, it can have +30% MoveSpeed.
-  Joachim starts with +20% MoveSpeed and can select to gain 1% MoveSpeed per level.
-  Blackmore starts with +40% MoveSpeed and can select to gain +1% MoveSpeed per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% MoveSpeed per level.
-  Leon gains +10% MoveSpeed upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
-  Cornell gains +40% MoveSpeed after he morphs upon obtaining  Trinum Custodem.
## From stages
- Mad Forest multiply MoveSpeed by 1.1.
- Hyper Mad Forest multiply MoveSpeed by 2.
- Inlaid Library, Dairy Plant, Gallo Tower, Holy Forbidden, Il Molise, Green Acres, The Bone Zone, Boss Rash, Bat Country, and Tiny Bridge multiply MoveSpeed by 1.25.
- Hyper mode of these stages provides multiply MoveSpeed by 1.9, except Holy Forbidden and Il Molise.
- Holy Forbidden does not have hyper mode.
- Hyper Il Molise multiply MoveSpeed by 2.
- Cappella Magna multiply MoveSpeed by 1.4.
- Hyper mode multiply MoveSpeed by 2.
- Moongolow multiply MoveSpeed by 1.35.
- Hyper mode Moongolow multiply MoveSpeed by 2.
- Eudaimonia Machine multiply MoveSpeed by 1.5.
- Eudaimonia Machine does not have Hyper mode.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
- Mt.Moonspell multiply MoveSpeed by 1.4.
- Hyper mode Mt.Moonspell multiply MoveSpeed by 2.
## Tips
- Movement Speed is additive.
- If MoveSpeed modifier is below -100%, i.e. the total MoveSpeed is negative, the controls become inverted.

The Neutron Bomb is an item pickup from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. 
The Neutron Bomb creates a large circular blue explosion that starts at the center and moves outward dealing a flat 1,000 damage to all enemies hit, which is unaffected by any stats. It functions identically to a level 1 Troll Bomb.
Neutron Bombs are permanently unlocked upon collecting the King's Gate, which is dropped by defeating Abaddon in the Ode to Castlevania stage. Once unlocked, they only appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage or while playing as a character from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in any other stage.

# Orologion
Orologion
| Orologion
| Freezes all enemies in time for 10 seconds.
| Pickup
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | OROLOGION
| Rarity | 2
| Unlock level | 4
| Affected by luck | Yes
| Effects | All enemies are frozen for 10 seconds, unable to move and damage the player.
| Notes | Some enemies are resistant to being frozen. Can be "evolved" into Starry Heavens with Celestial Voulge.
| {"notes":"*Some enemies are resistant to being frozen.\n*Can be \"evolved\" into Starry Heavens with Celestial Voulge.","description":"Freezes all enemies in time for 10 seconds.","id":"OROLOGION","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Orologion","effects":"All enemies are frozen for 10 seconds, unable to move and damage the player.","release":"27 October 2021","level":"4","luck":"Yes","rarity":"2"}
Orologion is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors that has a chance to drop from light sources. It can only drop after the character reaches level 4, and its drop rate is affected by  Luck.
Collecting Orologion for the first time will unlock  Clock Lancet.
Collecting 20 Orologions will unlock the Whiteout stage.
## Effects
Picking up Orologion creates a spinning blue ring around the player, freezing all enemies. Frozen enemies become tinted blue, do not move on their own, and do no damage to the player. The player is able to push enemies out of the way by walking into them. Enemies will continue to spawn, and new enemies will also become frozen. The effect will last for 10 seconds before the ring of blue will begin to fade. Once it has completely faded, all enemies will continue to move again. Some enemies are resistant to the freeze effect.
Orologion slows down  Gold Fever's duration decay by half to counteract enemies not moving in.
 Out of Bounds (XII) will double the drop rate rarity of Orologions from 2 to 4.
## Starry Heavens
 Celestial Voulge gains a special effect that turns Orologions into Starry Heavens. This effect causes frozen enemies to move towards the player while still not dealing damage, in addition to continuously dealing damage to all enemies.
## Trivia
- Orologion appears to be a watch of some kind that can stop time. It might have gotten its name from the Italian word orologio, which means clock.
- This weapon is inspired by the stopwatch sub-weapon from the Castlevania series.
=====

| {"stage-item":"Ante Chamber","description":"Gives you gold coins, then makes an improper use of your savings.","max-level":"9","id":"LEM_ACC_SABOTEUR","stacking":"Additive","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","dlc":"ac","sprite":"Sprite-Outer Saboteur.png","max-effect":"+4,500 gold","name":"Outer Saboteur","stat":"Gold","release":"28 October 2025","rarity":"35","icon":"Icon-Outer Saboteur.png","per-level":"See table."}
Outer Saboteur is a familiar passive item from the Ante Chamber DLC of Vampire Survivors. It can be obtained automatically by obtaining any base weapon from Ante Chamber ( Infernolatro,  Gros Michel,  Fibonacci Spritz, or  Celestial Booster) or playing as any character from the DLC ( Jimbo,  Canio,  Chicot, or  Perkeo), even if sealed.
It does not take up passive item slots, which means players can obtain up to six other passive items through normal means, such as leveling up.
Outer Saboteur can be found as a stage item in Ante Chamber.
## Effects
Outer Saboteur provides 100 and spawns a floating Jimbo head that follows the player's character as a familiar which uses a special  Sossoldi, dividing the threshold to shoot higher gold value projectiles by 10 and locked with a 1-second  Cooldown.
The follower's stats scale with player's stats. 
After evolving any weapons from the Ante Chamber DLC, the floating Jimbo will cover the character's head instead of following them and is referred to as Inner Saboteur internally, except for  Chula-Reh,  Secretino Dagsson, Jimbo, Canio, Chicot, and Perkeo.
When using this item to evolve either  Infernolatro [DLC] or  Fibonacci Spritz [DLC] in co-op, all players with this item must have Outer Saboteur at max level in order to receive any evolutions. This is likely a bug.
## Levels
Outer Saboteur has 9 levels. At max level, Outer Saboteur provides a total of 4,500.
| Level 1 | Gives you gold coins, then makes an improper use of your savings.
| Level 2 | Gain 200 gold coins.
| Level 3 | Gain 300 gold coins.
| Level 4 | Gain 400 gold coins.
| Level 5 | Gain 500 gold coins.
| Level 6 | Gain 600 gold coins.
| Level 7 | Gain 700 gold coins.
| Level 8 | Gain 800 gold coins.
| Level 9 | Gain 900 gold coins.
- ↑ Gain 100 gold coins.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Infernolatro | + | Max level:Outer Saboteur | = | NaneInferno
| Gros Michel | + | Max level:Outer Saboteur | = | Cavendish
| Fibonacci Spritz | + | Max level:Outer Saboteur | = | Royal Flush
| Celestial Booster | + | Max level:Outer Saboteur | = | Negative Space
- Despite appearing to be consumed, a single Outer Saboteur can be used to evolve all four Ante Chamber weapons.
## Trivia
- This item is based on the head of Jimbo from Balatro.
- Outer Saboteur,  Silver Ring,  Gold Ring,  Metaglio Left,  Metaglio Right, and  Torrona's Box are the only passive items with max level 9 instead of the usual 5.
- The fact that it does not take up extra space in the passive item slots may be a possible reference to the Negative property from Balatro.
- If Emergency Meeting (weapon) has been upgraded before one of the Ante Chamber ones has, the Outer Saboteur will instead be shown getting ejected rather then going on the character's head.

Listed weapons aim at the nearest enemy. They gain a Cooldown bonus affected by the character's projectile Speed.
Pale Diamond Incursion (V) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by finding ten  Golden Treasure Chests created by  Zi'Appunta Belpaese's light sources after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Pale Diamond Incursion (V) is active, weapons listed on the Darkana, and their evolutions, will target the nearest enemy when firing instead of their standard behavior, drastically changing how the player approaches these weapons.
- For example,  Knife and  Thousand Edge will fire at the nearest enemy instead of in the faced direction.
Also, weapons listed on the Darkana and their evolutions gain a multiplier to their cooldown, scaling with  Speed. This multiplier acts independently of  Cooldown, allowing for weapons to fire even faster with high  Speed, much like  Tragic Princess (III). This multiplier is directly equal to Speed, where +100% Speed, or a total of x2, means weapons will fire twice as fast. This bonus will not continue after Speed is capped at +400%, or x5.
Note that counterparts created by  Gemini (I) are not affected.
This Arcana applies to the following weapons:
-  Knife,  Thousand Edge and  Million Cut [DLC]
-  Axe,  Death Spiral and  Legacy of Death: Soul River [DLC]
-  Fire Wand,  Hellfire and  Kardía Phlegeton [DLC]
-  Santa Water,  La Borra and  Hydro Pump Climax [DLC]
-  Runetracer,  NO FUTURE and  Carnage Heart [DLC]
-  Lightning Ring,  Thunder Loop and  Vjaya Sisters [DLC]
-  Peachone,  Ebony Wings and  Vandalier
-  Gatti Amari and  Vicious Hunger
-  Shadow Pinion,  Valkyrie Turner and  Darkness Illusion [DLC]
-  Vento Sacro and  Fuwalafuwaloo (which still can only attack horizontally)
-  Bone and  Anima of Mortaccio
-  Carréllo and  Carrozza!

## Tips
- The  Golden Treasure Chests required to unlock this Darkana must be from  Zi'Appunta Belpaese's exclusive light sources. Treasure Chests modified into Golden Treasure Chests by  Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) do not count, and light sources summoned by  Greatest Jubilee or certain Survarot skills cannot spawn as her exclusive ones.
- An easy way to achieve this is to play on Il Molise with  Endless Mode enabled, and consistently move while breaking the aforementioned light sources. There are plenty of builds that can make this trivial, but  Song of Mana with  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) with 2 Max Weapons is simple yet effective. Other effective stages include Moongolow and The Bone Zone, due to all three stages having incredibly high natural light source spawns.
- The player also does not need to open 10 Golden Treasure Chests; they just need to let them appear. This is useful if they player wishes to avoid the clutter or lag from having additional weapons.
- This Darkana pairs very well with  Chaos in the Dark Night (V) and  Hidden Anathema (III), as both cards provide  Speed.
- For the same reason, characters with uncapped scaling  Speed, such as  Giovanna Grana and  Zi'Assunta Belpaese can make amazing use of it.
-  Gatti Amari loses its ability to create cat fight clouds when affected by this Arcana, reducing its effectiveness, however, it also nullifies the risk of self-damage from this weapon.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Pitch Black Intrusion", which is the theme song for The Lost Village in Castlevania: Dawn of Sorrow.
- Pale Diamond Incursion (V) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "apapli", the reverse of "ilpapa" or "the pope" in Italian.
- This makes Pale Diamond Incursion (V) a representation of The Hierophant Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing unpredictability, challenging the status quo, and rebellion.
- However, its ability to change the direction of usually unpredictable projectiles in more desirable ways is closer to the Hierophant in upright position, symbolizing conformity, righteousness, preservation, and tradition.

| {"description":"Increases invulnerability time after getting damaged by 50ms.","max-level":"5","id":"PARMA","rarity":"40","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","stage-item":"The Coop, Ode to Castlevania, Emerald Diorama","sprite":"Sprite-Parm Aegis.png","max-effect":"+250ms","name":"Parm Aegis","stacking":"Additive","release":"31 October 2024","stat":"invulnerability","icon":"Icon-Parm Aegis.png","per-level":"+50ms"}
Parm Aegis is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by collecting it in any stage.
Parm Aegis increases invulnerability time by 50ms per level.
Parm Aegis can be found as a stage item on The Coop, Ode to Castlevania, and Emerald Diorama.
## Levels
Parm Aegis has 5 levels. At max level, provides +250ms of Invunerability Time.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | Increases invulnerability time after getting damaged by 50ms.
| Level 2 | Increases invulnerability time after getting damaged by 50ms.
| Level 3 | Increases invulnerability time after getting damaged by 50ms.
| Level 4 | Increases invulnerability time after getting damaged by 50ms.
| Level 5 | Increases invulnerability time after getting damaged by 50ms.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Discus | + | Max level:Parm Aegis | = | Stellar Blade
| Iron Shield | + | Parm Aegis | = | Dark Iron Shield
| Gaze of Gaea | + | Parm Aegis | = | Embrace of Gaea
| Khukuri | + | Parm Aegis+Moonfall Slash tech | = | Jetstream
## Trivia
- The Parm Aegis appears similar to a buckler, a small circular shield commonly used during the Medieval and Renaissance eras.
- The aegis is a small shield, carried by Athena and Zeus in Greek mythology, thus its ability increases the invulnerability duration.
- It was made available for the base game alongside the release of the Ode to Castlevania DLC, and later made fully unlockable by finding it in The Coop.
- While the unlocks menu states that it must be found in The Coop, it simply needs to be obtained once to unlock, much like  Arma Dio.
=====
# Passive items
Spinach is a passive item that slightly increases damage dealt by the player.
Passive Items (also referred to as Passive Weapons) are non-weapon items that give the player bonuses to their stats, similar to PowerUps. They are offered to the player alongside weapons whenever the player levels up.
The player has 6 slots for passive items, meaning that they can select up to 6 non-gift passive items from level ups. Though Mini Crewmates take up a passive item slot, they can be selected after reaching the passive limit.
It is possible however to carry more passive items after 6 have already been obtained. This can be done through picking up the passives that generate with the stage, through [A sprite icon representing Treasure Chest 4 with no link] dark chests spawned by  Moonlight Bolero (VI) or  Call of a Mad Moon (XIII) , or from an  Arma Dio spawned by  Hail from the Future (X). Most passive items must be unlocked before they can be offered to the player. 
The Survarot of Zi'Assunta Belpaese will provide +1 passive weapon slot after gaining a set amount of gold.  Congregation [DLC] gains +1 passive weapon slot for every +1   Revival they possess beyond one. Perkeo Perkeo [DLC] has 100 slots for Passive Weapons. 
Most passive items are unique, which means a character can only get them once per run, and there is no way to get the same passive item multiple times (even if it is consumed, like Tirajisú). Exceptions include  Weapon Power-Up [DLC] and Familiars from  Familiar Forge [DLC].
Most passive items can be used to evolve a particular normal weapon into a stronger one. 
See Combos page for a list of which Weapon and Arcana is affected by stats and their corresponding passive items, PowerUps, and Arcana. 
## Types
The base game has 21 passive items, 17 of which are normal passive items and 4 of which are special passive items. In addition, there are 13 passive items that are only available in DLCs: 1 from the Tides of the Foscari DLC, 8 from the Emergency Meeting DLC, 1 from the Operation Guns DLC, 2 from the Ode to Castlevania DLC, and 1 from the Ante Chamber DLC, bringing the total number of passive items in the game to 34.
There are certain passive items in the game referred to as "special passive items", which cannot appear normally from the level-up choices and must be collected from a stage or by fulfilling set conditions in order to obtain them. These special passives include the passive items unlocked after collecting the Yellow Sign and most of DLC-exclusive passive items.

## Mini Crewmates
The Emergency Meeting DLC introduces a group of new passive items known as mini crewmates. Each of them are exclusive to a certain Emergency Meeting DLC weapon, and only occur in item pool once matching weapon reaches certain level. Instead of stat bonus, they provide a hidden weapon and a pet that follows the character. All of evolutions from this DLC needs maxing out both weapon and the exclusive mini crewmate, and the mini crewmate will be consumed in evolution (the pet will be kept).

## Weapon Power-Up
The Operation Guns DLC introduces a new passive items named Weapon Power-Up. It will only occur in item pool after taking any Operation Guns DLC weapon, and evolving each of these weapon needs to consume one Weapon Power-Up. Weapon Power-Up will occur in item pool again after previous one being consumed.
When picked, Weapon Power-Up upgrades a random weapon and gives +1 revival to the character. It won't be counted by inventory in the passive item limit and can be acquired even if the character has reached the passive item limit.

Isn't counted by inventory in the passive item limit. Required by all Operation Guns weapons to evolve, which will consume it. | Becomes available in a run after obtaining any Operation Guns weapons. | 40
## Familiars
The Ode to Castlevania DLC introduces real, AI-controlled familiars (secondary characters) to the game. They can be obtained by taking a  Familiar Forge or via certain characters' passive features. Familiars won't be counted by inventory slot in the passive item limit, and will level up along with the character. Each Familiar has its own starting weapons, its own leveling up options (which are limited and randomly picked), and won't take damage. A character can get multiple copies of the same Familiar.

| | Imp | Trident (level 7 Max) | Wings | Stays close to enemies.
| | Alleged Ghost | Summon Spirit (level 7 Max) | Wings | Moves opposite to the character's movement and distract enemies.
| | Wood Rod | Fire Wand, Lightning Ring, and Magic Wand (all level 2 Max) | Wings | Follows the character's moving path.
| | Faerie | Refectio (level 3 Max) | Wings, Pummarola | Stays close to the character.
| | Pumpkin | Curved Knife (level 7 Max), Troll Bomb (level 1 Max) | Wings | Runs away from the character and distract enemies.
| | Sacred Cardinal | Raging Fire (level 3 Max) | Wings | Stays close to the character.
| | Sacred Dragon | Fulgur (level 3 Max) | Wings | Stays close to the character.

## Stage items
An Empty Tome lying on a stool in Inlaid Library.
On each normal stage, certain passive items spawn on the map. Milky Way Map displays the locations of these items. Each item is guaranteed to appear at least once, but certain items have a chance of spawning more than once, depending on the starting Luck.
Stages spawn the following items: 
- Mad Forest: Spinach, Hollow Heart, Pummarola, Clover, Skull O'Maniac.

- Up to 3 more Clovers (30%, 20%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Inlaid Library: Stone Mask, Empty Tome.
- Up to 3 more Empty Tomes (40%, 30%, 20%) may be spawned.
- Dairy Plant: Attractorb, Candelabrador, Wings, Armor.
- Gallo Tower: Bracer, Spellbinder.
- Cappella Magna: Duplicator, Crown, Tirajisú.
- Moongolow and Boss Rash: All normal passives except Torrona's Box.
- Whiteout: Wings, Spinach, and Skull O'Maniac
- Space 54: 5 Empty Tomes and 5 Candelabradors
- Laborratory: Spellbinder, Attractorb, and Clover
- Chance to get one of the items by flicking levers found on rooms in the stage.
- Bat Country: 9 rotating Gold Rings.
- Mazerella: Armor, Attractorb, Candelabrador, and Wings.
- Up to 4 more Armor may be spawned.
- Up to 4 more Attractorb may be spawn.
- Up to 4 more Candelabrado may be spawn.
- Up to 4 more Wings may be spawn.
- Tiny Bridge: Wings and Attractorb,
- Mt.Moonspell [DLC]: Spinach, Pummarola, Candelabrador, Duplicator, Attractorb, and Stone Mask
- Up to 2 more Spinach (10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Up to 2 more Pummarola (10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Up to 2 more Candelabrador (10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Lake Foscari [DLC]: Armor, Bracer, Clover, Crown, Skull O'Maniac, and Academy Badge [DLC]
- Up to 4 more Armor (10%, 10%, 10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Up to 4 more Bracer (10%, 10%, 10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Up to 4 more Clover (10%, 10%, 10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Up to 4 more Crown (10%, 10%, 10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Up to 4 more Skull O'Maniac (10%, 10%, 10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Abyss Foscari [DLC]: Armor, Empty Tome, Attractorb, Crown, Stone Mask, Skull O'Maniac, Torrona's Box, and Academy Badge [DLC]
- Up to 4 more Empty Tome (10%, 10%, 10%, 10%) may be spawned.
- Up to 5 more Attractorb (20%, 20%, 20%, 20%, 20%) may be spawned.
- Up to 4 more Crown (20%, 20%, 20%, 20%) may be spawned.
- Up to 3 more Stone Mask (20%, 20%, 20%) may be spawned.

- Ode to Castlevania [DLC]: Tirajisú
- Parm Aegis and Karoma's Mana begin spawning after obtaining the Stallion Gate [DLC] and Beast Gate [DLC] respectively.
Upon moving to an item, it can be picked up or discarded. If the player already has the item, it grants a free level if not at max level. If they already have 6 or more passive items, it becomes an additional passive item and can be thereafter upgraded from level ups. If discarded, the item disappears and can no longer be picked up. If the item was already Banished, either from the Collection menu or during the run, picking it up will show a third "unbanish" option. This picks up the item and allows it to be levelled again as normal.
## Hidden stage items
After obtaining the Yellow Sign, four additional passive items will spawn in all normal, bonus and challenge stages. These passive items cannot be obtained through any other means than picking them up during a run. The first time entering a stage after getting Yellow Sign, the game will automatically pause to show the new hidden stage items that were added:
- Silver Ring, usually found due north of the starting area.
- Gold Ring, usually found due south of the starting area.
- Metaglio Left, usually found due west of the starting area.
- Metaglio Right, usually found due east of the starting area.
Each hidden stage item is guarded by an Atlantean that spawns when any player gets close to the item. The player does not have to defeat the guard to pick up the item, but they will continue to follow the player until defeated. If the stage item was attracted using Mad Groove (VIII), the guard will not appear until the item is interacted with.
- By approaching near the item without picking it up (prior to obtaining Mad Groove (VIII)), If it is later picked up from Mad Groove, a second Atlantean can be spawned for each hidden stage item.
## Tips
- It is wise to devote the majority of your passive item slots (at least 4) with stats that benefits weapons like Might, Area, Projectile Speed, Duration, Cooldown, and Amount. Save items that you only get for evolutions like Armor, Wings, Stone Mask, etc. for after you already have a clear direction of your build.
- If the passive item you need for your build is already a stage item, take it after you have filled your passive item slots to get the most benefit out of all of them.
- However, there are situations where a stage item is too far from your initial location and selecting it early from level-up choices would be a better decision. This is the case for items where it is needed to evolve a weapon, or for items that are necessary to save your current run from an imminent game-over.
- It can be helpful to Banish all stage items and weapons in the Collection menu before a run. This prevents those weapons and items from being drawn instead of others you may want. You can still get them by picking them up in the stage and selecting the "unbanish" option to pick it up and allow you to level it again as normal.
## Trivia
- In the game files for Abyss Foscari, there are six Attractorb that can spawn, five of which are at 20%, but since the player can only have five levels, the sixth is simply removed at the start of the stage. This appears to be an oversight as there are only three extra Stone Masks are programmed for four spawns, meaning the extra Attractorb is taking up a Stone Mask slot.
## External links
- Build Planner

| {"stage-item":"","description":"","max-level":"6","id":"","stacking":"","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","dlc":"lm","sprite":"Sprite-Pearl magatama.png","max-effect":"","name":"Pearl magatama","stat":"","release":"28 August 2026","rarity":"","icon":"Icon-Pearl magatama.png","per-level":""}
Pearl magatama is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is the starting passive item of  Gekkojin. It is unlocked by starting a run as Gekkojin. 
As a gift, this item does not take up a passive item slot, once unlocked, it will occur as a level up option even if the player has reached the passive limit.
After being unlocked, Pearl magatama can be found as a stage item in the northwest corner of Mt. Moonspell. Approaching the Pearl magatama will spawn a  Goshadokuro to defend it.
## Levels
Pearl magatama has 6 levels. At max level, Pearl magatama provides +1.5  Recovery and +2  Revival, but -35%  Greed. By the time Pearl magatama is max level, the player will also be made immune to Health Drain and have fully healed 6 times.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | Gift. Will provide bonus Recovery and Revivals in exchange for Greed. Fully heals.
| Level 2 | Greed is reduced by 7%. Health recovery increases by 0.5 HP per second. Fully heals.
| Level 3 | Greed is reduced by 7%. Adds 1 Revival. Fully heals.
| Level 4 | Greed is reduced by 7%. Health recovery increases by 0.5 HP per second. Fully heals.
| Level 5 | Greed is reduced by 7%. Adds 1 Revival. Fully heals.
| Level 6 | Greed is reduced by 7%. Health recovery increases by 0.5 HP per second. Fully heals. Makes the player immune to Health Drain.
- ↑ Not shown in-game.
Pearl magatama is not used to evolve any weapons, but it is required to morph  Miang Moonspell into  Megalo Miang Moonspell.
## Trivia
- Magatama are curved beads found in Japan, used in prehistoric times as jewelry.
- The appearance of the inventory sprite is probably a nod to Ace Attorney series, particularly Phoenix Wright: Ace Attorney – Justice for All, which also introduced the child medium character Pearl Fey.

# Pickups
Pickups are collectibles in Vampire Survivors dropped by destroying light sources or by defeating enemies.
The Magnet stat affects the radius pickups can be gathered from.
Most pickups' drop rate increases with Luck.
## List of Pickups
Note that DLC-exclusive pickups only appear when playing on specific maps or as specific characters.
Rarity is the relative weight of the item in the light source loot pool. When rarity is 0, this means it has no chance of dropping.
Unlock level is the level at which the item becomes available as a light source drop. The unlock level is ignored when rarity is 0, even if the player were to hit the item's given unlock level.

## Trivia
- As per the nature of this game, a lot of items reference the Castlevania series:
- Gold and Coin Bag is inspired by the Gold.
- Rosary is inspired by the Rosary item.
- Floor Chicken is inspired by the Food from Castlevania: Dracula X.
- Orologion is inspired by the Stopwatch sub-weapon.
- Picking up Floor Chicken and its variants provide a brief period of invulnerability. This can be chained when farming Sammys to provide minutes of invulnerability.
- The Big Coin Bag, just like the Coin Bag and Rich Coin Bag, had an old sprite.
- Despite having an "Unlock Level", items with rarity 0 do not drop from light sources even if said level is reached.

Pile of Secrets is a relic from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors, which grants the ability to unlock the DLC's new secrets. After obtaining the Pile of Secrets, the music track played on the Secrets menu is changed from Temptation of the Occult to Mysterious Curse until the player unlocks all remaining Ode to Castlevania characters.
- Note that this relic does not unlock the Secrets menu. The player must obtain  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane in order to view this menu.
## Obtaining
Pile of Secrets can be obtained by defeating  Beelzebub [DLC] in Ode to Castlevania to the farthest northwest of Dracula's Castle, which only appears after obtaining the  Black Disk [DLC]. However, the door to Beelzebub's boss fight can only be opened after obtaining all of the gate keys, including  Serpent Gate [DLC] and  Beast Gate [DLC].
## Trivia
The Dracula quote where this item gets its name. 
- The name of this relic is taken from a quote said by Dracula during the intro of Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Pile of Secrets appears identical to the sprite for Peanuts from Castlevania: Symphony of the Night as it references the obscure method of consuming it. Alucard needs to toss them up and then must catch them in his mouth by pressing ↑ when the peanut is about to land on Alucard's head before it hits the floor. The arc of each peanut varies randomly, requiring some positioning for each one.

# PowerUps
Power Ups
Reroll can only be obtained as a PowerUp. It can be used several times in a game to get a new set of level up options or Arcanas.
PowerUps are purchasable permanent improvements to stats that apply to all characters. They can be bought with Gold Coins in the PowerUp Selection menu. PowerUps with a golden background do not have an associated passive item in the game.
After maxing out a PowerUp (except Rerolls, Skips, Banishes and Seals), the player can then disable the PowerUp, preventing the need to refund all PowerUps to test out PowerUp setups or to get rid of Curse.
## Interface
PowerUps menu, with all of the PowerUps shown here being maxed out.
The interface of the menu consists three parts:
- A grid of squares, with each square showing the stat name on the top, the icon at the center, and a series of checkboxes depicting acquired upgrades. By default, a square has a gray background if a PowerUp is not fully upgraded. If it is fully upgraded and active, it will have a yellowish background instead. If it is fully upgraded and not active, then the background will be dark gray.
- The upper part of the menu, which consists of the menu's title and a blue button that allows for the player to refund their PowerUps.
- The lower part of the menu, which shows more information about the selected stat. If a PowerUp is not bought or fully upgraded, then there will be an option to buy or upgrade it. If it is fully upgraded, then there will be an option for the player to activate a PowerUp in most cases, except for  Reroll,  Skip,  Banish, and  Seal. The background colors of this part of the menu is shared with those of the middle part of the menu.
Outside of the menu, at the top of the screen, there is the gold counter and a back button found next to it, which leads back to the main menu.
## Types
There are 27 different PowerUps in the game (including the separate seal powerups), affecting the various player stats.

## Cost
This article has a calculator here.
Calculators determine dynamic values based on user input and wiki data.
For the formulas in this section please use the following key: 
Formula Terms
| Term | Description
| Price | Final price of a specific PowerUp rank.
| InitialPrice | Price of desired PowerUp when no PowerUp ranks of any type have been bought. This can be found by refunding all PowerUps and then immediately checking the price (or by checking the table above)
| Bought | Number of ranks already purchased of the specific PowerUp whose Price is being calculated.
| TotalBought | Total number of ranks already purchased of all PowerUps including the one whose Price is being calculated
| BaseCost | The price of a rank without accounting for fees.
| Fees | A value calculated based on the "TotalBought" value. More details below.
## Price
The price of a PowerUp rank is calculated by this formula: 
Price=BaseCost+Fees
## Base Cost
The BaseCost portion of a PowerUp rank price is calculated by this formula:
BaseCost=InitialPrice⋅(1+Bought)
Essentially, every time a rank is bought, the base cost of the next rank of the same PowerUp increases by the initial price. For example, if a PowerUp has an InitialPrice of 200, the base cost of 5 ranks will be 200+400+600+800+1000=3000 (not accounting for fees).
## Fees
The fees portion of a PowerUp rank price is calculated by this formula:
Fees={0,if TotalBought=0⌊20⋅1.1TotalBought⌋,if TotalBought≥1
This basically means that when no ranks of any type have been bought (TotalBought = 0), fees are not applied at all. Once any PowerUp rank is bought, the fees for every rank of all types going forward will be 20*1.1TotalBoughtrounded down to an integer (thats what the ⌊ & ⌋ floor operations are)
## Putting it all together
So the final formula for the Price of a specific rank is the following:
Price={InitialPrice,if TotalBought=0InitialPrice⋅(1+Bought)+⌊20⋅1.1TotalBought⌋,if TotalBought≥1
## Additional Info
In older versions, the order in which the player would buy PowerUp ranks used to matter because the fees were multiplicative. However, as of update v0.7.2 (9 June 2022), the fees are additive, which means the order no longer matters.
The total cost of every PowerUp rank is 27,148,513, with 2,469,640 of that being from the Base costs and 24,678,873 being from the fees.
By pressing the "Refund PowerUps" button, the player will lose all of their PowerUp ranks and get their gold back. The gold returned to them will be deducted by a negligible amount (most likely due to a rounding error).
## External links
- Vampire Survivors PowerUp Calculator (Note: Outdated; currently only updated to game version v0.11.107)

# Preserve
Preserve is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It provides a chance to retain uses of  Reroll,  Skip, and  Banish.
## Effects
Preserve has a chance to keep a Reroll, Skip, and Banish action after being used, allowing more uses of them in a run overall. 
## PowerUp
| Preserve
| Reroll, Skip, and Banish actions have a 10% chance to be preserved per rank (max 50%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 28 October 2025 (Update)
| ID | RECYCLE
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +10%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +50%
| Initial cost | 500
| {"description":"Reroll, Skip, and Banish actions have a 10% chance to be preserved per rank (max 50%).","cost":"500","id":"RECYCLE","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","image":"","name":"Preserve","release":"28 October 2025","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+10%"}
The Preserve PowerUp increases the chance of preserving Reroll, Skip, and Banish by 10% per rank, with a maximum rank of 5. At rank 5, there is a 50% chance to preserve them. It initially costs 500, and is unlocked by collecting  Wax Fetish in Mazerella.
| Level | Preserve
| 1 | 10%
| 2 | 20%
| 3 | 30%
| 4 | 40%
| 5 | 50%
## Tips
- Similar to  Cooldown, while the effect of each rank is additive, the result is exponential as the chance of a recycled action getting recycled again increases with each rank. If getting Preserve to 100% were possible, it would grant infinite uses of  Reroll,  Skip, and  Banish as long as there is one to start with.
## Trivia
- Preserve,  Charm,  Fever,  Defang, Invulnerability Time, and Thorns are the only character stats in the game that are not displayed on the stat panel.
=====

Pummarola

| {"description":"Character recovers 0.2 HP per second.","max-level":"5","id":"REGEN","stage-item":"Mad Forest, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Mt.Moonspell","update":"Patch 0.2.5: new stage and achievements","stacking":"Additive","sprite":"Sprite-Pummarola.png","max-effect":"+1.0","name":"Pummarola","stat":"Recovery","release":"2 January 2022","rarity":"90","icon":"Icon-Pummarola.png","per-level":"+0.2"}
Pummarola is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by surviving 5 minutes as  Gennaro Belpaese.
Pummarola increases  Recovery by 0.2 per level.
It can be found as a stage item in Mad Forest, Moongolow, Boss Rash, and Mt.Moonspell.
## Levels
Pummarola has 5 levels. At max level, Pummarola provides +1  Recovery.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Character recovers 0.2 HP per second."
| Level 2 | Health recovery increases by 0.2 HP per second.
| Level 3 | Health recovery increases by 0.2 HP per second.
| Level 4 | Health recovery increases by 0.2 HP per second.
| Level 5 | Health recovery increases by 0.2 HP per second.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Garlic | + | Pummarola | = | Soul Eater
| Silver Wind | + | Max level:Pummarola | = | Festive Winds
| Star Flail | + | Max level:Pummarola | = | Moon Rod
| Guardian's Targe | + | Pummarola | = | Sacred Beasts Tower Shield
| Kick | + | Pummarola+Thunder Kick tech | = | Triangle Kick
| Scarlet Needle | + | Max level:Pummarola | = | Cardinal Rain
## Tips
- Pummarola benefits the character. It only benefits Soul Eater where health healed increases the base damage of the weapon.
- Anything that makes the player take less damage will make the Pummarola more effective, such as bonuses to  Armor.
## Trivia
- "Pummarola" means "tomato sauce" in the Neapolitan dialect.
- Pummarola is a heart, most commonly used in video game as an indicator of health related items. However, unlike most other things in this game, Hearts in the Castlevania series are very different. It is more akin to ammunition for various sub-weapons that uses a physical weapon and is sometimes separated from mana and spell casting.

# Randomazzo
| Randomazzo
| Enables the unlocking and the activation of Arcanas. Enables the unlocking and the activation of Arcanas.
| Relic
| Release | 12 April 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_RANDOMAZZO
| Effects | Unlocks Arcanas.
| Location | Due north of Gallo Tower.
| {"description":"Enables the unlocking and the activation of Arcanas.","id":"RELIC_RANDOMAZZO","update":"Patch 0.5.0","effects":"Unlocks Arcanas.","sprite":"Sprite-Randomazzo.png","name":"Randomazzo","release":"12 April 2022","icon":"Icon-Randomazzo.png","location":"Due north of Gallo Tower."}
The Randomazzo is a relic located in Gallo Tower. Once collected, it permanently enables the Arcanas system and awards  Sarabande of Healing (VI).
## Obtaining
The Randomazzo is located to the far north of the starting area in Gallo Tower. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. It is guarded by enemies that fire red circular projectiles and slowing beams that rotate clockwise similar to the  Clock Lancet. Once the Randomazzo has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, the Randomazzo can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane, by casting the spell "randomazzami".
## Notes
- In the items panel at the bottom of the Randomazzo, currently obtained items are indicated through a yellow circle and are positioned before unobtained weapons/items.
## Trivia
- The Randomazzo is a portmanteau of "Random" and "Mazzo" (mah-tso), which is an Italian term for deck.

| {"notes":"* Can only appear when playing on Neo Galuga or Hectic Highway, or when playing as a character from the Operation Guns DLC.\n* Not affected by stats.","description":"Temporarily gives +30% Speed and -30% Cooldown.","id":"FB_RAPIDFIRE","update":"","dlc":"og","image":"","name":"Rapid Fire","effects":"Gives +30% Speed and -30% Cooldown for 10 seconds. Rapid Fire is one of the pickups that has a chance to drop from light sources. Its drop rate is affected by  Luck. 
Rapid Fire can only appear when playing in Neo Galuga or Hectic Highway, or as a character from the Operation Guns DLC.  Simondo Belmont is also able to spawn this pickup without the need for light sources.
## Effects
Rapid Fire gives +30%  Speed and -30%  Cooldown for 10 real-time seconds (not in-game time). Speed has no upper limit. Cooldown is limited to -90%.
## Tips
- The Cooldown reduction is an incredibly powerful effect. Due to Rapid Fire lasting 10 seconds, it is important to not immediately pick up Rapid Fire if the player is able to save it to maximize the uptime on the stat benefits.
=====
# Recovery
Recovery
Recovery is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It determines the amount of health that is regenerated per second.
The base value of Recovery is 0, which means that health does not regenerate without any Recovery bonuses. Recovery also boosts healing from pickups, each 0.1 translates to an additive 10% boost. It does not always increase healing from weapons. Bonuses to Recovery are flat bonuses and thus stack additively.
There is no upper limit to how much Recovery the player can have.
Recovery also affects:
- Base damage of  Sharp Tongue [DLC],  Silver Tongue [DLC], and  Impostongue [DLC]
- Base damage of  Star Flail [DLC] and  Moon Rod [DLC]
- Healing effect of  Lifesign Scan [DLC] and  Paranormal Scan [DLC]
- Healing effect of  Refectio [DLC] and  Sanctuary [DLC]
## PowerUp
| Recovery
| Recovers 0.1 HP per rank (max 0.5) per second.
| PowerUp
| Release | 2 January 2022 (Update)
| ID | REGEN
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +0.1
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +0.5
| Initial cost | 200
| {"description":"Recovers 0.1 HP per rank (max 0.5) per second.","cost":"200","id":"REGEN","update":"Patch 0.2.5: new stage and achievements","image":"","name":"Recovery","release":"2 January 2022","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+0.1"}
The Recovery PowerUp increases Recovery by 0.1 per rank, with a maximum rank of 5. At rank 5, it provides +0.5 Recovery. It initially costs 200.
| Rank | Recovery
| 1 | +0.1
| 2 | +0.2
| 3 | +0.3
| 4 | +0.4
| 5 | +0.5
## From items
Recovery's associated passive item is the  Pummarola, which provides +0.2 Recovery per level, up to a maximum of +1 Recovery at level 5.
 Metaglio Left also provides +0.1 Recovery per level, up to a maximum of +0.8 Recovery at level 9.
 Pearl magatama [DLC] provides +0.5 recovery every other level, up to a maximum of +1.5 recovery at level 6.
 Centralis Custos [DLC] provides +2 Recovery when the player character takes damage from enemies.
 Trinum Custodem [DLC] provides a passive +2 Recovery and an additional +2 Recovery when the player character takes damage from enemies.
 Golden Egg permanently increases Recovery by 0.1 for the character that collected it.
## From darkana
 Crystal Cries (XII) generates crystals when killing frozen enemies that can be collected to slightly increase  Max Health, Recovery, and  Growth.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Clerici,  Minnah, and  Robbert start with +0.5 Recovery.
-  Divano starts with +1 Recovery.
-  Smith gains +0.01 Recovery every level, with no cap.
-  Cosmo starts with +1 Recovery and gains +1 Recovery every level, with no cap.
-  Sigma starts with +3 Recovery.
-  Retired Poe Ratcho starts with +5 Recovery and a Level 5  Pummarola which provides an additional +1 Recovery.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Miang starts with +1 Recovery.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Luminaire starts with +0.5 Recovery.
-  Sammy starts with +2 Recovery.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate has a chance to gain a random amount of Recovery every time a weapon or passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Shapeshifter starts with +1 Recovery.
-  Guardian starts with +0.1 Recovery and provides +0.1 Recovery to nearby characters.
-  Scientist can select to gain +0.01 Recovery per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +0.05 Recovery per level.
## Operation Guns
-  Lucia starts with +1 Recovery and gains +0.1 Recovery after reviving, with no cap.
-  Sheena starts with +1 Recovery.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Mina starts with +0.5 Recovery.
-  Wind starts with +1 Recovery.
-  Keremet starts with +2 Recovery.
-  Julia starts with +3 Recovery.
-  Joachim can select to gain +0.01 Recovery per level.
-  Blackmore can select to gain +0.01 Recovery per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +0.05 Recovery per level.
-  Sara gains +0.02 Recovery every level, without cap.
-  Leon gains +0.1 Recovery upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
## Tips
- Recovery is additive and not multiplicative. The highest possible Recovery is attainable by playing as Cosmo, with rank 5 Recovery PowerUp (+0.5), level 5  Pummarola (+1), and level 9  Metaglio Left (+0.8), combining to give the character (player level+3.3) health regenerated every second. It can be doubled with  Sarabande of Healing (VI) to (2 x player level + 6.6) every second.
- The small amount of Recovery may look weak, but it can be very useful for healing off small bits of damage. Anything that makes the player take less damage will thus make the Recovery more effective, such as bonuses to  Armor.

Red Moon Manor Map is a relic from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors. It is located in Red Moon Manor. Once collected, it displays a map of Red Moon Manor.
## Obtaining
Red Moon Manor Map is found by collecting it in Red Moon Manor. Once Red Moon Manor Map has been collected, it will not appear again.
Alternatively, the Red Moon Manor Map can be unlocked in  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "liecontrosole".

# Relics
Relics
Relics are permanent items that unlock new mechanics and features once collected. They are very similar to stage items as they are simply spawned on the map and are collected upon touching them. Once collected, they are permanently added to the collection menu and do not appear again. Until picked up, their locations are indicated by green arrows once the player enters a stage that contains them.

# Reroll
Reroll
Reroll is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects the amount of times the player can reroll the rewards when leveling up or selecting an Arcana.
The items that were discarded by a reroll cannot appear in the new set of items. However, this does not completely remove them from the pool, and they can be offered again in the same level up if more rerolls are used. Arcanas that are rerolled, on the other hand, are removed from the pool and do not appear again in the same chest.
There is no upper limit to how many Rerolls the player may have.
## PowerUp
| Reroll
| Twice per rank, allows you to get different choices when leveling up.
| PowerUp
| Release | 13 February 2022 (Update)
| ID | REROLL
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +2
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +10
| Initial cost | 1,000
| {"description":"Twice per rank, allows you to get different choices when leveling up.","cost":"1000","id":"REROLL","update":"Patch 0.2.12 - small update","image":"","name":"Reroll","release":"13 February 2022","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+2"}
The Reroll PowerUp increases Reroll by 2 per rank, with a maximum rank of 5 after unlocking all Reroll achievements. With one rank unlocked it initially costs 1,000.
| Rank | Reroll
| 1 | +2
| 2 | +4
| 3 | +6
| 4 | +8
| 5 | +10
Each rank of PowerUp is unlocked by these achievements:
- Reaching level 80 with  Mortaccio
- Reaching level 80 with  Yatta Cavallo
- Reaching level 80 with  Bianca Ramba
- Reaching level 80 with  O'Sole Meeo
- Reaching level 80 with  Sir Ambrojoe
## From items
Reroll does not have any associated passive item.
The  Rerollo grants 1 Reroll for the character that collected it.
The  Golden Egg permanently increases Reroll by 0.1 for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
 Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) provides 3 Reroll.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Gallo starts with +3 Reroll.
-  Avatar starts with +10 Reroll.
-  Sigma starts with +108 Reroll.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Babi-Onna starts with +10 Reroll.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Eleanor starts with +2 Reroll.
-  Genevieve starts with +5 Reroll.
-  Je-Ne-Viv starts with +7 Reroll.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Engineer starts with +20 Reroll.

-  Lucia and  Sheena start with +8 Reroll.

# Revival
Revival
Revival is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. Each point of Revival a player has allows them to revive once upon death with 50% of their  Max Health.
The player has 0 base Revivals before modifications. Bonuses to Revival are flat bonuses and thus stack additively.
There is no upper limit to how many Revivals the player may have.
## Effects
If the player dies and has at least 1 Revival, instead of getting the usual "Quit" button to return to the main menu, they will instead be greeted with a "Revive" button. An animation is played and the player resumes control of their character exactly where they left off, with 50% of their  Max Health also being restored. The player is additionally given a large amount of invulnerability frames. The health gained upon reviving counts as healing, causing it to interact with effects related to restoring health, such as  Soul Eater and  Sarabande of Healing (VI).
If Revivals are carried to the end of the game, the player will still be allowed to Revive after being killed by  The Reaper. Every Revival used like this will add gold to the bonus gold for completing the stage, at +100 gold per revival, increasing by 100 for every additional revival used. For example, if the player has 3 revivals and use them all on The Reaper, they will die and get the normal 500 bonus gold, then revive and die and get an extra 100, then an extra 200, then an extra 300, and end the round with 1100 bonus gold. The scaling gold increase caps at 400 and any additional unused Revivals will only give 100 bonus gold.
Revival also affects: 
- Base Damage and Amount of  Phieraggi
- Base Damage of  Meal Ticket [DLC]
## On Revival
Main article: On Revival
On Revival or On Revive is a mechanic in Vampire Survivors where a specific character or item triggers a special skill or mechanic immediately after being revived. For example,  Malice Bloodmoon triggers a Blood Hex on revival.
## PowerUp

| Revives once with 50% health.

The Revival PowerUp increases Revival by 1, and only has one rank. It initially costs 10,000. 
| Rank | Revival
| 1 | +1
## From items
- Revival's associated passive item is  Tirajisú, which provides 1 additional revival per level, up to a maximum of 2 at level 2.
-  Golden Eggs can permanently increase Revival by 0.1 for the character that collected it.
- Survarot cards featuring  Pasqualina Belpaese provide +1 Revival upon selection. The card's edition-exclusive skill (or any edition card as a random bonus) can grant up to +3 Revivals after reaching certain kill thresholds.
-  Academy Badge [DLC] has 6 levels and provides +1 Revival at levels 3 and 5, for a maximum of +2 Revival.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] may provide 0.2 Revival whenever it activates, requiring 5 such bonuses to give +1 Revival, with no cap.
-  Weapon Power-Up [DLC] provides +1 Revival on pickup.
## From arcana
-  Awake (IV) provides +3 Revivals when chosen and gives +10%  Max Health, +1  Armor, and +5%  Might,  Area,  Duration, and  Speed per Revival consumed.
-  Hidden Anathema (III) may provide additional Revivals when obtaining food-based Pickups, with no cap.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Krochi Freetto starts with +1 Revival and gains +1 Revival at level 33, for a total of +2 Revivals.
-  Cosmo Pavone starts with +1 Revival and gains +1 Revival every 100 levels, with no cap.
-  Gyorunton and  Bats Bats Bats both start with +2 Revivals.
-  Queen Sigma and  Avatar Infernas both start with +1 Revival.
-  Gazebo may gain additional Revivals when Overhealing, up to a cap of +3 Revivals.
-  Reborn She-Moon Eeta starts with +7 Revivals.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Luminaire Foscari starts with +7 Revivals.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Crewmate Dino has a chance to gain a random amount of Revivals every time a passive item is upgraded to its maximum level, with no cap.
-  Guardian Pina starts with +1 Revival, and has a chance to gain +1 Revival when anyone, including Pina themself, revives.
## Operation Guns
-  Lucia Zero starts with +1 Revival.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Leon,  Christopher,  Simon,  Julius,  Shaft,  Master Librarian,  Soleil and  Galamoth all start with +1 Revival.
-  Leon also gains +1 Revival upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer, for a total of +2 Revivals.
-  Quincy Morris starts with +4 Revivals.
-  Vlad Tepes Dracula,  Megalo Dracula, and  Dark Lord Soma Cruz gain +1 Revival every 100 levels, with no cap.
-  Nathan Graves and  Chaos both start with  Awake (IV) and thus start with +3 Revivals.
## Emerald Diorama
-  Dolores starts with +1 Revival.
-  Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr start with +8 Revivals.
-  Kugutsu gain +1 Revival and +5%  Growth every time they Glimmer a new tech, up to a maximum of +35 Revivals (and +175% Growth) should they collect and Glimmer all compatible weaponry.
## Tips
- Revivals are a powerful tool that can save runs. While they provide no benefit until you die (except for Phieraggi), they can save the player from sloppy mistakes and being suddenly overwhelmed.
- Phieraggi benefits from this stat by getting +X Base Damage and +X Amount equal to the value of this stat. Be sure not to use up revives to achive that weapons maximum effectiveness.
- Revivals are especially useful when playing as characters with less survivability, such as Exdash and Toastie.
- Revival stat is additive. The maximum amount of Revivals a player can have can be obtained by getting the PowerUp (+1), Level 2 Tirajisú (+2), playing as Krochi or Gyorunton (+2), and taking the Awake (IV) Arcana (+3), netting +8 extra lives. If these are taken to the end of the round and exchanged with The Reaper for extra gold, they will be worth +1900 bonus gold altogether.
- Revivals can be especially useful for runs where the goal is to survive as long as possible against The Reaper, and can greatly extend runs when paired with other defensive measures.
- Revivals can be useful for gathering loot otherwise unobtainable before The Reaper ends the run. Even 1 revival can allow the player to get a few more levels or one extra Treasure Chest.

# Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle
| Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle
| Permanently allows you to speed up gameplay. Permanently allows you to speed up gameplay.
| Relic
| Release | 10 April 2025 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_SPEEDUP
| Effects | Unlocks a button that speeds up gameplay.
| Location | Located due east of The Coop.
| {"description":"Permanently allows you to speed up gameplay.","id":"RELIC_SPEEDUP","update":"Vampire Survivors: Emerald Diorama is free and out now","effects":"Unlocks a button that speeds up gameplay.","sprite":"","name":"Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle","release":"10 April 2025","icon":"","location":"Located due east of The Coop."}
Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle is a relic located in The Coop. Once collected, it permanently unlocks the ability to speed up gameplay.
After the player has obtained this relic, a speed modifier button will be added to the left of the pause button. By default, the game will play in ×1 speed. If the player clicks on the speed modifier button once, the game will play in ×1.5 speed. Doing so again will make the game play in ×2 speed. Clicking once more will return the game to ×1 speed. 
- PC players can also use the Tab key to modify their gameplay speed instead of needing to use the cursor to click on the speed modifier button, while console players can use the Left and Right Bumpers to decrease and increase the speed, respectively.
## Obtaining
Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle is located at the farthest eastern end of The Coop. Once collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, Roast Chicken with a Clock in the Middle can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "yeoldeshoppe".
## Notes
- If the player uses this relic and has Hurry Mode enabled in the stage that they are currently playing, time-based events (such as enemy spawns) will double on top of the speed that this relic provides. For example, if the player has ×2 speed enabled from this relic with Hurry Mode, the timer and all time-based events will have ×4 speed, but everything else happening in the game that is not a time-based event (such as player movement, enemy movement, and weapon projectiles) will be at ×2 speed.
- This relic is particularly disabled in Carlo Cart, regardless if the player sealed this relic in their Collection or not.
## Sprites

## Other

## Trivia
- This relic is a reference to the "Rubber Chicken With a Pulley in the Middle" item from the Monkey Island series.
- The spell "yeoldeshoppe" is a reference to the "Ye Olde Rubber-Chicken-With-a-Pulley-in-the-Middle Shoppe" from the first game in the series, The Secret of Monkey Island.
- Its icon is a reference to a kitchen timer with a red bottom and a chicken on top.

# Rosary
Rosary
| Rosary
| Destroys all enemies in sight.
| Pickup
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | ROSARY
| Rarity | 1
| Unlock level | 8
| Affected by luck | Yes

| Notes | Some enemies are resistant or immune to Rosary. Can be "evolved" into Weird Souls Purifier with Seraphic Cry.
| {"notes":"* Some enemies are resistant or immune to Rosary.\n* Can be \"evolved\" into Weird Souls Purifier with Seraphic Cry.","description":"Destroys all enemies in sight.","id":"ROSARY","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Rosary","effects":"Kills all enemies that are not immune to it.","release":"27 October 2021","level":"8","luck":"Yes","rarity":"1"}
The Rosary is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors that has a chance to drop from light sources. It can only drop after the character reaches level 8, and its drop rate is affected by Luck.
Collecting the Rosary for the first time unlocks the  Cross.
Picking up 33 Rosaries will unlock the Laborratory stage.
## Effects
Picking up the Rosary instantly kills all enemies on the screen.
Rosaries can attack and defeat the otherwise intangible  Stalkers and  Drowners in Dairy Plant, Gallo Tower, and Cappella Magna before the 30:00 mark, causing them to drop  Treasure Chests. However, some enemies, such as  The Reaper, are resistant or immune to the Rosary's effect.
## Weird Souls Purifier
 Seraphic Cry gains a special effect that turns Rosaries into Weird Souls Purifiers. This effect triggers the Rosary's effect four times while also turning enemies into experience gems. Seraphic Cry also multiplies the drop weight of Rosaries by 3, increasing their weight from 1 to 3.
## Trivia
- This is a reference to Rosary from Castlevania series.

The effect is further augmented on listed active weapons.","id":"D01_SAPPHIRE_MIST","update":"Patch 1.11: Bug fixes and improvements","icon":"","image":"","name":"Sapphire Mist (I)","affects":["Bracelet","Bi-Bracelet","Tri-Bracelet","Flames of Misspell","Ashes of Muspell","Penshin Fatcha","Magi-Stone","Kyra-Stones","Mille Bolle Blu","Boo Roo Boolle","Eagle Gun","Pendragon","Party Popper","Party Pooper","Luck"],"release":"15 August 2024","unlock":"Reach a Cooldown bonus of -85% with Space Dude.","arcana name":"Sapphire Mist","type":"Darkana"}
All weapons have a chance to fire twice instead of once when activated. The effect is further augmented on listed active weapons.
Sapphire Mist (I) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching a  Cooldown bonus of -85% while playing as  Space Dude after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Sapphire Mist (I) is active, there is a 28% chance for a weapon to fire twice, scaling with  Luck. Sapphire Mist makes a chime sound and produces a small mist effect around the player when it triggers.
- A Luck bonus of +257% is required to guarantee this effect.
The following weapons have a chance to fire more than twice instead: 
-  Bracelet,  Bi-Bracelet, and  Tri-Bracelet
-  Flames of Misspell and  Ashes of Muspell
-  Penshin Fatcha, all of its morphs, and  Miracle of Multiplication.
-  Magi-Stone and  Kyra-Stones
-  Mille Bolle Blu [DLC] and  Boo Roo Boolle [DLC]
-  Eagle Gun [DLC] and  Pendragon [DLC]
-  Party Popper [DLC] and  Party Pooper [DLC]
## Tips
- The simplest method for the player to unlock Sapphire Mist (I) is to limit weapons to 1, select  Silent Old Sanctuary (XX), and then max out the  Empty Tome. The player can then exit the stage once this is done.
- Alternate methods to reach -85% Cooldown are using  Golden Eggs or  Paranormal Scan [DLC].
- The fastest method is to simply collect a  Gold Finger, such as from Astral Stair or Space 54, to gain -100% Cooldown and immediately exit the stage.
- This is an extremely flexible Darkana that can work with nearly any build. With enough  Luck, it can become a permanent damage multiplier to weapons.
## Audio
 Activation Sound effect
Small chime that plays whenever Sapphire Mist's effect activates. Its pitch gets shifted a little between activations.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Emerald Mist", which is the theme song for Ruvas Forest and Argila Swamp in Castlevania: Order of Ecclesia.
- Sapphire Mist (I) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ottagabli", the reverse of "ilbagatto" or "the magician" in Italian.
- This makes Sapphire Mist (I) a representation of The Magician Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing cunning, illusions, manipulation, wasted potential, and trickery.
- This is reflected in its ability to make weapons fire twice when activated, something that is normally impossible, with high enough Luck.
- It is one of the few arcanas to have their own sound effects.

| {"rarity":"0","id":"BONUS_SCORCHEDSOUL","update":"Vampire Survivors: Legacy of the Bloodmoon is coming this summer!","effects":"Increases Max Health and Might by a minuscule amount, which is multiplied by the Base XP value of the enemy that dropped it.","image":"","name":"Scorched Soul","release":"6 June 2026","level":"1000000","luck":"No","description":"Pick up to get a minor bonus to MaxHealth[sic] and Might."}
The Scorched Soul is a pickup item in Vampire Survivors. It is dropped by defeating enemies using  Fire damage while  Heir of Fate (XIX) is active.
The stat bonuses provided by a Scorched Soul are based on the Base XP value of the enemy that dropped it:
- -  Max Health = 0.1 * Base XP Value
-  Might = 0.01% * Base XP Value
- For example, a  Skelegem, with a Base XP of 3, will drop a Scorched Soul that gives +0.3 Max Health and +0.03% Might.
## Trivia
- This pickup's design combines the inner shading of the  Pummarola (but it is orange instead) and the outer shape of the  Hollow Heart.

The Scorpion Gate is a relic from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. It is the second Gate Key that grants access to new areas in the Ode to Castlevania stage, and can be obtained upon defeating the  Puppet Master.
The Scorpion Gate opens the entrance to the Castle's Library and Stairway and activates the warp gate that teleports the player to the Laboratory. # Seal
Seal is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects the amount of times the player can remove an item choice before the run in the collection menu. 
Seal effectively allows the player to  Banish an item from level up choices or a pickup from light sources before entering a stage. It cannot be used on relics, Arcanas, the  Experience Gem,  Gold Coin,  Coin Bag,  Rich Coin Bag,  Treasure Chest,  Friendship Amulet,  Golden Egg, weapons acquired from  Gemini (I), passive items acquired from the Yellow Sign, and  Academy Badge.
After buying any of the Seal PowerUps, and having installed at least one DLC, the MEGA SEAL module is also unlocked in the collection menu, allowing the player to seal all weapons in a specific version category at once, without taking up any normal Seal slots. However, it is not possible to "un-seal" a specific weapon sealed by the MEGA SEAL.

Each Seal Powerup has a maximum rank of 10. There are four Seal PowerUps: Seal I, Seal II, Seal III, and Seal All. Seal I adds 1 Seal per rank, Seal II adds 2, Seal III adds 3 and Seal All adds 4. Each one initially costs 10,000. Once all four Seal Powerups are maxed out, the usable Seal slots in the collection becomes ∞ (infinite).

## Unlocking
Each PowerUp is unlocked by these achievements:
| PowerUp | Unlocking
| Seal I | Banish 10 or more weapons in a single run.
| Seal II | Banish 20 or more weapons in a single run.
| Seal III | Banish 40 or more weapons in a single run.
| Seal All | Banish 80 or more weapons in a single run.
If no DLCs are owned the amount of weapons that can be banished through the level up choices will be lower than 80. To remedy this the player has to go to the collection and seal weapons that normally wouldn't show up in the level up screen like for example evolutions, around 20 seals will be needed to cover the missing weapons.
## Tips
- Individually sealed items contribute as Banishes for Seal unlocks. However, items sealed by MEGA SEAL do not contribute to the unlocks.
- When an evolved or united weapon is sealed, the base weapon(s) cannot transform into them without a  Super Candybox II Turbo.
- Sealing an item will not prevent it from appearing as a starting item, appearing on the stage or appearing in Item Selectors (e.g.  Candybox). Sealed items obtained with those methods will behave as if the player Banished the item at level 1, preventing it from being leveled up.
- MEGA SEAL is able to seal  Gemini (I)'s counterpart weapons, but cannot seal any other type of unsealable item.

# Serpent Gate
DLC Content (Ode to Castlevania)
This article primarily features content only available in DLC, which may not be available in all copies of the game.

| Effects | Allows Endo Gears, Peri Pendulum, Myo Lift and Epi Head to appear in Ode to Castlevania (stage), opens the entrance to the Ice Cave, Catacombs and the eastmost areas of Ode to Castlevania (stage), and enables the warp gate to the Sewer of Dracula's Castle.

| {"description":"Allows clockwork weapons to materialize and grants access to a new part of Dracula's Castle.","id":"TP_RELIC_TELEPORT5","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","dlc":"oc","sprite":"","name":"Serpent Gate","release":"31 October 2024","location":"Dropped by Menace, in the right side of Sewer in Dracula's Castle.","icon":"","effects":"Allows Endo Gears, Peri Pendulum, Myo Lift and Epi Head to appear in Ode to Castlevania (stage), opens the entrance to the Ice Cave, Catacombs and the eastmost areas of Ode to Castlevania (stage), and enables the warp gate to the Sewer of Dracula's Castle."}
The Serpent Gate is a relic from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. It is the fifth Gate Key that grants access to new areas in the Ode to Castlevania stage, and can be obtained upon defeating  Menace.
The Serpent Gate opens the entrance to the Castle's Ice Cave, Catacombs, and easternmost areas and activates the warp gate that teleports the player to the Sewer. Additionally, the Serpent Gate also causes  Endo Gears,  Peri Pendulum,  Myo Lift, and  Epi Head to permanently appear in the Clock Tower of Dracula's Castle.

# Seventh Trumpet
Seventh Trumpet
| Seventh Trumpet
| Permanently allows to disable all reapers and fight endlessly. Permanently allows to disable all reapers and fight endlessly.
| Relic
| Release | 20 October 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_TRUMPET
| Effects | Unlocks Endless Mode.
| Location | Given after entering Eudaimonia Machine and choosing the "Too Hard" option. If the "Too Easy" option had been chosen instead, start and quit a run in inverse mode, go back to Eudaimonia Machine and it will be given.
| {"description":"Permanently allows to disable all reapers and fight endlessly.","id":"RELIC_TRUMPET","update":"Patch 1.0","effects":"Unlocks Endless Mode.","sprite":"Sprite-Seventh Trumpet.png","name":"Seventh Trumpet","release":"20 October 2022","icon":"Icon-Seventh Trumpet.png","location":"Given after entering Eudaimonia Machine and choosing the \"Too Hard\" option. If the \"Too Easy\" option had been chosen instead, start and quit a run in inverse mode, go back to Eudaimonia Machine and it will be given."}
The Seventh Trumpet is a relic found in Eudaimonia Machine. Once collected, it permanently unlocks endless mode in the stage selection menu. 
Endless mode disables  Reapers from spawning upon reaching a stage's time limit. Instead, reaching a stage's final minute resets the enemy waves to the beginning, finishing a "cycle". For every cycle, enemies gain +100% of their base HP, their spawn quantity and frequency is increased by +50%, and their damage is increased by +25%. The player's incoming damage cap is also reduced by 1. While endless mode is enabled, the  Merchant will also reappear upon completing a cycle, and sells another Arcana card if inverse mode is also enabled.
## Obtaining
Upon entering Eudaimonia Machine for the first time, approach  The Directer. It will then ask the player a question, and if they pick "Too Hard", it will give them the Seventh Trumpet.
If the player picks the other option and obtains the  Gracia's Mirror, the player will need to start and end a run with inverse mode enabled, and then re-eneter Eudaimonia Machine and interact with The Directer a second time to unlock the Seventh Trumpet.
Alternatively, the Seventh Trumpet can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "dootdoot".
## Trivia
- This relic is a reference to the Book of Revelation, in which seven trumpets sounded by seven angels preceded the events of the apocalypse described by John of Atmos.

| ID | T20_SINKING
| Icon | 
| Affects | [Reroll][Skip][Banish]
| Unlocked by | Reach minute 31 in the Dairy Plant.
| {"notes":"","description":"Gives +3 Reroll, Skip, and Banish. Gives +20% Might and -8% Cooldown for each active weapon slot left empty.","id":"T20_SINKING","update":"Patch 0.7.3","icon":"","image":"","name":"Silent Old Sanctuary (XX)","affects":["Reroll","Skip","Banish"],"release":"23 June 2022","unlock":"Reach minute 31 in the Dairy Plant.","arcana name":"Silent Old Sanctuary","type":"Arcana"}
Gives +3 Reroll, Skip, and Banish. Gives +20% Might and -8% Cooldown for each active weapon slot left empty.
Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching the 31:00 mark in Dairy Plant. 
 Lisa Tepes [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) is active, the player is given 3  Rerolls,  Skips, and  Banishes each. Additionally, for each active weapon slot left empty, the character gains +20%  Might and -8%  Cooldown, for a maximum of +100% Might and -40% Cooldown.
## Tips
- The player can use the  Mindbender to limit a character's maximum amount of weapons to benefit from this Arcana for the entire duration of a run without getting eventually forced into picking weapons.
- This is a great second Arcana for runs using  Game Killer (0) with characters like  Porta Ladonna and  Suor Clerici.
- This can be an extremely powerful Arcana for one-weapon builds by allowing you to focus limit breaking a single weapon.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "The Sinking Old Sanctuary", which is the theme song of the Atlantis Shrine in Castlevania: Bloodlines.
- Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ilgiudizio", which means "the judgement" in Italian.
- This makes Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) a representation of the Judgement Major Arcana card, symbolizing absolution, assessment, redemption, catharsis, spiritual awakening, and moving on from one's past.
- This is reflected in its ability to grant significant strength to players using fewer weapons.

Silver Ring is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked after obtaining Yellow Sign. It cannot be found via level ups, and must be found as a stage item.
Silver Ring increases  Duration and  Area by 5% per level.
Silver Ring, alongside  Gold Ring, is required for the evolution of  Clock Lancet,  Infinite Corridor.
Silver Ring cannot be obtained from level ups, but can be found as a stage item in every stage, except Bat Country, Tiny Bridge, Eudaimonia Machine, and Whiteout. Generally, it will spawn north of the starting area. It will always be guarded by a Moon Atlantean.
## Levels
Silver Ring has 9 levels. At max level, Silver Ring provides +40% Duration and Area.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | Wear ... Clock ...
| Level 2 | Base Area up by 5%. Effect lasts 5% longer.
| Level 3 | Base Area up by 5%. Effect lasts 5% longer.
| Level 4 | Base Area up by 5%. Effect lasts 5% longer.
| Level 5 | Base Area up by 5%. Effect lasts 5% longer.
| Level 6 | Base Area up by 5%. Effect lasts 5% longer.
| Level 7 | Base Area up by 5%. Effect lasts 5% longer.
| Level 8 | Base Area up by 5%. Effect lasts 5% longer.
| Level 9 | Base Area up by 5%. Effect lasts 5% longer.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Clock Lancet | + | Max level:Silver Ring+Gold Ring | = | Infinite Corridor
## Tips
- Silver Ring does not provide any stats at level 1.
## Trivia
- Together with the description of  Gold Ring, it forms "Wear With Clock Lancet", which is a reference to "Wear in Clock Tower", the combined description of the Silver Ring and Gold Ring in the game Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Silver Ring, Gold Ring,  Metaglio Left,  Metaglio Right,  Torrona's Box, and  Outer Saboteur [DLC] are the only passive items with max level 9 instead of the usual 5.
