# Skip
Skip
Skip is one of the stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects the amount of times the player can skip the rewards when leveling up and gain experience instead.
There is no upper limit to how many Skips the player may have.
## PowerUp
| Skip
| Twice per rank, allows you to skip level up choices and get Experience instead.
| PowerUp
| Release | 13 February 2022 (Update)
| ID | SKIP
| Max level | 5
| Bonus per level | +2
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +10
| Initial cost | 100
| {"description":"Twice per rank, allows you to skip level up choices and get Experience instead.","cost":"100","id":"SKIP","update":"Patch 0.2.12 - small update","image":"","name":"Skip","release":"13 February 2022","stacking":"Additive","max level":"5","bonus":"+2"}
The Skip PowerUp increases Skip by 2 per rank, with a maximum rank of 5 after unlocking all Skip achievements. With one rank unlocked it initially costs 100.
| Rank | Skip
| 1 | +2
| 2 | +4
| 3 | +6
| 4 | +8
| 5 | +10
Each rank of PowerUp is unlocked by these achievements:
- Surviving 15 minutes in Il Molise.
- Surviving 30 minutes in Green Acres.
- Surviving 30 minutes in The Bone Zone.
- Surviving 15 minutes in Moongolow.
- Surviving 15 minutes in Boss Rash.
## From items
Skip does not have any associated passive item.
 Golden Egg permanently increases Skip by 0.2 for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
 Silent Old Sanctuary (XX) provides 3 Skip.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Gallo starts with +3 Skip.
-  Sigma starts with +108 Skip.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Engineer starts with +20 Skip.
## Operation Guns
-  Bill and  Lance start with +4 Skip.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Juste,  Soma and  Lisa Tepes start with +5 Skip.
-  Dracula,  Charlotte,  Sypha,  Julia,  Carrie,  Yoko,  Rinaldo,  Mina,  Elizabeth,  Shanoa,  Saint Germain,  Carmilla,  Olrox,  Megalo Elizabeth,  Megalo Olrox,  Megalo Death and  Megalo Dracula start with +10 Skip.
-  Isaac and  Hector start with +20 Skip.
-  Master Librarian starts with +100 Skip.
-  Chaos starts with +65536 Skip.
## Tips
- Use up all the  Reroll and  Banish before using Skip, as they provide 20% of the experience to the next level so using it later when each level requires more exp will be more efficient. Having more weapons and passive items early on is much better than using a level to reroll choices because it translates to more kills and experience when it take less to level up.

Skull O'Maniac is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by surviving 30 minutes with Lama.
Skull O'Maniac increases Curse by 10% per level.
Skull O'Maniac is required for the evolution of Song of Mana, Mannajja. It is also required for the evolution of Shadow Servant, Ophion, in which Skull O'Maniac must also be fully leveled.
Skull O'Maniac can be found as a stage item in Mad Forest, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Lake Foscari, and Abyss Foscari.

Skull O'Maniac has 5 levels. At max level, Skull O'Maniac provides +50% Curse.

| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Song of Mana | + | Skull O'Maniac | = | Mannajja

Enables critical hits for listed weapons. Doubles overall critical damage.
Slash (XVI) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Lama Ladonna.
 Reinhardt Schneider [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Slash (XVI) is active, certain weapons can deal critical damage. More importantly, critical hits from all sources deal double damage. 
This Arcana applies to the following weapons:
-  Whip and  Bloody Tear (Enables critical hits)
-  Knife,  Thousand Edge and  Million Cut [DLC] (Enables critical hits)
-  Axe,  Death Spiral and  Legacy of Death: Soul River [DLC](Enables critical hits)
-  Heaven Sword and  Arch Angle [DLC]
-  Vento Sacro and  Fuwalafuwaloo
-  Ammo Appalate,  Horse Cartridge, and  Gunastrophe (Enables critical hits)
-  Victory Sword (Enables critical hits earlier than normal)
-  Celestial Voulge
-  Santa Javelin,  Levelin'Eh, and  Seraphic Cry
-  Greatest Jubilee (Unlisted in game, doubles critical hit damage of light show finisher)
-  Yatta Daikarin (Unlisted in game, doubled critical hit of constellations)
-  Muramasa [DLC]
-  Ashella [DLC] and  Bloodlust [DLC]
-  Descent Into Misery [DLC] and  Shimmering Sands [DLC]
-  Eskizzibur [DLC] and  Legionnaire [DLC] (Enables critical hits)
-  Flash Arrow [DLC] and  Millionaire [DLC]
-  Impostongue [DLC]
-  Alchemy Whip [DLC] and  Vampire Killer [DLC] (Enables critical hits for the whip attack)
-  Wind Whip [DLC] and  Spirit Tornado Tip [DLC] (Enables critical hits for the whip attack)
-  Platinum Whip [DLC] and  Cross Crasher Tip [DLC] (Enables critical hits for the whip attack)
-  Dragon Water Whip [DLC] and  Hydrostormer Tip [DLC] (Enables critical hits for the whip attack)
-  Sonic Whip [DLC] and  Crissaegrim Tip [DLC] (Enables critical hits for the whip attack)
-  Jet Black Whip [DLC] and  Mormegil Tip [DLC] (Enables critical hits for the whip attack)
-  Vibhuti Whip [DLC] and  Daybreaker Tip [DLC] (Enables critical hits for the whip attack)
-  Vanitas Whip [DLC] and  Aurablaster Tip [DLC] (Enables critical hits for the whip attack)
-  Gungnir-Souris [DLC] (Unlisted in game)
-  Tyrfing [DLC] and  Rune Sword [DLC]
-  Confodere [DLC],  Vol Confodere [DLC], and  Melio Confodere [DLC](Enables critical hits)
-  Pocket Knife [DLC] and  Claimh Solais [DLC]
-  Twin Dragon [DLC] and  Gekkabijin [DLC]
-  Khukuri [DLC] and  Jetstream [DLC]
-  Bullova [DLC] and  Falconwind [DLC]
-  Flamberge [DLC],  Zweihander [DLC], and  Galatyn [DLC]
-  Gros Michel [DLC] and  Cavendish [DLC] (Unlisted in game)
## Tips
- Slash increases the potential damage output for the listed weapons by a staggering amount; aside from Arcana that focus on stat increases,  Iron Blue Will (VII) and  Waltz of Pearls (XI) are recommended to increase the potential number of enemies being hit.
- Slash synergizes with the  Luck stat, which increases the chance for a critical hit to occur. For this reason, it works best on characters with scaling Luck or those picking  Wicked Season (XIII) early on.
-  Claimh Solais [DLC] is almost mandatory in Slash-centric builds, as it significantly increases the critical damage of all weapons.
- Synergies perfectly with  Cavendish [DLC], which always crit and increases overall critical chance.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Slash", which is the theme song of the Moutains in Castlevania: Rondo of Blood.
- Slash (XVI) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "latorre", which means "the tower" in Italian.
- This makes Slash (XVI) a representation of The Tower Major Arcana card, symbolizing sudden upheaval, premonition, ill omens, chaos, disaster, and destruction.
- This is reflected with the brush stroke being a line in the middle and in its ability to grant the player's bladed weapons the chance of dealing or increasing critical damage.
- The name and sprite of this Arcana is also a reference to "Power Slash", which is a Celestial Brush technique from Ōkami. By stroking a horizontal line of ink across some objects, it will cut them in half. While the sprite does not show a horizontal line, it is still an ink trail. Its effect also has the theme of dealing damage as Power Slash is used to deal supplementary damage while in combat.
- The weapons that Slash can affect are traditionally melee weapons that can cause laceration (which is almost all of them in the displayed list of weapons), fitting for its name.
- This may explain why it does not affect  Cross, which is a boomerang without sharp edges in Castlevania that bludgeons enemies, while Heaven Sword is affected.
- Curiously,  Curved Knife and its evolution  Bwaka Knife are unaffected.
- Some weapons like  Phiera Der Tuphello,  Eight The Sparrow,  Phieraggi, and most DLC weapons have the parameter for crit chance and multiplier filled in. However, their ability to crit is disabled and Slash does not activate them.

# Sorceress' Tears
Sorceress' Tears
| Sorceress' Tears
| Permanently allows to speed up time in Stage Selection. Permanently allows to speed up time in Stage Selection.
| Relic
| Release | 5 April 2022 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_TEAR
| Effects | Unlocks Hurry Mode.
| Location | Due south of Gallo Tower.
| {"description":"Permanently allows to speed up time in Stage Selection.","id":"RELIC_TEAR","update":"Patch 0.4.2","effects":"Unlocks Hurry Mode.","sprite":"Sprite-Sorceress' Tears.png","name":"Sorceress' Tears","release":"5 April 2022","icon":"Icon-Sorceress' Tears.png","location":"Due south of Gallo Tower."}
Sorceress' Tears is a relic located in Gallo Tower. Once collected, it permanently unlocks hurry mode in the stage selection menu.
Hurry mode speeds up the in-game timer to be twice as fast as it is normally and increases the stage's XP Bonus by +25%. The stages' time-based events still occur at their usual timestamps on the in-game timer.
## Obtaining
The Sorceress' Tears is located to the far south from the starting area in Gallo Tower, and spawns a circle of 11  Sword Guardians around it when approached. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. Once the Sorceress' Tears has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, the Sorceress' Tears can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "timecompression".
## Trivia
- The Sorceress' Tears and the Spell may together be a reference to Final Fantasy VIII, in which a 'sorceress' uses 'time compression' to fortify her power across time. The same plot point moves the protagonists rapidly forward in time.

# Speed
This article is about projectile speed. For movement speed, see Move Speed.
Speed (also known as Projectile Speed) is one of the player stats in Vampire Survivors. It affects how fast the projectiles of most weapons move.
The player has 100% base Speed before modifications, such as the characters bonuses or PowerUps. The Speed in the stat panel is given as the difference to the base Speed, meaning that Speed stat of +30% equals to 130% total Speed, for example. Speed modifications from characters, PowerUps, and items stack additively. Other modifiers, such as Arcanas, stack multiplicatively with the collective bonuses from the aforementioned.
The upper limit of total Speed after character, PowerUp and item modifications is 500%. Bonuses from Arcanas are unaffected by the limit. If total Speed is negative, projectiles move backwards. At 0 Speed, they do not move at all.
Speed also affects:
- Base Damage of SpellString [DLC],  SpellStrom [DLC],  Confodere [DLC],  Vol Confodere [DLC],  Sonic Dash [DLC],  Rapidus Fio [DLC],  Hydro Storm [DLC]
- Base Damage and Cooldown of  Melio Confodere [DLC]
- Singularity damage of  SpellStrom [DLC]
- Critical Damage of  Rune Sword [DLC]
- Further increases the projectile speed of  Thunderbolt Spear [DLC]'s special attack.
-  Jonathan Morris [DLC]'s charged ability
-  Newt Plissken [DLC] gains +1% Might per +1% Speed.
## PowerUp
| Speed
| Projectiles move 10% faster per rank (max 20%).
| PowerUp
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | SPEED
| Max level | 2
| Bonus per level | +10%
| Stacking | Additive
| Max effect | +20%
| Initial cost | 300

The Speed PowerUp increases Speed by 10% per rank, with a maximum rank of 2. At rank 2, it provides +20% Speed. It initially costs 300.
| Rank | Speed
| 1 | +10%
| 2 | +20%
## From items
Speed's associated passive item is the  Bracer, which provides +10% Speed per level, up to a maximum of +50% Speed at level 5.
-  Torrona's Box provides +4%, then +3% per level, up to a maximum of +25% at level 8.
-  Rapid Fire [DLC] provides +30% Speed for 10 seconds, this stacks with no cap.
-  Paranormal Scan [DLC] has a chance of granting a Speed stat bonus of +4% upon activation.
-  Golden Egg can permanently increase Speed by +1% for the character that collected it.
## From arcana
-  Awake (IV) provides +5% Speed when chosen per  Revival consumed.
-  Chaos in the Dark Night (V) provides a multiplier cycling between 0.5 to 1.5 every 10 seconds, and gives +1% Speed every level.
## From characters
## Base Game
-  Pasqualina starts with +10% Speed and gains +10% Speed every 5 levels, up to +30% at level 15. In total, she can have +40% Speed.
-  Dommario starts with +40% Speed bonus.
-  Giovanna gains +1% Speed every level, with no cap.
-  Zi'Assunta gains +0.5% Speed every level, with no cap.
-  Exdash and  Toastie start with -50% Speed.
-  Smith starts with -50% Speed and gains +0.7% Speed every level, with no cap.
-  Marrabbio starts with -110% Speed.
-  Minnah's Speed cycles between multiplied by 2 and divided by 2 every minute.
## Legacy of the Moonspell
-  Syuuto starts with -30% Speed. He gains +30% Speed after he morphs when his  starting weapon evolves.
## Tides of the Foscari
-  Keitha starts with +20% Speed.
## Emergency Meeting
-  Scientist can select to gain +1% Speed per level every 10 levels until level 50, for a maximum of up to +5% Speed per level.
## Operation Guns
-  Bill and  Lance start with +20% Speed and gain 2.5% Speed at specific kill thresholds up to 50%.
-  Ariana starts with +20% Speed.
-  Lucia gains +4% Speed after reviving, with no cap.
-  Brad gains up to +10% Speed based on missing percentage of  Max Health scaling linearly.
## Ode to Castlevania
-  Eric and  Isaac start with +20% Speed.
-  Yoko starts with +40% Speed.
-  Joachim can select to gain 1% Speed per level.
-  Blackmore can select to gain +1% Speed per level after defeating 1000, 5000, 9000, 13000 and 17000 enemies, for a maximum of up to +5% Speed per level.
-  Maxim gains +1% Speed per 24 health of overhealing.
-  Leon gains +10% Speed upon obtaining the  Vampire Killer.
## Tips
- Projectile Speed stat is additive. The highest possible Projectile Speed is possible while playing as Giovanna, with the rank 2 Speed PowerUp (+20%), rank 5 Omni PowerUp (+10%), level 5 Bracer (+50%), and level 8  Torrona's Box (+25%) for a total of +(player level+105)% Projectile Speed.
- Minnah's stat cycling cannot stack with  Chaos in the Dark Night (V).
## Trivia
- In the old game demo, the Speed stat used to be called "Velocity."
=====

# Stake to Your Heart (0)
| Stake to Your Heart (0)
| Halts XP gain. Enemies drop Gold Coins. Damage is dealt to Gold Coins instead of HP. A special merchant spawns every minute.
| Darkana
| Release | 6 June 2026 (Update)
| Darkana # | 0
| ID | D00_STAKE_TO_YOUR_HEART
| Icon | 
| Affects | [Gold Coin][Experience Gem]
| Unlocked by | Defeat a Bat Dragon with Big Troubler while using the Game Killer Arcana.
| Notes | Halted XP gain is ignored by Sammy via its gold-to-XP passive.
| {"notes":"Halted XP gain is ignored by Sammy via its gold-to-XP passive.","description":"Halts XP gain. Enemies drop Gold Coins. Damage is dealt to Gold Coins instead of HP. A special merchant spawns every minute.","id":"D00_STAKE_TO_YOUR_HEART","update":"Vampire Survivors: Legacy of the Bloodmoon is coming this summer!","icon":"","image":"","name":"Stake to Your Heart (0)","affects":["Gold Coin (currency)","Experience Gem"],"release":"6 June 2026","unlock":"Defeat a Bat Dragon with Big Troubler while using the Game Killer Arcana.","arcana name":"Stake to Your Heart","type":"Darkana"}
Halts XP gain. Enemies drop Gold Coins. Damage is dealt to Gold Coins instead of HP. A special merchant spawns every minute.
Stake to Your Heart (0) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by defeating a  Bat Dragon with  Big Troubler while using the  Game Killer (0) Arcana after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Stake to Your Heart (0) is active, the player will no longer be able to level up using  Experience Gems, much like Game Killer (0). 
- If the player is playing as  Sammy [DLC], they will still be able to level up using gold.
Whenever an enemy is defeated, they will always drop a  Gold Coin.
Instead of the player taking damage to their  Max Health, gold obtained during the run will be depleted in its place, similarly to  Big Troubler's passive effect. If the player has not collected any more gold during the current run, they will then start taking damage to their Max Health as per usual.
Additionally, while Stake to Your Heart (0) is active,  Rich Coin Bags and  Gilded Clovers will have their minimum spawn level reduced to 0, allowing them to appear regardless of the player's level.
## Merchant
Every minute, at X:00, a special merchant spawns near the player. This merchant allows the player to exchange the gold they earned during the run for a selection of weapons and the option to level up their weapons. Up to twelve weapons will be available at a time, and what is offered is based on what the player could normally find in level ups. Four slots will give new items, prioritizing weapons and passive items that evolve what the player currently has, while the other eight slots will be levels for existing weapons and passives. This selection can be rerolled using  Rerolls. The amount of gold required to level up a weapon increases with the weapon's level, and the cost to purchase or level up a weapon is decreased with  Greed. Limit Break levels cannot be purchased at this merchant. This merchant will be marked on the map, and there is no limit to how many can be present at once.
The special merchant summoned by Stake to Your Heart will give the player the option to sell their weapons. The amount of gold offered scales with both the weapons level and all gold multipliers, such as  Greed, but not Limit Break levels. Evolutions are worth far more gold compared to other items.
The cost of an item is determined by its base cost, the level to-be-purchased, and Greed. The base cost of a weapon is multiplied by the following value:
L×(L+1)2
where 'L' is the level of the purchase. Thus, a new weapon will not have its base cost modified by this, but upgrading it to level two will be three times as expensive compared to the base. 
- This value is a triangular number.
Every +1% of Greed will provide a discount of 0.2%. A maximum discount of 90% can be achieved at +450% Greed.
- There is no limit to how much gold is offered when selling a weapon, however, so Greed past +450% will continue to be useful.
## Tips
-  Sarabande of Healing (VI) works well with this Darkana, as with proper gold farming strategies, the player can heal extremely quickly.
- Similarly to  Game Killer (0), this Darkana works well with characters with a powerful level 1 bonus that depreciates as they level up, including  Suor Clerici,  Porta Ladonna,  Sir Ambrojoe and  Tera [DLC]. Unlike Game Killer, it allows these characters to still personalize their build without being limited to solely merchant weapons, stage items and random drops from  Moonlight Bolero (VI). It also works well with characters that have high starting stats and no endless scaling, and especially those with high  Greed like the  Master Librarian [DLC].
- As enemies still drop experience gems in addition to the gold coins,  Game Killer (0) synergizes well with Stake To Your Heart since it allows the player to still make use of these gems. In addition, Game Killer's bonus to chests allows the player to make more gold while simultaneously relying less on the merchant to evolve their items, and  Moonlight Bolero (VI) is also fantastic for similar reasons.
- This arcana is almost a must-pick for  Fleaman [DLC] as it mostly negates both of his weaknesses: the nonexistent health and the inability to level up.
- While  Big Troubler shares the gold-to-health ability provided by Stake to Your Heart (0), the ability to make every enemy drop a gold coin upon death allows him to consistently, and rapidly, trigger  Unearthly Bolt and its evolution,  Spirit Disturbance.
- Stake to Your Heart is the only way of removing a character's weapons and passives in the game, other than subsuming them into another weapon or into  Alucard Shield [DLC]. This has a few niche use cases, such as  Babi-Onna [DLC] selling her  Mirage Robe [DLC] in order to increase the chance of more impactful weapons like  Troll Bomb [DLC] triggering from her ability.
- Thanks to its ability to gain experience from gold coins,  Sammy [DLC] can continue leveling up even with Stake to Your Heart (0). This Darkana synergizes extremely well with Sammy thanks to the guaranteed gold for every kill, drastically increasing both gold and experience gain.
- If one is not planning to fight and kill  The Reaper, it is recommended to quit the stage shortly before the 30 minute mark instead of completing the stage by getting killed as usual. Otherwise, the player will not get any gold from the stage as the Reaper's attacks will deplete it before killing you. To turn in a little bit of extra profit, the player should sell all their weapons and items to a merchant right before quitting or before being killed by the  White Hand.
- Like Game Killer, this makes  Survarots 006 (Porta Ladonna), 009 (Suor Clerici), and 021 (Ambrojoe) insanely powerful, as one can get -90%  Cooldown, 400%  Area or +10  Amount and keep it (due to no further levels ever being gained).
- The normally awkward  Vicious Hunger, which in other builds can greatly slow down leveling rate, is a key weapon in this build as transforming enemies into coins has no downside at all.
- This darkana synergizes well with  Victorian Horror (XVIII), as the latter grants a way to increase Greed without needing to level up.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Cross Your Heart", which is the theme song for the Graveyard, the first stage in Haunted Castle. Haunted Castle is the first arcade game tied to the Castlevania series, and was released in 1988.
- Stake to Your Heart (0) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ottamli", the reverse of "ilmatto" or "the fool" in Italian.
- This makes Stake to Your Heart (0) a representation of The Fool Major Arcana card in reverse position, symbolizing shortsighted decisions, insecurity, brashness, impulsiveness, and recklessness.
- This is reflected in its ability for the player to deviate from the standard gameplay formula by using gold to buy and sell their own weapons instead of using experience.
- According to the game's code, the merchant's code name is labeled as EX_GLAUCUS. This suggests that the name of the merchant may be Glaucus.
- The merchant's sprite is based on the Room 1665 enemy,  Shendi Darkasso, which in of itself is based on a sculpture from the Gardens of Bomarzo.

The Stallion Gate is a relic from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. It is the first Gate Key that grants access to new areas in the Ode to Castlevania stage, and can be obtained upon defeating the  Giant Bat.
The Stallion Gate opens the entrance to the Castle's Gallery and Laboratory and activates the warp gate outside Dracula's Castle, allowing the player to teleport to any unlocked gate. The Stallion Gate's warp gate teleports the player to the room beyond the First Hallway. Additionally, the Stallion Gate also permanently unlocks  Heart Refreshes, allowing them to appear in the Ode to Castlevania stage or while playing as any character from its associated DLC.

| Starry Heavens
| 
| Pickup
| Release | 19 October 2023 (Update)
| ID | ? (edit)

| Unlock level | 4
| Affected by luck | Yes

| Notes | Some enemies are resistant to being frozen.
| {"notes":"* Some enemies are resistant to being frozen.","description":"","id":"","update":"New Content Patch 1.7.0: Whiteout","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Starry Heavens","effects":"All enemies are frozen for 10 seconds and take damage periodically, unable to damage the player. They still move towards the player.","release":"19 October 2023","level":"4","luck":"Yes","rarity":"2"}
Starry Heavens is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors that has a chance to drop from light sources after evolving  Glass Fandango into  Celestial Voulge. It can only drop after the character reaches level 4, and its drop rate is affected by  Luck. It is an improved version of  Orologion.
## Effects
Picking up Starry Heavens creates a starry background that briefly pulls into the character and spins clockwise. Then, the background is split into five, centered around the character and slowly emits stars in the counterclockwise direction. Shortly after, lines converge around the character in a circle which forms the twelve zodiac signs. It freezes all enemies and damages them periodically. During the effect, the pitch of a sound effect keeps increasing. Frozen enemies are tinted blue and do no damage to the player, but can still move. Enemies will continue to spawn, and new enemies will also become frozen. The effect will last for 10 seconds, and the background will fade away. Some enemies are resistant to the freeze effect.
Starry Heavens slows down  Gold Fever's duration decay by half.
 Out of Bounds (XII) will double the drop rate rarity of Starry Heavens from 2 to 4.
## Trivia
- Starry Heavens was inspired by the Galaxy Stop ability from Final Fantasy Tactics.

Stone Mask is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by finding it as a stage item for the first time.
Stone Mask increases Greed by 10% per level.
Stone Mask is required for the evolution of Gatti Amari, Vicious Hunger. It is also required for the evolution of Night Sword, Muramasa, in which Stone Mask must also be fully leveled.
Stone Mask can be found as a stage item in Inlaid Library, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Mt.Moonspell, and Abyss Foscari.
## Levels
Stone Mask has 5 levels. At max level, Stone Mask provides +50% Greed.
| Level | Description

| Rock Riot | + | Max level:Stone Mask | = | Gemma Torpor
| Sanguine Star | + | Stone Mask+Blood Rage tech | = | Blood Chalice
## Tips
- Stone Mask is not vital for winning a run, but rather getting the most money out of it. If you find yourself unable to finish a run, it is wise to avoid taking the Stone Mask unless you are already set up for the late game.
- Stone Mask increases the value of the coin also increases how much they can heal with Disco of Gold (XV).
## Trivia
- Stone Mask is a two-tiered reference, both to the Castlevania series, and more directly, to the JoJo's Bizarre Adventure series: most Castlevania games since Symphony of the Night have had a Stone Mask as a piece of wearable equipment, along with a number of other JoJo references, due to the series’ creator, Koji Igarashi, being a devoted fan of JoJo’s Bizarre Adventure. The origin of the item itself is found within the JoJo’s Bizarre Adventure series, in which they were ancient tribal masks that transformed anyone who wore them into vampires, and which one of the series’ antagonists, Dio Brando, notably uses for that reason, rejecting his humanity.

| {"image":"Icon-Tirajisú.png","description":"Revives once with 50% Health.","max-level":"2","id":"REVIVAL","stage-item":"Cappella Magna, Moongolow, Boss Rash","update":"Patch 0.2.11","stacking":"Additive","sprite":"Sprite-Tirajisú.png","max-effect":"+2","name":"Tirajisú","stat":"Revival","release":"5 February 2022","rarity":"40","icon":"Icon-Tirajisú.png","per-level":"+1"}
Tirajisú is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by surviving for 20 minutes as Krochi Freetto.
Tirajisú increases Revival by 1 per level.
Tirajisú is required for the evolution and union of Phiera Der Tuphello and Eight The Sparrow, Phieraggi.
Tirajisú can be found as a stage item in Cappella Magna, Moongolow, Boss Rash and Ode to Castlevania.
## Levels
Tirajisú has 2 levels. At max level, Tirajisú provides +2 Revival.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Revives once with 50% health."
| Level 2 | Adds 1 Revival.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Phiera Der TuphelloEight The Sparrow | + | Tirajisú | = | Phieraggi
| Unearthly Bolt | + | Max level:Tirajisú | = | Spirit Disturbance
| C-U-Laser | + | Tirajisú+Weapon Power-Up | = | Pronto Beam
| Alchemy Whip | + | Tirajisú | = | Vampire Killer
| Wine Glass | + | Max level:Tirajisú | = | Meal Ticket
## Tips
- If you die with Tirajisú and haven't fully upgraded it, it will disappear from the inventory but you can get it again through a level up. The level cap resets and you can repeat it as many time as you like. However, due to its rarity, repeating this mechanic consistently is unlikely.
- Tirajisú will only be removed from the inventory when the last Revive is used up. If you extended your accessory limit with floor items, the slot that Tirajisú had taken up will be filled by the next exceeding item.
- Tirajisú will be removed from your inventory after using all other Revival sources, including the Revival Power-Up, Awake (IV) Arcana, and Revival from character stats.
- With the stage item, the player can still get it after the original Tirajisú is used up and it gives you one more Revival. However, it cannot be leveled up.
- Tirajisú is a powerful tool that can save runs. While it provides no benefit until you die, it can save the player from sloppy mistakes and being suddenly overwhelmed.
- Tirajisú are especially useful when playing as characters with less survivability, such as Exdash and Toastie.
- With Phieraggi, Revivals increases Amount and Base Damage of the weapon. Any use of Revivals will decrease the bonuses and make the weapon less effective. Consider conserving Revivals before fully using it up to create an empty passive item slot.
- Since Tirajisú is not a permanent passive item like the others, it is possible to use up all the Revivals from this item and PowerUp, losing it before being able to evolve Phiera Der Tuphello and Eight The Sparrow. This can be extremely detrimental as the base weapons are not the strongest and will take up two slots which could be used for better options.
- Tirajisú can be useful for runs where the goal is to survive as long as possible against Death, and can greatly extend runs when paired with other defensive measures.
- Tirajisú can be useful for gathering loot otherwise unobtainable before Death ends the run. Even 1 revival can allow the player to get a few more levels or one extra Treasure Chest.
- Tirajisú should be considered against alternative Health boosting options, such as the Pummarola and Hollow Heart. It may be worth taking them over the Tirajisú if the player has the weapons they evolve.
## Trivia
- Tirajisú is a pun on the Italian dessert tiramisú, a layer cake composed of ladyfingers dipped in coffee, a whipped mixture of egg yolks, sugar, and mascarpone, and topped with cocoa powder. In Italian, tiramisú literally means 'pull/lift-me-up' (tira-mi-sú), or pick-me-up. It is most likely a portmanteau with Gesù, the Italian form of the name Jesus, hence it is resurrecting instead of being a simple upper.
- Prior to patch 1.8, Tirajisú was named "Tiragisú", likely based on the spelling of Gesù, and likely changed to clarify the pronunciation.
- Tirajisú and  Duplicator are the only passive items with a max level of 2 instead of the usual 5.
=====

Torrona's Box is a passive item in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by holding 6 different weapon evolutions at once.
Torrona's Box increases Omni ( Might,  Speed,  Duration, and  Area) by 4% at the first level, then 3% per level until the last level increases  Curse by 100%.
Torrona's Box is required for the gift of  Victory Sword,  Sole Solution, the evolution of  Flames of Misspell,  Ashes of Muspell, as well as the evolution of  Pocket Knife [DLC],  Claimh Solais [DLC], all of which Torrona's Box must also be fully leveled.
Torrona's Box can be found as a stage item in Abyss Foscari.
## Levels
Torrona's Box has 9 levels. At max level, Torrona's Box provides +25% Omni and +100% Curse.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | Cursed item, but increases Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, and Area by 4%.
| Level 2 | Increases Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, and Area by 3%.
| Level 3 | Increases Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, and Area by 3%.
| Level 4 | Increases Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, and Area by 3%.
| Level 5 | Increases Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, and Area by 3%.
| Level 6 | Increases Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, and Area by 3%.
| Level 7 | Increases Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, and Area by 3%.
| Level 8 | Increases Might, Projectile Speed, Duration, and Area by 3%.
| Level 9 | Increases enemy speed, health, quantity, and frequency by 100%.
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Flames of Misspell | + | Max level:Torrona's Box | = | Ashes of Muspell
| Victory Sword | + | Max level:Torrona's Box | = | Gift:Sole Solution
| Pocket Knife | + | Max level:Torrona's Box | = | Claimh Solais
| Festive Winds | + | Max level:Torrona's Box | = | Argent Flow
| Blacken Firmament | + | Max level:Torrona's Box | = | Damnation
## Tips
- If it is not used for evolving weapons, it is advisable to  Banish Torrona’s Box when it is at level 8 to avoid the  Curse bonus, while maximizing the positive effects.
- However, Curse can be useful for certain characters and weapons:
-  Hex [DLC] and its evolved form  Nightmare [DLC], as well as  Twilight Requiem (II)'s explosions, have their damage affected by Curse.
-  Colonel Bahamut [DLC] and  Vlad Tepes Dracula [DLC] have passives where Curse also affects their damage.
## Trivia
- Torrona's Box,  Silver Ring,  Gold Ring,  Metaglio Left,  Metaglio Right, and  Outer Saboteur [DLC] are the only passive items with max level 9 instead of the usual 5.
- This passive item is a reference to the Pandora's Box where opening it unleashes all physical and emotional curses upon mankind with only hope left behind. Torrona's Box seems to be the opposite, in that all the good comes before bad.
- Torrona is also likely a reference to turrón, an Italian nougat confection, in the similar way pandoro is, and both are traditionally served during the Christmas season.

The cooldown of the listed weapons reduces when moving.
Tragic Princess (III) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Porta Ladonna.
 Yoko Belnades [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Tragic Princess (III) is active, the following weapons gain reduced Cooldown when the player is moving, which include:
-  Garlic,  Soul Eater and  Dark Frogamorphosis [DLC]
-  Santa Water,  La Borra and  Hydro Pump Climax [DLC]
-  Lightning Ring,  Thunder Loop and  Vjaya Sisters [DLC]
-  Santa Javelin,  Levelin'Eh, and  Seraphic Cry
-  Carréllo
-  Scarlet Needle [DLC] and  Cardinal Rain [DLC]
-  Blacken Firmament [DLC] and  Damnation [DLC]
-  Fulgur [DLC],  Speculo Fulgur [DLC], and  Tenebris Tonitrus [DLC]
-  Keremet Bubbles [DLC],  Speculo Keremet Bubbles [DLC], and  Keremet Morbus [DLC]
-  Kick [DLC] and  Triangle Kick [DLC]
This cooldown reduction modifier acts independently of the  Cooldown stat, allowing it to reduce the cooldown of listed weapons even after the player has reached -90% Cooldown.
## Tips
- Since this Arcana encourages the player to move, it pairs up really well with  Shadow Pinion,  Valkyrie Turner,  Vento Sacro,  Fuwalafuwaloo,  Celestial Dusting and  Valmanway even without directly benefiting from it.
- The faster the player moves, the greater the Cooldown reduction. This is particularly apparent while using the  Lightning Ring and the  Thunder Loop, owing to their sound cues. Increases to  Move Speed, such as with  Wings, pair up very well with this Arcana, and decreases will weaken it.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "The Tragic Prince", which is the theme song for the Clock Tower in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Tragic Princess (III) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "limperatrice", which means "the empress" in Italian.
- This makes Tragic Princess (III) a representation of The Empress Major Arcana card, symbolizing elegance, nurturing, fertility, compassion, initiative, and abundance.
- This is reflected in its ability to trigger weapons that are slower to activate more often through the player's movements. Its name as well as the crown symbol on the card is also representative.

# Treasure Chest
Treasure chest
| Treasure Chest
| Awards coins and Power ups. Dropped by strong enemies.
| Pickup
| Release | 27 October 2021 (Update)
| ID | TREASURE
| Rarity | 0
| Unlock level | 100,000
| Affected by luck | Yes
| Effects | Gives coins, and levels up or evolves weapons. A purple chest offers an Arcana instead.
| Notes | Drops from bosses, not from light sources. Doesn't get attracted to the player. Only the quality of the chest is affected by Luck. Grants an upgrade to 1, 3 or 5 owned weapons and passive items, depending on the tier of the chest. Amount of coins gained is multiplied by Greed.
| {"notes":"* Drops from bosses, not from light sources. Doesn't get attracted to the player.\n* Only the quality of the chest is affected by Luck.\n* Grants an upgrade to 1, 3 or 5 owned weapons and passive items, depending on the tier of the chest.\n* Amount of coins gained is multiplied by Greed.","description":"Awards coins and Power ups. Dropped by strong enemies.","id":"TREASURE","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Treasure Chest","effects":"Gives coins, and levels up or evolves weapons. A purple chest offers an Arcana instead.","release":"27 October 2021","level":"100000","luck":"Yes","rarity":"0"}
Treasure Chests are items that can be dropped by bosses. They can contain a number of things, including weapons, passive items, coins, and, if the conditions are met, evolutions. 
## Mechanics
## Prize amount
Chests will most commonly contain one item, but have a chance to contain three or five items instead, depending on Luck. Typically, chests will contain a random weapon or passive item the player currently has, leveling up the item by one. Chests containing more than one item may contain the same item multiple times, in which case the item will be leveled up the appropriate amount of times. In addition to whatever items a chest contains, the player will also receive a random amount of gold:
- 1-item chests reward between 100 - 200 gold at base.
- 3-item chests reward between 300 - 600 gold at base.
- 5-item chests reward between 500 - 1,000 gold at base.
The chances for obtaining a 1, 3, or 5-item chest are individually configured per boss enemy. Using the first Silver Bat that appears in Mad Forest at minute 9:00 as an example, it has a treasure config that looks like the following:
| | Level: 1Evolution: No
| [Treasure Chest 3] 3% | [Treasure Chest 2] 10% | [Treasure Chest] 50%
| Rewards: Upgrade (weapon) Upgrade (any) Upgrade (any) Upgrade (weapon) Upgrade (weapon)
This means, before factoring in the Luck value, there is a base 3% chance for a 5-item chest, and if that roll fails then a 10% chance for a 3-item chest, and if that roll fails then a 50% chance for a 1-item chest. If the third roll also fails, then this specific Silver Bat has a level 1 fallback, so it will drop a level 1 (1-item) chest if all 3 rolls fail. The chance for each roll including Luck is as follow:
chanceWithLuck=levelChance⋅totalLuck
Where levelChance is each of the percentages defined in the enemy's treasure config. The chanceWithLuck is compared against a randomly generated number between 0 and 100, with each check having a separate random number.
## Prize types
When the requirements for an evolution (or union) are fulfilled, certain chests will be able to evolve (or unite) a weapon. These chests typically drop from bosses that spawn after 10:00, or the respective time in a cycle of endless mode, but certain maps or bosses will be able to evolve before this point. If the player has the Yellow Sign, any chest that is able to evolve will be silver. Generally, only one weapon can be evolved per chest, including the higher tier chests. Much like the ability to evolve, there are also a multitude of exceptions for chests that are able to evolve multiple weapons.
- For examples of which chests, from before 10:00, that can evolve weapon, see the evolution page.
If all items are at max level and no evolutions are possible, all items in regular chests are replaced with Big Coin Bags ( 25). The Treasure Chest will also randomize the amount the player will receive, which is normally around 150, but this number grows based on how many items the player received in said chest and multiplied by Greed.
Additionally, if the player has not yet received a  Candybox from Treasure Chests or level ups, and all items have been replaced with Big Coin Bags, then each Coin Bag in a Treasure Chest will have a 1% chance to award the player with a Candybox. This chance can be increased by  Luck, and can only happen once per run. The player is guaranteed this reward from Treasure Chests that award five items.
- Once the player has obtained a Candybox via any means, then this chance will instead award a  Super Candybox II Turbo. Note that Super Candybox II Turbos can only be awared in evolution chests, as it is a gift.
## Beginner's luck
The first six chests picked up in a save will always drop items in the set sequence 1-1-3-1-1-5. These first six chests' animations cannot be skipped. After the first six chests, the animations of chests with one or three items can be skipped. Five-item chests' animations will be skippable once the player has seen the final fireworks, collected 50 five-item chests, or collected a total of 500 chests.
## Skipping
As mentioned prior, one-item and three-item chests will be skippable once the player has opened seven chests, with five-item chests being skippable later. Once the player unlocks the ability to skip five-item chests, any chests that contain only Big Coin Bags will have their animation automatically skipped in the overworld, granting the player the coins immediately.
Once the player has unlocked  Edge of the Earth (VIII), by triggering a Lavatrix Machina as  Para Kooleo after obtaining the  Darkasso, they will unlock the option to toggle "Quick Treasures" in options during a run. This will cause all chests to avoid opening the menu, instantly granting the prizes they normally would have, much like they would if they only contained gold. "Quick Treasures" does not affect the aforementioned mechanic for skipping gold-only chests.
## Types
## Bronze
[A sprite icon representing Treasure Chest with no link]
Bronze chests are usually incapable of providing weapon evolutions or unions. They are typically obtained as drops from bosses spawned before 10:00.
## Silver
[A sprite icon representing Treasure Chest 2 with no link]
Silver chests are capable of providing weapon evolutions and unions. They are typically obtained as drops from bosses spawned after 10:00.
## Gold
[A sprite icon representing Treasure Chest 3 with no link]
Golden chests are capable of providing weapons the player does not currently have, including base versions of equipped evolutions/unions, and will have those weapons appear as stage items that must be physically picked up.  Zi'Appunta Belpaese can have these spawn from her special light sources with a  Luck-based chance. Chests may also be upgraded to this type via  Call of a Mad Moon (XIII).
## Arcana
[A sprite icon representing Treasure Chest 5 with no link]
Arcana chests provide the player with an Arcana. They will offer to choose one out of 4 or 6 Arcana options depending on the amount of Arcanas unlocked. Will provide two additional items if the player has  Game Killer (0). They are typically obtained as drops from bosses that spawn at 11:00 and 21:00. If two Arcanas are obtained from these chests while extra Arcana chests exist on the stage, they will turn into bronze chests. If a newer player has all of their unlocked arcanas in use (say that they have only unlocked 1-2 arcanas), any extra Arcana chests will act like Silver chests instead.
## Dark
[A sprite icon representing Treasure Chest 4 with no link]
Dark chests have a 50% chance to function identically as bronze chests, except they will always contain only one item. The other 50% of the time, dark chests provide the player with a new passive item that they do not currently have which is not banished or sealed, whether all of the player's items are max level or not. However, if all of the available passive items are already obtained, the new passive option cannot upgrade any existing weapons or passive items so they only provide coins. If the player has Game Killer (0), dark chests can provide multiple new passive items at once. They can only be obtained as drops from  Levarsee Darkasso spawned by  Moonlight Bolero (VI) or  The Blinder spawned by  Call of a Mad Moon (XIII).
## Audio
## Jingles
When the player finds a chest, Treasure Found jingle will play. Then one of the following jingles would play, depending if the treasure chest contains 1, 3, or 5 items:
- Treasure A jingle (for a 1-item treasure chest)
- Treasure B jingle (for a 3-item treasure chest)
- Treasure C jingle (for a 5-item treasure chest)
If the player went to Options and enabled "Blast Processed" sound effects, Blast Processed Treasure Found jingle will play instead. Then one these jingles would play:
- Blast Processed Treasure A jingle (for a 1-item treasure chest)
- Blast Processed Treasure B jingle (for a 3-item treasure chest)
- Blast Processed Treasure C jingle (for a 5-item treasure chest)
## Sprites

## Obtaining treasure chests

## Trivia
- A treasure chest appears during the game's loading screen.
- When a player completes a round having collected at least one treasure chest that contains 5 items, the pickups section of the results screen will display a [A sprite icon representing Treasure Chest 3 with no link] golden chest. The [A sprite icon representing Treasure Chest 2 with no link] silver chest in that section would indicate chests containing 3 items.[Verify]
- The jingles that play when obtaining a 1-item chest, 3-item chest, or a 5-item chest, along with their variations, are all composed and arranged by William Davies, the same person who made "The Beginning" soundtrack.
- Purple/arcana chests are indicated by a purple arrow while all of the other different kinds of chests are indicated by a yellow/golden arrow.
- Likewise, in the  Milky Way Map, purple/arcana chests are uniquely indicated as purple while every other chest is indicated as a bronze chest.
- The golden treasure chest is the only chest where its items are not automatically given to the player. Instead, its items appear as pickups near the location of the chest.

# Trisection
| Trisection
| Enables random events in all stages. Enables random events in all stages.
| Relic
| Release | 12 June 2023 (Update)
| ID | RELIC_TRISECTION
| Effects | Adds a Random Events option in the Stage Selection menu.
| Location | Located at the end of the bottom path of Astral Stair.
| {"description":"Enables random events in all stages.","id":"RELIC_TRISECTION","update":"Patch 1.5.0","effects":"Adds a Random Events option in the Stage Selection menu.","sprite":"Sprite-Trisection.png","name":"Trisection","release":"12 June 2023","icon":"Icon-Trisection.png","location":"Located at the end of the bottom path of Astral Stair."}
The Trisection is a relic located in Astral Stair. Once collected, it permanently unlocks Random Events in the stage selection menu, which creates a three-ring roulette of the different masks of  The Directer at the bottom right corner of the screen before selecting a random event. It also allows  Gold Fingers to spawn on any stage once it is obtained.
## Obtaining
The Trisection is located to the southheast of the starting area in Astral Stair in the bottom row of rooms, where it can be found in the last room. Its location is tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. Once the Trisection has been collected, it will no longer appear on the map.
Alternatively, the Trisection can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "haeralisploy".
## Trivia
- The Trisection’s name and spell both reference the 1997 game Final Fantasy Tactics. The relic is named for the first battle music of the game, while the spell references the epithet “Haeralis the Brave” given to the character Delita Heiral.

Explosions damage is affected by Curse.","id":"T02_TWILIGHT","update":"Patch 0.9.0","icon":"","image":"","name":"Twilight Requiem (II)","affects":["King Bible","Unholy Vespers","Lightning Ring","Thunder Loop","Peachone","Ebony Wings","Runetracer","Shadow Pinion","Bone","Celestial Dusting","Bi-Bracelet","Silver Wind","Festive Winds","Argent Flow","Scarlet Needle","Cardinal Rain","Prismatic Missile","Luminaire","C-U-Laser","Pronto Beam","Santa Javelin","Seraphic Cry","Vampire Killer","Jet Black Whip","Mormegil Tip","Refectio","Sanctuary","Hex","Nightmare","Ninth Circle","Vjaya Sisters","Lapiste Tepisto","Spirit Rings","Rings of Calamity","Sanguine Star","Blood Chalice","Glaive","Feather Spear","Lohengrin"],"release":"21 July 2022","unlock":"Reach Level 50 with Dommario.","arcana name":"Twilight Requiem","type":"Arcana"}
Listed weapon projectiles generate explosions when they expire. Explosions damage is affected by Curse.
Twilight Requiem (II) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Dommario.
 Mina Hakuba [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Twilight Requiem (II) is active, the projectiles from listed weapons summon a horizontal explosion when they expire, dealing damage proportional to the player's  Curse. Damage increases linearly where every +100% Curse provides around 13.5 damage starting at 10 damage for +0% Curse. For example: +200% Curse deals 40 damage, +900% Curse deals 140 damage, and +9900% Curse deals 1,340 damage per explosion.
Affected weapons are:
-  King Bible,  Unholy Vespers, and  Ninth Circle [DLC]
-  Lightning Ring,  Thunder Loop, and  Vjaya Sisters [DLC]
-  Peachone and  Ebony Wings
-  Runetracer
-  Shadow Pinion
-  Bone and  Anima of Mortaccio
-  Celestial Dusting and  Profusione D'Amore
-  Bi-Bracelet,  Tri-Bracelet, and  Lapiste Tepisto [DLC]
-  Santa Javelin and  Seraphic Cry
-  Silver Wind [DLC],  Festive Winds [DLC], and  Argent Flow [DLC]
-  Scarlet Needle [DLC] and  Cardinal Rain [DLC]
-  Prismatic Missile [DLC] and  Luminaire [DLC]
-  C-U-Laser [DLC] and  Pronto Beam [DLC]
-  Vampire Killer [DLC]
-  Jet Black Whip [DLC] and  Mormegil Tip [DLC]
-  Refectio [DLC],  Speculo Refectio [DLC], and  Sanctuary [DLC]
-  Hex [DLC],  Speculo Hex [DLC], and  Nightmare [DLC]
-  Spirit Rings [DLC] and  Rings of Calamity [DLC]
-  Sanguine Star [DLC] and  Blood Chalice [DLC]
-  Glaive [DLC],  Feather Spear [DLC], and  Lohengrin [DLC]
## Tips
- The Prismatic Missile and Luminaire will only be affected by Twilight Requiem if it's picked before  Jail of Crystal (XIV) and/or  Heart of Fire (XIX).
- For most weapons, the explosion is created where the projectile expires. However, there are exceptions:
-  C-U-Laser [DLC] and  Pronto Beam [DLC]'s explosions are created at the muzzle after firing.
-  Jet Black Whip [DLC]'s explosions are created when the projectiles it creates appear.
-  Vampire Killer [DLC],  Hex [DLC] and  Nightmare [DLC]'s projectiles don't expire, but explode upon initial contact upon hitting the enemy. Additional explosions are not created in subsequent hits with the same projectile. The whip attacks and skull projectile are not affected by the Arcana.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Twilight Stigmata", the alternative name of An Empty Tome, Shanoa's theme in Castlevania: Order of Ecclesia.
- Twilight Requiem (II) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "lapapessa", which means "the high priestess" in Italian.
- This makes Twilight Requiem (II) a representation of The High Priestess Major Arcana card, symbolizing intuition, wisdom, enlightenment, divine knowledge, balance between masculinity and feminity, and the subconscious mind.
- This is reflected in its ability to amplify the power of magic weapons while using Curse, which would normally be a disadvantageous stat.
- This Arcana is one of the most prominent cases, alongside  Divine Bloodline (IX), of an Arcana applying mostly to base weapons but not their evolutions, even when said weapons are not mechanically different.
- When playing using the weapon  Celestial Dusting in Il Molise, the explosions generated by this arcana heal the plants and give back an  Experience Gem for healing, similar to the main attack.

| {"notes":"* Ignores all pickups.\n* It takes time for all gems to reach the player.\n* Can be \"evolved\" into Lavatrix Machina with an evolved Penshin Fatcha.","description":"Gathers all Experience Gems left on the ground.","id":"VACUUM","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","dlc":"","image":"","name":"Vacuum","effects":"All Experience Gems are pulled to the player.","release":"27 October 2021","level":"12","luck":"Yes","rarity":"2"}
The Vacuum is a item pickup in Vampire Survivors that has a chance to drop from light sources. It can only drop after the character reaches level 12, and its drop rate is affected by Luck.
Collecting the Vacuum for the first time will unlock the  Attractorb.
Picking up 50 Vacuums will unlock the The Lycaeum stage.
## Effects
Picking up the Vacuum pulls all the  Experience Gems lying on the ground towards the player's character and collects them.
## Lavatrix Machina
 Penshin Fatcha that has been evolved once gains a special effect that turns Vacuums into Lavatrix Machinas. This effect spins enemies around and can collect Treasure Chests, light sources, stage items, and pickups.
## Trivia
- While the Vacuum and  Attractorb look very similar, one notable difference between the looks of the two is the fact that the Vacuum has two rings while the Attractorb has one.

Triggers LastBreath, OnRevival, and HPCritical skills.","max-level":"6","id":"BMN_DODECAHEDRON","stacking":"","update":"Legacy of the Bloodmoon is out now!","dlc":"bm","sprite":"Sprite-Velvet Dodecahedron.png","max-effect":"+60% Greed, +60% Growth, -50% Max Health","name":"Velvet Dodecahedron","stat":"Growth, Greed, Max Health","release":"28 August 2026","rarity":"","icon":"Icon-Velvet Dodecahedron.png","per-level":""}
Velvet Dodecahedron is a passive item from the Legacy of the Bloodmoon DLC of Vampire Survivors.
It is unlocked by evolving  Screams from the Void. It can also be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "notashinytrapezohedron".
Velvet Dodecahedron reduces  Max Health in exchange for increasing  Greed and  Growth. When levelling it up, it triggers LastBreath, OnRevival, and HPCritical skills.
As a gift , this item does not take up a passive item slot, once unlocked, it will occur as a level up option even if the player has reached the passive limit.
## Levels
Velvet Dodecahedron has 6 levels. At max level, Velvet Dodecahedron provides +60%  Greed, +60%  Growth, and -50%  Max Health. By the time it has reached max level, the player will also have triggered listed skills 6 times.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | Gift. Will provide bonus Growth and Greed in exchange for MaxHealth. Triggers LastBreath, OnRevival, and HPCritical skills.
| Level 2 | Gold Coins value increases by 20%. Max Health is reduced by 10%. Triggers LastBreath, OnRevival, and HPCritical skills.
| Level 3 | XP gain increases by 30%. Max Health is reduced by 10%. Triggers LastBreath, OnRevival, and HPCritical skills.
| Level 4 | Gold Coins value increases by 20%. Max Health is reduced by 10%. Triggers LastBreath, OnRevival, and HPCritical skills.
| Level 5 | XP gain increases by 30%. Max Health is reduced by 10%. Triggers LastBreath, OnRevival, and HPCritical skills.
| Level 6 | Gold Coins value increases by 20%. Max Health is reduced by 10%. Triggers LastBreath, OnRevival, and HPCritical skills.
Velvet Dodecahedron is not used to evolve any weapons, but it is required to morph  Baal'Thasar and  Sargon into  Baal'Thamut and  Megalo Sargon, respectively.

Once per life, triggers Cursed World Eater after receiving fatal damage.","id":"D18_VICTORIAN_HORROR","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","icon":"","image":"","name":"Victorian Horror (XVIII)","affects":["Magnet","Greed","Curse","Little Heart"],"release":"28 october 2025","unlock":"Survive 30 minutes with no weapons in Inverse Green Acres.","arcana name":"Victorian Horror","type":"Darkana"}
Enemies within Magnet range receive Greed based damage. Once per life, triggers Cursed World Eater after receiving fatal damage.
Victorian Horror (XVIII) is one of the Darkanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by completing a 30:00 run in Green Acres in Inverse Mode without any active weapons after obtaining the  Darkasso.
## Overview
While Victorian Horror (XVIII) is active, it grants the player  Insatiable, a black, skull-shaped forcefield around the character that deals 10 base damage. This damage is multiplied by  Greed and  Might. If the player has more than +10  Amount, then every +1 Amount over 10 will also contribute +10% damage. Much like  Blood Astronomia (XXI), its radius is based on  Magnet. However, instead of solely being based on Magnet, this radius will also be multiplied by  Area. 
- Note that the Amount and Area scaling effects are only present when this Darkana is present. If the player is playing as  Je-Ne-Viv [DLC] without using Victorian Horror, then neither form of extra scaling will be present.
Additionally, enemies killed by Insatiable have a 2.5% chance to drop  Cursed Soul pickups. This chance cannot be modified. Cursed Souls heal the player for 1 HP, like that of a  Little Heart, while also increasing  Max Health, Greed, and  Curse by a miniscule amount. There is also a 200-250 pickup limit for Cursed Souls that can be on the stage at any point.
- The stats provided by  Cursed Souls are based on the base  Experience value of the enemy that dropped it. The stat bonuses provided by a Cursed Soul are:
-  Max Health = 0.1 * Base XP
-  Greed = 0.05% * Base XP
-  Curse = 0.05% * Base XP
- For example, a  Skelegem, with a Base XP value of 3, will drop a Cursed Soul that grants +0.3 Max Health, +0.15% Greed, and +0.15% Curse.
Once per life, when the player reaches 1 health (at their Last Breath), a Cursed World Eater is triggered, killing all enemies on-screen before turning them into Cursed Souls that are then magnetized to the player.
## Tips
- Notably, the unlock for this Darkana separately checks whether the player has passed 30:00 on Inverse Green Acres and whether they have no weapons. Thus, selling all of your weapons with  Stake to Your Heart (0) after 30:00 will count.
- Should this ever be fixed, Stake to Your Heart (0) will remain useful due to it allowing the player to remove their weapons while also providing survivability through the use of Gold as Health.
- Weapon selectors, like  Candybox and  Arma Dio, are not considered active weapons for the purposes of this unlock as long as you don't select an active weapon in them. This also applies to hidden weapons and passive weapons.
- Weapons that deal no damage, like  Laurel and  Lifesign Scan [DLC], are still considered weapons.
- Certain characters who start with no weapons and take no damage, namely  Megalo Menya Moonspell [DLC] and  Young Maria Renard [DLC] can make this unlock incredibly trivial.
- For Young Maria Renard specifically, the  Familiar Forge [DLC] should be skipped. The game does not consider the weapons held by familiars as hidden weapons, which may cause issues with the unlock.
- If the player does not have the above characters, then the following options also work:
- Any character that starts with a weapon selector, like  Big Trouser. For this, the player must skip the initial selection (or pick  Arma Dio if it is available to get a passive item instead), and avoid any other weapons.
- Sealing and Banishing as many weapons as possible is advised.
- For which characters start with weapon selectors, see  Candybox,  Morning Star,  Coat of Arms,  Belnades' Spellbook,  Spectral Sword,  Ebony Diabologue, and  Intuition.
- The following characters all start with no active weapon, instead only having hidden weapons if any at all:
-  Cosmo Pavone
-  Torino
-  Scorej-Oni
-  Megalo Impostor Rina [DLC]
-  Simondo Belmont [DLC]
-  Ferryman [DLC]
-  Carmilla [DLC]
-  Fleaman [DLC]
-  Malphas [DLC]
-  Stone Skull [DLC]
-  Astarte [DLC]
-  Lolo, Hiss, Meow, and Purr [DLC]
-  Kina [DLC]
-  Imakoo [DLC]
-  Arcana that deal damage, like  Divine Bloodline (IX) and  Wandering the Jet Black (XXI), without the use of a weapon are allowed. Pickups are also allowed for this unlock.
## Trivia
- The name of this Darkana is a reference to the song "Victorian Fear", which is the theme song for the City of Haze in Castlevania: Portrait of Ruin.
- Victorian Horror (XVIII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "anulal", the reverse of "laluna" or "the moon" in Italian.
- This makes Victorian Horror (XVIII) a representation of The Moon Major Arcana card, symbolizing inner turmoil, repressed thoughts, or emotional release.
- This is reflected in its ability to create a damaging forcefield around the player that drops pickups which boost stats with negative connotations, like Greed and Curse.

Listed weapon projectiles gain up to 3 bounces.
Waltz of Pearls (XI) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Imelda Belpaese.
 Carrie Fernandez [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Waltz of Pearls (XI) is active, the following weapons' projectiles gain up to three bounces:
-  Magic Wand,  Holy Wand and  Spirit of Light [DLC]
-  Fire Wand,  Hellfire and  Kardía Phlegeton [DLC]
-  Cross,  Heaven Sword and  Arch Angle [DLC]
-  Carréllo
-  Hats [DLC]
-  Sonic Bloom [DLC] and  Wave Beam [DLC]
-  Gale Force [DLC] and  Pneuma Tempestas [DLC]
-  Rock Riot [DLC] and  Gemma Torpor [DLC]
-  Gros Michel [DLC] and  Cavendish [DLC]
Magic Wand, Holy Wand, Fire Wand and Sonic Bloom can hit 3 more targets per shot, which effectively increases their crowd control.
Instead of bouncing on enemies three times before using up their own pierce like other weapons, Hellfire and Wave Beam bounce off the edges of the screen once, Cross and Heaven Sword twice, and Carréllo three more times. They already have infinite pierce and benefit more from staying on screen longer.
On the other hand, Rock Riot, Gemma Torpor, Gale Force and Pneuma Tempestas gets less benefit from this arcana, as they already have high speed and piercing: Rock Riot and Gale Force's traveling path can be changed, while Pneuma Tempestas just keeps how it goes.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Dance of Pales/Waltz of Pearls", which is the theme song of Olrox's Quarters in Castlevania: Symphony of the Night.
- Waltz of Pearls (XI) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "lagiustizia", which means "justice" in Italian.
- This makes Waltz of Pearls (XI) a representation of the Justice Major Arcana card, symbolizing truth, equity, integrity, fairness, accountability, causality, and decisiveness.
- This is reflected in its ability to amplify its boosted weapons' chances to strike groups of enemies multiple times.
- Waltz of Pearls (XI) is very similar to  Iron Blue Will (VII) with how both Arcanas give weapons extra bounces, but the two Arcanas differ mainly by applying to different weapon sets.
- Carréllo is affected by both Arcanas.

| {"description":"Allows you to buy the Preserve PowerUp.","id":"RELIC_RECYCLE","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ante Chamber out now, for free!","effects":"Unlocks the Preserve PowerUp.","sprite":"","name":"Wax Fetish","release":"28 October 2025","icon":"","location":"Carried by a dancer in Mazerella, being dropped once he is defeated in the rightmost wooden side room."}
Wax Fetish is a relic located in Mazerella. Once collected, it permanently unlocks the Preserve PowerUp.
## Obtaining
In Mazerella, Wax Fetish will spawn to the player's right (left in  Inverse Mode with the "Visually Invert Stages" Option enabled) and be promptly stolen by the male dancer. Chasing this dancer to the wooden side room at the far right of the stage and then defeating him will drop the relic on the floor, free for the player to pick up. Once collected, it will not appear again.
Alternatively, Wax Fetish can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "rererecycle".
## Trivia
- The spell "rererecycle" refers to Wax Fetish unlocking the Preserve PowerUp, as well as the spells of the stages which reuse floor tiles of the original stages, like "recycletheforest" for Bat Country or "recycletheplant" for Mazerella. The Preserve PowerUp was also briefly renamed "Recycle" in a beta build before it was reverted for the official release.

Does not occupy a weapon slot.","max-level":"1","id":"FB_WEAPONPU","update":"","dlc":"og","sprite":"Sprite-Weapon Power-Up.png","stage-item":"Neo Galuga","name":"Weapon Power-Up","rarity":"200","release":"","stat":"RevivalLevels-up a random weapon in the player's inventory","icon":"Icon-Weapon Power-Up.png","stacking":"Additive"}
Weapon Power-Up is a passive item from the Operation Guns DLC of Vampire Survivors.
Weapon Power-Up is added to the level-up item pool after obtaining any weapon from the Operation Guns DLC. The item may appear to occupy a passive item slot, but the inventory doesn't count it toward the passive item limit. Consequently, the player will still be able to acquire an additional item, even if the passive item inventory appears to be full. Although it does not take up space, an empty passive item slot is still needed in order to receive the Weapon Power-Up from level-up choices. 
For each Weapon Power-Up acquired, Revival is increased by 1 and a random weapon in the player's inventory that is not at its maximum level is upgraded by 1 level. Every weapon in the Operation Guns DLC requires and consumes it for evolution, in addition to a distinct passive item required by a weapon. Upon being consumed, the Weapon Power-Up will disappear from the inventory and return as a level-up choice, but the player will keep its conferred bonuses.
Up to 4 Weapon Power-Ups can be found as stage items on Neo Galuga. 
## Effects
Each Weapon Power-Up will grant +1 Revival and will level up one of the player's equipped weapons.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Levels up a random equipped weapon. Gives +1 Revival. Does not occupy a weapon slot."
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Long Gun | + | Weapon Power-Up | = | Prototype A
| Short Gun | + | Weapon Power-Up+Bracer | = | Prototype B
| Spread Shot | + | Weapon Power-Up+Empty Tome | = | Prototype C
| C-U-Laser | + | Weapon Power-Up+Tirajisú | = | Pronto Beam
| Firearm | + | Weapon Power-Up+Candelabrador | = | Fire-L3GS
| Sonic Bloom | + | Weapon Power-Up+Armor | = | Wave Beam
| Homing Miss | + | Weapon Power-Up+Duplicator | = | Multistage Missiles
| Diver Mines | + | Weapon Power-Up+Attractorb | = | Atmo-Torpedo
| Blade Crossbow | + | Weapon Power-Up+Clover | = | BFC2000-AD
| Prism Lass | + | Weapon Power-Up+Wings | = | Time Warp
| Metal Claw | + | Weapon Power-Up+Hollow Heart | = | Big Fuzzy Fist

| {"notes":"* Some enemies are resistant or immune to Weird Souls Purifier.","rarity":"3","id":"","update":"Patch 1.10: Laborratory","effects":"Kills all enemies that are not immune to it.","image":"","name":"Weird Souls Purifier","release":"30 April 2024","level":"8","luck":"Yes","description":""}
Weird Souls Purifier is an item pickup in Vampire Survivors that has a chance to drop from light sources after evolving  Santa Javelin into  Seraphic Cry. It can only drop after the character reaches level 8, and its drop rate is affected by  Luck. It is an improved version of  Rosary.
## Effects
Picking up Weird Souls Purifier creates two rotating rings around the character, and kills all enemies on the screen as a spear from the bottom right is thrown to the middle of the circle, then kills all enemies three more times as one is thrown from the bottom left, then the bottom (forming the icon of Seraphic Cry), and finally when the spears disappears and the rings expands.
Weird Souls Purifiers can attack and defeat the otherwise intangible  Stalkers and  Drowners in Dairy Plant, Gallo Tower, and Cappella Magna before the 30:00 mark, causing them to drop  Treasure Chests. However, some enemies, such as  The Reaper, are resistant or immune to Weird Souls Purifier's effect.
## Trivia
- When a Weird Souls Purifier is picked up, the animation that plays resembles the attack Nibelung Valesti from the role-playing game series Valkyrie Profile.
- The name "Weird Souls Purifier" further references the Valkyrie Profile series, specifically the exclamation "Purify Weird Soul!!" which appears when a Soul Crush is available in Valkyrie Profile: Lenneth.

| {"notes":"Stacks multiplicatively with other sources of Growth, Luck, Greed, and Curse","description":"Overall Growth, Luck, Greed, and Curse are doubled at fixed intervals. The character starts gaining +1% Growth, Luck, Greed, and Curse every 2 levels.","id":"T13_WICKED","update":"Patch 0.10.0","icon":"","image":"","name":"Wicked Season (XIII)","affects":["Growth","Luck","Greed","Curse"],"release":"4 August 2022","unlock":"Reach Level 50 with Christine.","arcana name":"Wicked Season","type":"Arcana"}
Overall Growth, Luck, Greed, and Curse are doubled at fixed intervals. The character starts gaining +1% Growth, Luck, Greed, and Curse every 2 levels.
Wicked Season (XIII) is one of the Arcanas in Vampire Survivors. It is unlocked by reaching level 50 while playing as  Christine Davain.
 Graham Jones [DLC] starts with this Arcana.
## Overview
While Wicked Season (XIII) is active, overall  Growth,  Luck,  Greed, and  Curse are temporarily doubled, changing every 10 seconds in that order. When each stat is double, the item representing that stat appear below the health bar and a colored half circle background acts as the timer as it shrinks to the middle, with colors reminiscent of seasons (blue, green, yellow, red). Every level up gives +0.5% to all of those stats.
## Tips
- The Arcana only starts giving stat boosts when the player selects it, and does not give extra stats retroactively. If the stats are desirable, select the Arcana early.
- As it gives scaling Growth and Curse, it can be extremely powerful at ramping up the power of the character.
- The additional 200% HP bonus enemies gain in Inverse Mode makes this a much riskier choice when playing on this mode due to Curse boosts to enemy speed and HP making it easier to get swarmed. As such, the player may want to buy this Arcana from  Big Trouser and pair it with  Awake (IV), or vice versa, if seeking it early in the run, in order to prevent the run being ended early.
- The player has 100% base Growth, Luck, Greed and Curse before modifications. These base values will be doubled by the Arcana as well, giving an effective +100% boost on top of doubling increases to those stats.
## Trivia
- The name of this Arcana is a reference to the song "Wicked Child", which is the theme song of the Outdoors in Castlevania.
- Wicked Season (XIII) can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "lamorte", which means "death" in Italian.
- This makes Wicked Season (XIII) a representation of the Death Major Arcana card, symbolizing abrupt and major changes, personal transformation, rejuvenation, and renewal.
- This is reflected in its ability to intermittently change seasons that significantly increase stats, putting the player in beneficial or dangerous situations, while subtly amplifying these stats over time as the player levels up.
- This may also be a reference to the song "(Don't Fear) The Reaper" by American rock band Blue Öyster Cult, particularly the lyric "Seasons don't fear the Reaper".

| {"description":"Character moves 10% faster.","max-level":"5","id":"MOVESPEED","stage-item":"Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Tiny Bridge","update":"There exist tales that don't exist.","stacking":"Additive","sprite":"Sprite-Wings.png","max-effect":"+50%","name":"Wings","stat":"Move Speed","release":"27 October 2021","rarity":"50","icon":"Icon-Wings.png","per-level":"+10%"}
Wings are a passive item in Vampire Survivors. They are unlocked by reaching Level 5 as any character.
Wings increase Move Speed by 10% per level.
Wings are required for the evolution of Shadow Pinion, Valkyrie Turner, and for the evolution of Glass Fandango, Celestial Voulge.
Wings can be found as a stage item in Dairy Plant, Moongolow, Boss Rash, Whiteout, and Tiny Bridge.
## Levels
Wings have 5 levels. At max level, Wings provide +50% Move Speed.
| Level | Description
| Level 1 | "Character moves 10% faster."
| Level 2 | Movement speed increases by 10%
| Level 3 | Movement speed increases by 10%
| Level 4 | Movement speed increases by 10%
| Level 5 | Movement speed increases by 10%
## Evolutions
| Base weapon | | Passive item | | Evolution
| Shadow Pinion | + | Wings | = | Valkyrie Turner
| Glass Fandango | + | Max level:Wings | = | Celestial Voulge
| Prism Lass | + | Wings+Weapon Power-Up | = | Time Warp
| Alucard Spear | + | Wings | = | Thunderbolt Spear
| Sonic Dash | + | Max level:Wings | = | Rapidus Fio
## Tips
- Wings do not have much usage, and are essentially a personal preference. Movement speed is arguably not as important as bonuses to your weapons or experience gain though.
- Peachone, Ebony Wings, and Vandalier move faster depending on Movement Speed bonuses. However, it does not have any tangible bonus to the power of the weapons as they already aim at the bombarding circle no matter the distance.
- La Borra's attacks moves and expands faster with both higher Speed and Move Speed, with Movement Speed giving a bigger boost.
- Shadow Pinion, Valkyrie Turner, and Celestial Dusting shoot out extra projectiles based on the character animation changes. Movement Speed increases the rate of the animation changing thus increases the overall fire rate of the weapon. Note that it does not affect Cooldown of them and the base Cooldown is quite high at 6 seconds.

| {"description":"Summons the Master Librarian in any stage.","id":"TP_RELIC_LIBRARIAN","update":"Vampire Survivors: Ode to Castlevania is out now!","dlc":"oc","sprite":"","name":"Wood Carving Score","release":"31 October 2024","location":"South East from the starting point of Ode to Castlevania (stage).","icon":"","effects":"The Master Librarian now appears in all stages."}
The Wood Carving Score is a relic from the Ode to Castlevania DLC in Vampire Survivors. Once collected, it permanently unlocks the Master Librarian as a merchant in any stage, where he is located near the regular Merchant.
## Obtaining
The Wood Carving Score can be obtained by defeating the  Treant in the Ode to Castlevania stage. The Treant's glyph only appears after the player obtains the  Pile of Secrets [DLC] and is located in the south of the starting point in the woods. Follow the path from the starting point leading south into the woods and turn east after the path leads there. Further east, after passing through a line of trees, the glyph of the Treant can be found on the floor before entering the building.
=====
# Yellow Sign

| {"description":"Permanently allows to detect hidden items in all stages.","id":"RELIC_YELLOW","update":"Patch 0.6.1","effects":"Unlocks the Silver Ring, Gold Ring, Metaglio Left, and Metaglio Right. Also enables certain hidden relics and events.","sprite":"No","name":"Yellow Sign","release":"23 May 2022","icon":"Icon-Yellow Sign.png","location":"Due east of Holy Forbidden."}
The Yellow Sign is a relic located in Holy Forbidden. Once collected, it permanently unlocks the  Silver Ring,  Gold Ring,  Metaglio Left, and  Metaglio Right, causing them to appear as stage items in all stages except the following:
- Bat Country
- Tiny Bridge
- Astral Stair
- Room 1665
- Carlo Cart
- The Coop
- Eudaimonia Machine
Additionally,  Treasure Chests containing evolutions will now permanently appear silver, and it is also required to unlock certain secret characters and events.
## Obtaining
The Yellow Sign is located by following the path east in Holy Forbidden. The stage itself is unlocked by defeating the  Trinacria that appears in Moongolow during the Lunar Eclipse Event. Its location is also tracked by a green arrow on the player's screen. Once the Yellow Sign has been collected, the run will automatically end, and Holy Forbidden will no longer be accessible. 
Alternatively, the Yellow Sign can be unlocked in the  Forbidden Scrolls of Morbane by casting the spell "ihaveseenitihaveseenitihaveseenitihaveseenitihaveseenitihaveseenitihaveseenit".
## Trivia
- There is a 1 in 65,535 chance for yellow squiggles to appear over the Yellow Sign's icon in the Collection. The squiggles are random every time. However, the icon will disappear after 9 seconds and the player have to scroll down the menu to see it, making it very easy to miss when people take too long and/or have no incentive to scroll down.
- The Yellow Sign is a reference to The King in Yellow by Robert W. Chambers, which is known to have served as the inspiration for the writings of H.P. Lovecraft. In the book, it never fully describes the shape and purpose of the Yellow Sign, and it is described instead as "a curious symbol or letter in gold. It was neither Arabic or Chinese, nor as I found afterwards did it belong to any human script."
- In The Repairer of Reputations, one of the stories in the collection, suggests that anyone who possesses or sees – even by accident – a copy of the Yellow Sign is susceptible to some form of insidious mind control or possession by the King in Yellow or one of his heirs. The stories also suggest that the original creator of the Yellow Sign was not human and possibly came from a strange alternate dimension. This may explain why there is a shadow of a pair of hands cast on the screen, congratulating the player after getting the Yellow Sign or after defeating  The Ender, which belong to  The Directer.
